============================================================
SNAPSHOT IGNORE POLICY
============================================================

This file is rebuilt from the current Cinerific workspace every time ./read_project.sh runs.
Ignored paths are omitted from the project tree and file dumps unless they appear inside an included text file such as .gitignore or read_project.sh.

The snapshot intentionally ignores:
- .git
- .gradle
- .idea
- app/build
- build
- .kotlin
- .tmp
- .cache
- .DS_Store
- local.properties
- *_PROJECT_SNAPSHOT.txt
- *.jks
- *.keystore

Binary image and screenshot files are inventoried by path, size, and modified time only. Raw binary bytes are not printed.

     1	package com.prahlin.cinerific
     2	
     3	import android.app.UiModeManager
     4	import android.content.pm.ActivityInfo
     5	import android.graphics.Color
     6	import android.os.Build
     7	import android.os.Bundle
     8	import androidx.activity.ComponentActivity
     9	import androidx.activity.SystemBarStyle
    10	import androidx.activity.compose.setContent
    11	import androidx.activity.enableEdgeToEdge
    12	import androidx.core.view.WindowCompat
    13	import com.prahlin.cinerific.ui.CinerificApp
    14	import com.prahlin.cinerific.ui.theme.CinerificTheme
    15	
    16	class MainActivity : ComponentActivity() {
    17	    override fun onCreate(savedInstanceState: Bundle?) {
    18	        requestedOrientation = ActivityInfo.SCREEN_ORIENTATION_FULL_SENSOR
    19	        if (Build.VERSION.SDK_INT >= Build.VERSION_CODES.S) {
    20	            getSystemService(UiModeManager::class.java).setApplicationNightMode(
    21	                UiModeManager.MODE_NIGHT_YES
    22	            )
    23	        }
    24	        enableEdgeToEdge(
    25	            statusBarStyle = SystemBarStyle.dark(Color.TRANSPARENT),
    26	            navigationBarStyle = SystemBarStyle.dark(Color.BLACK),
    27	        )
    28	        super.onCreate(savedInstanceState)
    29	        window.statusBarColor = Color.TRANSPARENT
    30	        window.navigationBarColor = Color.BLACK
    31	        if (Build.VERSION.SDK_INT >= Build.VERSION_CODES.P) {
    32	            window.navigationBarDividerColor = Color.BLACK
    33	        }
    34	        WindowCompat.getInsetsController(window, window.decorView).apply {
    35	            isAppearanceLightStatusBars = false
    36	            isAppearanceLightNavigationBars = false
    37	        }
    38	        setContent {
    39	            CinerificTheme {
    40	                CinerificApp()
    41	            }
    42	        }
    43	    }
    44	}

   152	@Composable
   153	fun CinerificApp() {
   154	    val configuration = LocalConfiguration.current
   155	    val isTablet = isCinerificTablet(configuration.smallestScreenWidthDp)
   156	    var showHome by rememberSaveable { mutableStateOf(false) }
   157	    var signedInProfile by rememberSaveable { mutableStateOf(CinerificProfile.Guest) }
   158	    var signInSessionId by rememberSaveable { mutableStateOf(0) }
   159	    var selectedLanguage by rememberSaveable { mutableStateOf(CinerificLanguage.English) }
   160	    var introSnapshot by rememberSaveable(stateSaver = CinerificIntroSnapshotSaver) {
   161	        mutableStateOf(CinerificIntroSnapshot())
   162	    }
   163	    var introView by remember { mutableStateOf<CinerificIntroView?>(null) }
   164	    var welcomeInputBlockedUntilMillis by remember { mutableStateOf(0L) }
   165	    var signOutLoadingVisible by remember { mutableStateOf(false) }
   166	
   167	    fun selectProfile(profile: CinerificProfile) {
   168	        if (SystemClock.uptimeMillis() < welcomeInputBlockedUntilMillis) return
   169	        introSnapshot = CinerificIntroSnapshot()
   170	        signedInProfile = profile
   171	        signInSessionId += 1
   172	        signOutLoadingVisible = false
   173	        showHome = true
   174	    }
   175	
   176	    BackHandler(enabled = !showHome && introSnapshot.activeFlowName.isNotBlank()) {
   177	        introView?.handleSystemBack()
   178	    }
   179	
   180	    Box(modifier = Modifier.fillMaxSize()) {
   181	        AndroidView(
   182	            modifier = Modifier.fillMaxSize(),
   183	            factory = { context ->
   184	                CinerificIntroView(context).apply {
   185	                    introView = this
   186	                    onIntroSnapshotChanged = { snapshot ->
   187	                        if (introSnapshot != snapshot) {
   188	                            introSnapshot = snapshot
   189	                        }
   190	                    }
   191	                    restoreIntroSnapshot(introSnapshot)
   192	                    onAvatarSelected = ::selectProfile
   193	                }
   194	            },
   195	            update = { view ->
   196	                view.onIntroSnapshotChanged = { snapshot ->
   197	                    if (introSnapshot != snapshot) {
   198	                        introSnapshot = snapshot
   199	                    }
   200	                }
   201	                view.restoreIntroSnapshot(introSnapshot)
   202	                view.onAvatarSelected = ::selectProfile
   203	            }
   204	        )
   205	
   206	        if (isTablet && !showHome && introSnapshot.activeFlowName.isBlank()) {
   207	            CinerificTabletIntroAccountActions(
   208	                onCreateAccount = { introView?.showCreateAccountFromTabletHost() },
   209	                onSignIn = { introView?.showSignInFromTabletHost() },
   210	                onForgotPassword = { introView?.showForgotPasswordFromTabletHost() }
   211	            )
   212	        }
   213	
   214	        if (showHome) {
   215	            CinerificLocalizedResources(selectedLanguage) {
   216	                CinerificMainExperience(
   217	                    isTablet = isTablet,
   218	                    signInSessionId = signInSessionId,
   219	                    signedInProfile = signedInProfile,
   220	                    selectedLanguage = selectedLanguage,
   221	                    onLanguageSelected = { selectedLanguage = it },
   222	                    onSignOutLoadingStarted = { signOutLoadingVisible = true },
   223	                    onSignOut = {
   224	                        signOutLoadingVisible = true
   225	                        welcomeInputBlockedUntilMillis = SystemClock.uptimeMillis() + 500L
   226	                        signedInProfile = CinerificProfile.Guest
   227	                        introSnapshot = CinerificIntroSnapshot()
   228	                        showHome = false
   229	                        signOutLoadingVisible = false
   230	                    }
   231	                )
   232	            }
   233	        }
   234	
   235	        if (signOutLoadingVisible) {
   236	            SignOutLoadingOverlay(modifier = Modifier.fillMaxSize())
   237	        }
   238	    }
   239	}
   240	
   241	internal enum class CinerificDestination {
   242	    Home,
   243	    Movies,
   244	    Shows,
   245	    Favorites,
   246	    Settings,
   247	    ProgramDetails
   248	}
   249	
   250	internal enum class CinerificProfile(
   251	    @DrawableRes val avatarResId: Int,
   252	    @DrawableRes val nameResId: Int
   253	) {
   254	    Steve(R.drawable.steve_avatar_bubble_edge50_body0_test, R.drawable.steve_name),
   255	    Martin(R.drawable.martin_avatar_bubble_edge50_body0_test, R.drawable.martin_name),
   256	    Janny(R.drawable.janny_avatar_bubble_edge50_body0_test, R.drawable.janny_name),
   257	    Guest(R.drawable.guest_avatar_bubble_edge50_body0_test, R.drawable.guest_name)
   258	}
   259	
   260	private data class CinerificDeviceIdentity(
   261	    val manufacturer: String,
   262	    val modelName: String,
   263	    val hardwareModel: String,
   264	    val releaseYear: Int?
   265	) {
   266	    val stableId: String
   267	        get() = Base64.encodeToString(
   268	            "$manufacturer|$hardwareModel".lowercase().toByteArray(Charsets.UTF_8),
   269	            Base64.URL_SAFE or Base64.NO_WRAP or Base64.NO_PADDING
   270	        )
   271	
   272	    val displayName: String
   273	        get() = buildString {
   274	            append(manufacturer)
   275	            append(' ')
   276	            append(modelName)
   277	            releaseYear?.let { year ->
   278	                append(" (")
   279	                append(year)
   280	                append(')')
   281	            }
   282	        }
   283	}
   284	
   285	private fun currentCinerificDevice(): CinerificDeviceIdentity {
   286	    val rawManufacturer = Build.MANUFACTURER.trim().ifBlank { "Unknown" }
   287	    val manufacturer = rawManufacturer.lowercase().replaceFirstChar { character ->
   288	        character.titlecase()
   289	    }
   290	    val hardwareModel = Build.MODEL.trim().ifBlank { "Unknown model" }
   291	    val normalizedManufacturer = rawManufacturer.lowercase()
   292	    val normalizedModel = hardwareModel.uppercase()
   293	    val knownModel = when {
   294	        normalizedManufacturer == "samsung" && normalizedModel.startsWith("SM-G990") ->
   295	            "Galaxy S21 FE 5G" to 2022
   296	        else -> hardwareModel to null
   297	    }
   298	    return CinerificDeviceIdentity(
   299	        manufacturer = manufacturer,
   300	        modelName = knownModel.first,
   301	        hardwareModel = hardwareModel,
   302	        releaseYear = knownModel.second
   303	    )
   304	}
   305	
   306	private fun accountDevicePrefix(profile: CinerificProfile): String =
   307	    "account:${profile.name}"
   308	
   309	private fun recordAccountDeviceUse(
   310	    preferences: SharedPreferences,
   311	    profile: CinerificProfile,
   312	    device: CinerificDeviceIdentity
   313	) {
   314	    val accountPrefix = accountDevicePrefix(profile)
   315	    val idsKey = accountPrefix + ACCOUNT_DEVICE_IDS_KEY_SUFFIX
   316	    val devicePrefix = "$accountPrefix:device:${device.stableId}"
   317	    val deviceIds = preferences.getStringSet(idsKey, emptySet()).orEmpty().toMutableSet()
   318	    deviceIds += device.stableId
   319	    preferences.edit()
   320	        .putStringSet(idsKey, deviceIds)
   321	        .putString("$devicePrefix:manufacturer", device.manufacturer)
   322	        .putString("$devicePrefix:model", device.modelName)
   323	        .putInt("$devicePrefix:release_year", device.releaseYear ?: 0)
   324	        .putInt(
   325	            "$devicePrefix:use_count",
   326	            preferences.getInt("$devicePrefix:use_count", 0) + 1
   327	        )
   328	        .putLong("$devicePrefix:last_used", System.currentTimeMillis())
   329	        .apply()
   330	}
   331	
   332	private fun mostUsedDeviceName(
   333	    preferences: SharedPreferences,
   334	    profile: CinerificProfile
   335	): String? {
   336	    val accountPrefix = accountDevicePrefix(profile)
   337	    val deviceIds = preferences
   338	        .getStringSet(accountPrefix + ACCOUNT_DEVICE_IDS_KEY_SUFFIX, emptySet())
   339	        .orEmpty()
   340	    val mostUsedDeviceId = deviceIds.maxWithOrNull(
   341	        compareBy<String> { deviceId ->
   342	            preferences.getInt("$accountPrefix:device:$deviceId:use_count", 0)
   343	        }.thenBy { deviceId ->
   344	            preferences.getLong("$accountPrefix:device:$deviceId:last_used", 0L)
   345	        }
   346	    ) ?: return null
   347	    val devicePrefix = "$accountPrefix:device:$mostUsedDeviceId"
   348	    val manufacturer = preferences.getString("$devicePrefix:manufacturer", null) ?: return null
   349	    val modelName = preferences.getString("$devicePrefix:model", null) ?: return null
   350	    val releaseYear = preferences.getInt("$devicePrefix:release_year", 0)
   351	    return buildString {
   352	        append(manufacturer)
   353	        append(' ')
   354	        append(modelName)
   355	        if (releaseYear > 0) {
   356	            append(" (")
   357	            append(releaseYear)
   358	            append(')')
   359	        }
   360	    }
   361	}
   362	
   363	@Composable
   364	private fun CinerificMainExperience(
   365	    isTablet: Boolean,
   366	    signInSessionId: Int,
   367	    signedInProfile: CinerificProfile,
   368	    selectedLanguage: CinerificLanguage,
   369	    onLanguageSelected: (CinerificLanguage) -> Unit,
   370	    onSignOutLoadingStarted: () -> Unit,
   371	    onSignOut: () -> Unit
   372	) {
   373	    var destination by rememberSaveable(signInSessionId) { mutableStateOf(CinerificDestination.Home) }
   374	    var detailOriginDestination by rememberSaveable(signInSessionId) {
   375	        mutableStateOf(CinerificDestination.Home)
   376	    }
   377	    var selectedProgramTitle by rememberSaveable(signInSessionId) { mutableStateOf("Sink or Swim") }
   378	    var favoriteProgramTitles by rememberSaveable { mutableStateOf(emptyList<String>()) }
   379	    var userProgramRatings by rememberSaveable { mutableStateOf(emptyMap<String, Int>()) }
   380	    val context = LocalContext.current
   381	    val playbackStatsPreferences = remember(context) {
   382	        context.getSharedPreferences(PLAYBACK_STATS_PREFERENCES, 0)
   383	    }
   384	    val accountDevicePreferences = remember(context) {
   385	        context.getSharedPreferences(ACCOUNT_DEVICE_PREFERENCES, 0)
   386	    }
   387	    var mostUsedDeviceName by remember(signedInProfile) {
   388	        mutableStateOf(mostUsedDeviceName(accountDevicePreferences, signedInProfile))
   389	    }
   390	    var programPlayCounts by remember {
   391	        mutableStateOf(
   392	            playbackStatsPreferences.all.mapNotNull { (key, value) ->
   393	                if (!key.startsWith(PLAYBACK_COUNT_KEY_PREFIX)) return@mapNotNull null
   394	                val count = value as? Int ?: return@mapNotNull null
   395	                key.removePrefix(PLAYBACK_COUNT_KEY_PREFIX) to count
   396	            }.toMap()
   397	        )
   398	    }
   399	    var programPlaytimeMillis by remember {
   400	        mutableStateOf(
   401	            playbackStatsPreferences.all.mapNotNull { (key, value) ->
   402	                if (!key.startsWith(PLAYBACK_TIME_KEY_PREFIX)) return@mapNotNull null
   403	                val durationMillis = value as? Long ?: return@mapNotNull null
   404	                key.removePrefix(PLAYBACK_TIME_KEY_PREFIX) to durationMillis
   405	            }.toMap()
   406	        )
   407	    }
   408	    var mostRecentlyPlayedTitle by remember {
   409	        mutableStateOf(
   410	            playbackStatsPreferences.getString(MOST_RECENTLY_PLAYED_TITLE_KEY, null)
   411	        )
   412	    }
   413	    var catalogRouteDestinationName by rememberSaveable(signInSessionId) { mutableStateOf("") }
   414	    var catalogRouteGenreName by rememberSaveable(signInSessionId) { mutableStateOf("") }
   415	    var catalogRouteModeName by rememberSaveable(signInSessionId) { mutableStateOf("") }
   416	    var favoritesFullPromptRequestId by remember { mutableStateOf(0) }
   417	    var autoLogoutEnabled by rememberSaveable { mutableStateOf(false) }
   418	    var userInitiatedPlaybackActive by remember { mutableStateOf(false) }
   419	    var activePlaybackTitle by remember { mutableStateOf<String?>(null) }
   420	    var activePlaybackLastTickMillis by remember { mutableStateOf(0L) }
   421	    var lastInteractionMillis by remember { mutableStateOf(SystemClock.uptimeMillis()) }
   422	    var primaryDestinationCanScroll by remember(destination) { mutableStateOf<Boolean?>(null) }
   423	    var portraitBottomNavHiddenFraction by remember(signInSessionId) { mutableFloatStateOf(1f) }
   424	    var portraitBottomNavHeightPx by remember { mutableFloatStateOf(1f) }
   425	    var showSignOutConfirmation by rememberSaveable(signInSessionId) { mutableStateOf(false) }
   426	    var signOutInProgress by remember(signInSessionId) { mutableStateOf(false) }
   427	    val destinationStateHolder = rememberSaveableStateHolder()
   428	    val scope = rememberCoroutineScope()
   429	    val configuration = LocalConfiguration.current
   430	    val isPortrait = configuration.screenHeightDp > configuration.screenWidthDp
   431	    val usesScrollLinkedPortraitBottomNav = destination in setOf(
   432	        CinerificDestination.Home,
   433	        CinerificDestination.Movies,
   434	        CinerificDestination.Shows,
   435	        CinerificDestination.Favorites,
   436	        CinerificDestination.Settings
   437	    )
   438	
   439	    LaunchedEffect(signInSessionId, signedInProfile) {
   440	        recordAccountDeviceUse(
   441	            preferences = accountDevicePreferences,
   442	            profile = signedInProfile,
   443	            device = currentCinerificDevice()
   444	        )
   445	        mostUsedDeviceName = mostUsedDeviceName(accountDevicePreferences, signedInProfile)
   446	    }
   447	    val portraitBottomNavScrollConnection = remember(
   448	        isPortrait,
   449	        destination,
   450	        primaryDestinationCanScroll,
   451	        portraitBottomNavHeightPx
   452	    ) {
   453	        object : NestedScrollConnection {
   454	            override fun onPostScroll(
   455	                consumed: Offset,
   456	                available: Offset,
   457	                source: NestedScrollSource
   458	            ): Offset {
   459	                if (
   460	                    isPortrait &&
   461	                    usesScrollLinkedPortraitBottomNav &&
   462	                    primaryDestinationCanScroll == true &&
   463	                    consumed.y != 0f
   464	                ) {
   465	                    portraitBottomNavHiddenFraction = (
   466	                        portraitBottomNavHiddenFraction +
   467	                            consumed.y * PORTRAIT_BOTTOM_NAV_SCROLL_MULTIPLIER /
   468	                            portraitBottomNavHeightPx
   469	                        ).coerceIn(0f, 1f)
   470	                }
   471	                return Offset.Zero
   472	            }
   473	        }
   474	    }
   475	
   476	    LaunchedEffect(destination, isPortrait, primaryDestinationCanScroll) {
   477	        portraitBottomNavHiddenFraction = if (
   478	            isPortrait &&
   479	            usesScrollLinkedPortraitBottomNav &&
   480	            primaryDestinationCanScroll != false
   481	        ) {
   482	            1f
   483	        } else {
   484	            0f
   485	        }
   486	    }
   487	    val playbackSessionController = remember(userInitiatedPlaybackActive) {
   488	        CinerificPlaybackSessionController(
   489	            isUserInitiatedPlaybackActive = userInitiatedPlaybackActive,
   490	            onUserInitiatedPlaybackStarted = {
   491	                userInitiatedPlaybackActive = true
   492	            },
   493	            finishUserInitiatedPlayback = {
   494	                userInitiatedPlaybackActive = false
   495	                lastInteractionMillis = SystemClock.uptimeMillis()
   496	            }
   497	        )
   498	    }
   499	    val catalogRoute = remember(
   500	        catalogRouteDestinationName,
   501	        catalogRouteGenreName,
   502	        catalogRouteModeName
   503	    ) {
   504	        cinerificSavedCatalogRoute(
   505	            destinationName = catalogRouteDestinationName,
   506	            genreName = catalogRouteGenreName,
   507	            modeName = catalogRouteModeName
   508	        )
   509	    }
   510	    val mostFrequentlyViewedProgramTitle = remember(
   511	        programPlayCounts,
   512	        mostRecentlyPlayedTitle
   513	    ) {
   514	        val highestPlayCount = programPlayCounts.values.maxOrNull()
   515	        if (highestPlayCount == null) {
   516	            null
   517	        } else {
   518	            mostRecentlyPlayedTitle?.takeIf { title ->
   519	                programPlayCounts[title] == highestPlayCount
   520	            } ?: programPlayCounts.entries.firstOrNull { (_, count) ->
   521	                count == highestPlayCount
   522	            }?.key
   523	        }
   524	    }
   525	    val mostFrequentlyViewedPlayCount = mostFrequentlyViewedProgramTitle?.let { title ->
   526	        programPlayCounts[title]
   527	    }
   528	    val longestPlaytimeProgramTitle = remember(
   529	        programPlaytimeMillis,
   530	        mostRecentlyPlayedTitle
   531	    ) {
   532	        val longestDuration = programPlaytimeMillis.values.maxOrNull()
   533	        if (longestDuration == null) {
   534	            null
   535	        } else {
   536	            mostRecentlyPlayedTitle?.takeIf { title ->
   537	                programPlaytimeMillis[title] == longestDuration
   538	            } ?: programPlaytimeMillis.entries.firstOrNull { (_, duration) ->
   539	                duration == longestDuration
   540	            }?.key
   541	        }
   542	    }
   543	    val longestPlaytimeMillis = longestPlaytimeProgramTitle?.let { title ->
   544	        programPlaytimeMillis[title]
   545	    }
   546	
   547	    LaunchedEffect(activePlaybackTitle) {
   548	        val title = activePlaybackTitle ?: return@LaunchedEffect
   549	        while (activePlaybackTitle == title) {
   550	            delay(1_000L)
   551	            if (activePlaybackTitle != title) break
   552	            val now = SystemClock.elapsedRealtime()
   553	            val elapsedMillis = (now - activePlaybackLastTickMillis).coerceAtLeast(0L)
   554	            activePlaybackLastTickMillis = now
   555	            val updatedDuration = (programPlaytimeMillis[title] ?: 0L) + elapsedMillis
   556	            programPlaytimeMillis = programPlaytimeMillis + (title to updatedDuration)
   557	            playbackStatsPreferences.edit()
   558	                .putLong(PLAYBACK_TIME_KEY_PREFIX + title, updatedDuration)
   559	                .apply()
   560	        }
   561	    }
   562	
   563	    fun clearCatalogRoute() {
   564	        catalogRouteDestinationName = ""
   565	        catalogRouteGenreName = ""
   566	        catalogRouteModeName = ""
   567	    }
   568	
   569	    LaunchedEffect(autoLogoutEnabled, userInitiatedPlaybackActive, lastInteractionMillis) {
   570	        if (!autoLogoutEnabled || userInitiatedPlaybackActive) return@LaunchedEffect
   571	        delay(AUTO_LOGOUT_TIMEOUT_MS)
   572	        val inactiveForMillis = SystemClock.uptimeMillis() - lastInteractionMillis
   573	        if (
   574	            autoLogoutEnabled &&
   575	            !userInitiatedPlaybackActive &&
   576	            inactiveForMillis >= AUTO_LOGOUT_TIMEOUT_MS
   577	        ) {
   578	            onSignOut()
   579	        }
   580	    }
   581	
   582	    fun showProgramDetails(title: String) {
   583	        if (destination != CinerificDestination.ProgramDetails) {
   584	            detailOriginDestination = destination
   585	        }
   586	        selectedProgramTitle = title
   587	        destination = CinerificDestination.ProgramDetails
   588	    }
   589	
   590	    fun showCatalog(route: CinerificCatalogRoute) {
   591	        catalogRouteDestinationName = route.destination.name
   592	        catalogRouteGenreName = route.genre.name
   593	        catalogRouteModeName = route.mode.name
   594	        destination = route.destination
   595	    }
   596	
   597	    fun rateProgram(title: String, rating: Int) {
   598	        userProgramRatings = userProgramRatings + (title to rating.coerceIn(1, 5))
   599	    }
   600	
   601	    fun recordProgramPlay(title: String) {
   602	        val updatedCount = (programPlayCounts[title] ?: 0) + 1
   603	        programPlayCounts = programPlayCounts + (title to updatedCount)
   604	        mostRecentlyPlayedTitle = title
   605	        playbackStatsPreferences.edit()
   606	            .putInt(PLAYBACK_COUNT_KEY_PREFIX + title, updatedCount)
   607	            .putString(MOST_RECENTLY_PLAYED_TITLE_KEY, title)
   608	            .apply()
   609	    }
   610	
   611	    fun startProgramPlaybackTimer(title: String) {
   612	        activePlaybackTitle = title
   613	        activePlaybackLastTickMillis = SystemClock.elapsedRealtime()
   614	    }
   615	
   616	    fun stopProgramPlaybackTimer(title: String) {
   617	        if (activePlaybackTitle != title) return
   618	        val now = SystemClock.elapsedRealtime()
   619	        val elapsedMillis = (now - activePlaybackLastTickMillis).coerceAtLeast(0L)
   620	        val updatedDuration = (programPlaytimeMillis[title] ?: 0L) + elapsedMillis
   621	        programPlaytimeMillis = programPlaytimeMillis + (title to updatedDuration)
   622	        playbackStatsPreferences.edit()
   623	            .putLong(PLAYBACK_TIME_KEY_PREFIX + title, updatedDuration)
   624	            .apply()
   625	        activePlaybackTitle = null
   626	        activePlaybackLastTickMillis = 0L
   627	    }
   628	
   629	    fun toggleFavoriteProgram(title: String) {
   630	        if (title in favoriteProgramTitles) {
   631	            favoriteProgramTitles = favoriteProgramTitles - title
   632	            return
   633	        }
   634	
   635	        val isShow = cinerificProgramIsShow(title)
   636	        val filledSlots = cinerificFilledFavoritePlaceholderCount(
   637	            titles = favoriteProgramTitles,
   638	            isShow = isShow
   639	        )
   640	        if (filledSlots >= CINERIFIC_FAVORITES_CAPACITY) {
   641	            favoritesFullPromptRequestId += 1
   642	            return
   643	        }
   644	
   645	        favoriteProgramTitles = favoriteProgramTitles + title
   646	    }
   647	
   648	    fun requestSignOut() {
   649	        if (signOutInProgress) return
   650	        showSignOutConfirmation = true
   651	    }
   652	
   653	    fun confirmSignOut() {
   654	        if (signOutInProgress) return
   655	        showSignOutConfirmation = false
   656	        signOutInProgress = true
   657	        onSignOutLoadingStarted()
   658	        scope.launch {
   659	            delay(SIGN_OUT_LOADING_MS)
   660	            onSignOut()
   661	        }
   662	    }
   663	
   664	    BackHandler {
   665	        when {
   666	            signOutInProgress -> Unit
   667	            showSignOutConfirmation -> showSignOutConfirmation = false
   668	            destination == CinerificDestination.ProgramDetails -> {
   669	                destination = detailOriginDestination
   670	            }
   671	            destination != CinerificDestination.Home -> {
   672	                clearCatalogRoute()
   673	                destination = CinerificDestination.Home
   674	            }
   675	            else -> requestSignOut()
   676	        }
   677	    }
   678	
   679	    CompositionLocalProvider(LocalCinerificPlaybackSessionController provides playbackSessionController) {
   680	        Box(
   681	            modifier = Modifier
   682	                .fillMaxSize()
   683	                .nestedScroll(portraitBottomNavScrollConnection)
   684	                .pointerInput(Unit) {
   685	                    awaitPointerEventScope {
   686	                        while (true) {
   687	                            awaitPointerEvent(PointerEventPass.Initial)
   688	                            lastInteractionMillis = SystemClock.uptimeMillis()
   689	                        }
   690	                    }
   691	                }
   692	        ) {
   693	            destinationStateHolder.SaveableStateProvider(destination) {
   694	                when (destination) {
   695	                    CinerificDestination.Home -> CinerificHomeScreen(
   696	                        onProgramSelected = ::showProgramDetails,
   697	                        onCatalogSelected = ::showCatalog,
   698	                        onVerticalScrollabilityChanged = { primaryDestinationCanScroll = it },
   699	                        modifier = Modifier.fillMaxSize()
   700	                    )
   701	                    CinerificDestination.Movies,
   702	                    CinerificDestination.Shows,
   703	                    CinerificDestination.Favorites,
   704	                    CinerificDestination.Settings -> CinerificDestinationScreen(
   705	                        destination = destination,
   706	                        signedInProfile = signedInProfile,
   707	                        selectedLanguage = selectedLanguage,
   708	                        onLanguageSelected = onLanguageSelected,
   709	                        autoLogoutEnabled = autoLogoutEnabled,
   710	                        onAutoLogoutEnabledChange = { enabled ->
   711	                            autoLogoutEnabled = enabled
   712	                            lastInteractionMillis = SystemClock.uptimeMillis()
   713	                        },
   714	                        onSignOut = ::requestSignOut,
   715	                        favoriteProgramTitles = favoriteProgramTitles,
   716	                        onFavoriteToggled = ::toggleFavoriteProgram,
   717	                        userProgramRatings = userProgramRatings,
   718	                        onProgramRated = ::rateProgram,
   719	                        mostFrequentlyViewedProgramTitle = mostFrequentlyViewedProgramTitle,
   720	                        mostFrequentlyViewedPlayCount = mostFrequentlyViewedPlayCount,
   721	                        longestPlaytimeProgramTitle = longestPlaytimeProgramTitle,
   722	                        longestPlaytimeMillis = longestPlaytimeMillis,
   723	                        programPlaytimeMillis = programPlaytimeMillis,
   724	                        mostUsedDeviceName = mostUsedDeviceName,
   725	                        onProgramSelected = ::showProgramDetails,
   726	                        catalogRoute = catalogRoute?.takeIf { it.destination == destination },
   727	                        onVerticalScrollabilityChanged = { primaryDestinationCanScroll = it },
   728	                        modifier = Modifier.fillMaxSize()
   729	                    )
   730	                    CinerificDestination.ProgramDetails -> CinerificProgramDetailsScreen(
   731	                        programTitle = selectedProgramTitle,
   732	                        favoriteProgramTitles = favoriteProgramTitles,
   733	                        onFavoriteToggled = ::toggleFavoriteProgram,
   734	                        userProgramRatings = userProgramRatings,
   735	                        onProgramRated = ::rateProgram,
   736	                        onProgramPlayed = ::recordProgramPlay,
   737	                        onProgramPlaybackStarted = ::startProgramPlaybackTimer,
   738	                        onProgramPlaybackStopped = ::stopProgramPlaybackTimer,
   739	                        onProgramSelected = ::showProgramDetails,
   740	                        modifier = Modifier.fillMaxSize()
   741	                    )
   742	                }
   743	            }
   744	
   745	            if (isTablet) {
   746	                CinerificTabletNavigation(
   747	                    currentDestination = destination,
   748	                    portraitHiddenFraction = portraitBottomNavHiddenFraction,
   749	                    onPortraitBarHeightChanged = { heightPx ->
   750	                        portraitBottomNavHeightPx = heightPx.coerceAtLeast(1f)
   751	                    },
   752	                    onDestinationSelected = {
   753	                        clearCatalogRoute()
   754	                        destination = it
   755	                    }
   756	                )
   757	            } else {
   758	                CinerificRightSideNavBar(
   759	                    currentDestination = destination,
   760	                    portraitHiddenFraction = portraitBottomNavHiddenFraction,
   761	                    onPortraitBarHeightChanged = { heightPx ->
   762	                        portraitBottomNavHeightPx = heightPx.coerceAtLeast(1f)
   763	                    },
   764	                    onDestinationSelected = {
   765	                        clearCatalogRoute()
   766	                        destination = it
   767	                    },
   768	                    modifier = Modifier.fillMaxSize()
   769	                )
   770	            }
   771	
   772	            FavoritesFullPromptOverlay(
   773	                requestId = favoritesFullPromptRequestId,
   774	                currentDestination = destination,
   775	                suppressed = userInitiatedPlaybackActive,
   776	                modifier = Modifier.fillMaxSize()
   777	            )
   778	
   779	            if (showSignOutConfirmation) {
   780	                if (isTablet) {
   781	                    CinerificTabletSignOutDialog(
   782	                        onConfirm = ::confirmSignOut,
   783	                        onDismiss = { showSignOutConfirmation = false }
   784	                    )
   785	                } else {
   786	                    SignOutConfirmationOverlay(
   787	                        onConfirm = ::confirmSignOut,
   788	                        onDismiss = { showSignOutConfirmation = false },
   789	                        modifier = Modifier
   790	                            .fillMaxSize()
   791	                            .zIndex(20f)
   792	                    )
   793	                }
   794	            }
   795	        }
   796	    }
   797	}

   873	                    textAlign = TextAlign.Center
   874	                )

   877	                    horizontalArrangement = Arrangement.spacedBy(14.dp)
   878	                ) {

   880	                        label = "Yes",
   881	                        backgroundColor = ColorFrame2Background,
   882	                        contentColor = Color.White,
   883	                        borderColor = Color(0xFFC86BE0),
   884	                        onClick = onConfirm,
   885	                        modifier = Modifier.weight(1f)
   886	                    )

   930	
   931	private fun cinerificSavedCatalogRoute(
   932	    destinationName: String,
   933	    genreName: String,
   934	    modeName: String
   935	): CinerificCatalogRoute? {
   936	    if (destinationName.isBlank() || genreName.isBlank() || modeName.isBlank()) return null
   937	
   938	    val destination = CinerificDestination.values().firstOrNull { it.name == destinationName } ?: return null
   939	    val genre = ViewportGenre.values().firstOrNull { it.name == genreName } ?: return null
   940	    val mode = ViewportMode.values().firstOrNull { it.name == modeName } ?: return null
   941	
   942	    return CinerificCatalogRoute(
   943	        destination = destination,
   944	        genre = genre,
   945	        mode = mode
   946	    )
   947	}
   948	
   949	@Composable
   950	private fun FavoritesFullPromptOverlay(
   951	    requestId: Int,
   952	    currentDestination: CinerificDestination,
   953	    suppressed: Boolean,
   954	    modifier: Modifier = Modifier
   955	) {
   956	    var visible by remember { mutableStateOf(false) }
   957	
   958	    LaunchedEffect(requestId, suppressed) {
   959	        if (suppressed) {
   960	            visible = false
   961	            return@LaunchedEffect
   962	        }
   963	        if (requestId == 0) {
   964	            visible = false
   965	            return@LaunchedEffect
   966	        }
   967	        visible = true
   968	        delay(FAVORITES_FULL_PROMPT_VISIBLE_MS)
   969	        visible = false
   970	    }
   971	
   972	    val alpha by animateFloatAsState(
   973	        targetValue = if (visible) 1f else 0f,
   974	        animationSpec = tween(durationMillis = if (visible) 140 else 190),
   975	        label = "favorites-full-prompt-alpha"
   976	    )
   977	    val promptScale by animateFloatAsState(
   978	        targetValue = if (visible) 1f else 0.94f,
   979	        animationSpec = tween(durationMillis = if (visible) 140 else 190),
   980	        label = "favorites-full-prompt-scale"
   981	    )
   982	
   983	    if (suppressed || requestId == 0 || (!visible && alpha <= 0.01f)) return
   984	
   985	    BoxWithConstraints(modifier = modifier) {
   986	        val stageScale = maxWidth.value / FIGMA_FRAME_WIDTH
   987	        val isPortraitDetail = currentDestination == CinerificDestination.ProgramDetails &&
   988	            maxHeight > maxWidth
   989	        val promptY = if (isPortraitDetail) {
   990	            DETAIL_HERO_LOGO_CENTER_Y - FAVORITES_FULL_PROMPT_HEIGHT / 2f
   991	        } else {
   992	            FAVORITES_FULL_PROMPT_Y
   993	        }
   994	        FavoritesFullPromptBubble(
   995	            stageScale = stageScale,
   996	            modifier = Modifier
   997	                .absoluteOffset(
   998	                    x = (FAVORITES_FULL_PROMPT_X * stageScale).dp,
   999	                    y = (promptY * stageScale).dp
  1000	                )
  1001	                .graphicsLayer {
  1002	                    this.alpha = alpha
  1003	                    scaleX = promptScale
  1004	                    scaleY = promptScale
  1005	                    transformOrigin = TransformOrigin(0.5f, 0f)
  1006	                }
  1007	        )
  1008	    }
  1009	}
  1010	
  1011	@Composable
  1012	private fun FavoritesFullPromptBubble(
  1013	    stageScale: Float,
  1014	    modifier: Modifier = Modifier
  1015	) {
  1016	    Box(
  1017	        modifier = modifier.requiredSize(
  1018	            width = (FAVORITES_FULL_PROMPT_WIDTH * stageScale).dp,
  1019	            height = (FAVORITES_FULL_PROMPT_HEIGHT * stageScale).dp
  1020	        )
  1021	    ) {
  1022	        Canvas(modifier = Modifier.fillMaxSize()) {
  1023	            val xScale = size.width / FAVORITES_FULL_PROMPT_WIDTH
  1024	            val yScale = size.height / FAVORITES_FULL_PROMPT_HEIGHT
  1025	            val pointer = Path().apply {
  1026	                moveTo(47.5f * xScale, 0f)
  1027	                lineTo(63f * xScale, FAVORITES_FULL_PROMPT_BOX_TOP * yScale)
  1028	                lineTo(32f * xScale, FAVORITES_FULL_PROMPT_BOX_TOP * yScale)
  1029	                close()
  1030	            }
  1031	            drawPath(pointer, ColorFavoritesFullPromptAccent)
  1032	            drawRoundRect(
  1033	                color = ColorFavoritesFullPromptFill,
  1034	                topLeft = Offset(0f, FAVORITES_FULL_PROMPT_BOX_TOP * yScale),
  1035	                size = Size(
  1036	                    width = FAVORITES_FULL_PROMPT_WIDTH * xScale,
  1037	                    height = FAVORITES_FULL_PROMPT_BOX_HEIGHT * yScale
  1038	                ),
  1039	                cornerRadius = CornerRadius(
  1040	                    x = FAVORITES_FULL_PROMPT_RADIUS * xScale,
  1041	                    y = FAVORITES_FULL_PROMPT_RADIUS * yScale
  1042	                )
  1043	            )
  1044	            drawRoundRect(
  1045	                color = ColorFavoritesFullPromptAccent,
  1046	                topLeft = Offset(
  1047	                    x = FAVORITES_FULL_PROMPT_STROKE * xScale / 2f,
  1048	                    y = FAVORITES_FULL_PROMPT_BOX_TOP * yScale +
  1049	                        FAVORITES_FULL_PROMPT_STROKE * yScale / 2f
  1050	                ),
  1051	                size = Size(
  1052	                    width = (FAVORITES_FULL_PROMPT_WIDTH - FAVORITES_FULL_PROMPT_STROKE) * xScale,
  1053	                    height = (FAVORITES_FULL_PROMPT_BOX_HEIGHT - FAVORITES_FULL_PROMPT_STROKE) * yScale
  1054	                ),
  1055	                cornerRadius = CornerRadius(
  1056	                    x = FAVORITES_FULL_PROMPT_RADIUS * xScale,
  1057	                    y = FAVORITES_FULL_PROMPT_RADIUS * yScale
  1058	                ),
  1059	                style = Stroke(width = FAVORITES_FULL_PROMPT_STROKE * min(xScale, yScale))
  1060	            )
  1061	        }
  1062	        Text(
  1063	            text = "You've\nfilled\nup on\nfavorites!",
  1064	            color = ColorFavoritesFullPromptAccent,
  1065	            fontFamily = CinerificAppTextFontFamily,
  1066	            fontSize = (14f * stageScale).sp,
  1067	            fontWeight = FontWeight.Black,
  1068	            lineHeight = (21.252f * stageScale).sp,
  1069	            textAlign = TextAlign.Center,
  1070	            modifier = Modifier
  1071	                .absoluteOffset(
  1072	                    x = (FAVORITES_FULL_PROMPT_TEXT_X * stageScale).dp,
  1073	                    y = (FAVORITES_FULL_PROMPT_TEXT_Y * stageScale).dp
  1074	                )
  1075	                .requiredSize(
  1076	                    width = (FAVORITES_FULL_PROMPT_TEXT_WIDTH * stageScale).dp,
  1077	                    height = (FAVORITES_FULL_PROMPT_TEXT_HEIGHT * stageScale).dp
  1078	                )
  1079	        )
  1080	    }
  1081	}

  1083	@Composable
  1084	private fun BootIntroFromFigma(progress: Float) {
  1085	    val p = progress.coerceIn(0f, 1f)
  1086	    val images = rememberIntroImages()
  1087	    val blackToPurple = linearSegmentMs(
  1088	        p,
  1089	        SCREEN_BLACK_HOLD_MS,
  1090	        SCREEN_BLACK_HOLD_MS + SCREEN_PINK_MS
  1091	    )
  1092	    val purpleToSettle = easedSegmentMs(p, 2500, 3900)
  1093	    val background = introBackgroundBrush(
  1094	        solidProgress = blackToPurple,
  1095	        gradientProgress = purpleToSettle
  1096	    )
  1097	    val logoAlpha = linearSegmentMs(p, 90, LOGO_ENTRY_START_MS)
  1098	    val logoEntryProgress = bouncySegmentMs(p, LOGO_ENTRY_START_MS, LOGO_ENTRY_END_MS)
  1099	    val logoFinalProgress = easedSegmentMs(p, FINAL_SETTLE_START_MS, FINAL_SETTLE_END_MS)
  1100	    val avatarSettleAlpha = easedSegmentMs(p, 2300, FINAL_SETTLE_END_MS)
  1101	    val avatarYOffset = lerpFloat(56f, 0f, avatarSettleAlpha)
  1102	
  1103	    Canvas(modifier = Modifier.fillMaxSize()) {
  1104	        val stageScale = min(size.width / FIGMA_FRAME_WIDTH, size.height / FIGMA_FRAME_HEIGHT)
  1105	        val stageLeft = (size.width - FIGMA_FRAME_WIDTH * stageScale) / 2f
  1106	        val stageTop = (size.height - FIGMA_FRAME_HEIGHT * stageScale) / 2f
  1107	        val stackShiftY = signInStackShiftY(size.width > size.height)
  1108	
  1109	        drawRect(brush = background, size = size)
  1110	
  1111	        if (logoAlpha > 0.01f) {
  1112	            val baseBounds = FigmaBounds(x = 222f, y = 150f, w = 750f, h = 535f)
  1113	            val simpleEntry = lerpBounds(
  1114	                start = FigmaBounds(x = 447f, y = -417f, w = 300f, h = 214f),
  1115	                end = baseBounds,
  1116	                amount = logoEntryProgress
  1117	            )
  1118	            val eyesEntry = lerpBounds(
  1119	                start = FigmaBounds(x = 453f, y = 1037f, w = 300f, h = 214f),
  1120	                end = baseBounds,
  1121	                amount = logoEntryProgress
  1122	            )
  1123	            val finalBounds = FigmaBounds(x = 297f, y = 11f, w = 600f, h = 428f)
  1124	
  1125	            drawFigmaImage(
  1126	                image = images.logoSimple,
  1127	                bounds = lerpBounds(simpleEntry, finalBounds, logoFinalProgress),
  1128	                stageLeft = stageLeft,
  1129	                stageTop = stageTop,
  1130	                stageScale = stageScale,
  1131	                alpha = logoAlpha
  1132	            )
  1133	            drawFigmaImage(
  1134	                image = images.logoEyes,
  1135	                bounds = lerpBounds(eyesEntry, finalBounds, logoFinalProgress),
  1136	                stageLeft = stageLeft,
  1137	                stageTop = stageTop,
  1138	                stageScale = stageScale,
  1139	                alpha = logoAlpha
  1140	            )
  1141	        }
  1142	
  1143	        if (avatarSettleAlpha > 0.01f) {
  1144	            val y = avatarYOffset
  1145	            drawFigmaImage(images.steveAvatar, FigmaBounds(152f, 450f + stackShiftY + y, SIGN_IN_AVATAR_SIZE, SIGN_IN_AVATAR_SIZE), stageLeft, stageTop, stageScale, avatarSettleAlpha, clipCircle = true)
  1146	            drawFigmaImage(images.martinAvatar, FigmaBounds(390f, 450f + stackShiftY + y, SIGN_IN_AVATAR_SIZE, SIGN_IN_AVATAR_SIZE), stageLeft, stageTop, stageScale, avatarSettleAlpha, clipCircle = true)
  1147	            drawFigmaImage(images.jannyAvatar, FigmaBounds(628f, 454f + stackShiftY + y, SIGN_IN_AVATAR_SIZE, SIGN_IN_AVATAR_SIZE), stageLeft, stageTop, stageScale, avatarSettleAlpha, clipCircle = true)
  1148	            drawFigmaImage(images.guestAvatar, FigmaBounds(866f, 450f + stackShiftY + y, SIGN_IN_AVATAR_SIZE, SIGN_IN_AVATAR_SIZE), stageLeft, stageTop, stageScale, avatarSettleAlpha, clipCircle = true)
  1149	
  1150	            drawFigmaImage(images.steveName, FigmaBounds(130f, SIGN_IN_NAME_TOP + stackShiftY + y, 220f, 72f), stageLeft, stageTop, stageScale, avatarSettleAlpha)
  1151	            drawFigmaImage(images.martinName, FigmaBounds(368f, SIGN_IN_NAME_TOP + stackShiftY + y, 220f, 72f), stageLeft, stageTop, stageScale, avatarSettleAlpha)
  1152	            drawFigmaImage(images.jannyName, FigmaBounds(606f, SIGN_IN_NAME_TOP + stackShiftY + y, 220f, 72f), stageLeft, stageTop, stageScale, avatarSettleAlpha)
  1153	            drawFigmaImage(images.guestName, FigmaBounds(854f, SIGN_IN_NAME_TOP + stackShiftY + y, 200f, 72f), stageLeft, stageTop, stageScale, avatarSettleAlpha)
  1154	        }
  1155	    }
  1156	}

  1349	
  1350	private fun DrawScope.drawFigmaImage(
  1351	    image: ImageBitmap,
  1352	    bounds: FigmaBounds,
  1353	    stageLeft: Float,
  1354	    stageTop: Float,
  1355	    stageScale: Float,
  1356	    alpha: Float = 1f,
  1357	    clipCircle: Boolean = false
  1358	) {
  1359	    val left = stageLeft + bounds.x * stageScale
  1360	    val top = stageTop + bounds.y * stageScale
  1361	    val width = bounds.w * stageScale
  1362	    val height = bounds.h * stageScale
  1363	
  1364	    if (clipCircle) {
  1365	        val clip = Path().apply {
  1366	            addOval(Rect(left, top, left + width, top + height))
  1367	        }
  1368	        clipPath(clip) {
  1369	            drawImageBitmap(image, left, top, width, height, alpha)
  1370	        }
  1371	    } else {
  1372	        drawImageBitmap(image, left, top, width, height, alpha)
  1373	    }
  1374	}
  1375	
  1376	private fun DrawScope.drawImageBitmap(
  1377	    image: ImageBitmap,
  1378	    left: Float,
  1379	    top: Float,
  1380	    width: Float,
  1381	    height: Float,
  1382	    alpha: Float
  1383	) {
  1384	    drawImage(
  1385	        image = image,
  1386	        dstOffset = IntOffset(left.roundToInt(), top.roundToInt()),
  1387	        dstSize = IntSize(width.roundToInt(), height.roundToInt()),
  1388	        alpha = alpha.coerceIn(0f, 1f)
  1389	    )
  1390	}

  1392	@Composable
  1393	private fun SettlingLogoLayer(
  1394	    scale: Float,
  1395	    entryProgress: Float,
  1396	    finalProgress: Float,
  1397	    alpha: Float
  1398	) {
  1399	    val baseBounds = FigmaBounds(x = 222f, y = 150f, w = 750f, h = 535f)
  1400	    val simpleEntry = lerpBounds(
  1401	        start = FigmaBounds(x = 447f, y = 1037f, w = 300f, h = 214f),
  1402	        end = baseBounds,
  1403	        amount = entryProgress
  1404	    )
  1405	    val eyesEntry = lerpBounds(
  1406	        start = FigmaBounds(x = 453f, y = -417f, w = 300f, h = 214f),
  1407	        end = baseBounds,
  1408	        amount = entryProgress
  1409	    )
  1410	    val finalBounds = FigmaBounds(x = 297f, y = 11f, w = 600f, h = 428f)
  1411	    val simpleBounds = lerpBounds(simpleEntry, finalBounds, finalProgress)
  1412	    val eyesBounds = lerpBounds(eyesEntry, finalBounds, finalProgress)
  1413	
  1414	    TransformedFigmaAssetImage(
  1415	        base = baseBounds,
  1416	        target = simpleBounds,
  1417	        scale = scale,
  1418	        resId = R.drawable.logo_simple_large,
  1419	        alpha = alpha
  1420	    )
  1421	    TransformedFigmaAssetImage(
  1422	        base = baseBounds,
  1423	        target = eyesBounds,
  1424	        scale = scale,
  1425	        resId = R.drawable.logo_eyes_large,
  1426	        alpha = alpha
  1427	    )
  1428	}

  1486	@Composable
  1487	private fun SignInPromptText(
  1488	    text: String,
  1489	    x: Float,
  1490	    y: Float,
  1491	    textSize: Float,
  1492	    fontWeight: FontWeight = FontWeight.SemiBold,
  1493	    textAlign: TextAlign = TextAlign.Center,
  1494	    scale: Float
  1495	) {
  1496	    val contentAlignment = when (textAlign) {
  1497	        TextAlign.Left -> Alignment.CenterStart
  1498	        TextAlign.Right -> Alignment.CenterEnd
  1499	        else -> Alignment.Center
  1500	    }
  1501	    Box(
  1502	        modifier = Modifier
  1503	            .absoluteOffset(
  1504	                x = figma(x, scale),
  1505	                y = figma(y, scale)
  1506	            )
  1507	            .requiredSize(
  1508	                width = figma(ACCOUNT_PROMPT_WIDTH, scale),
  1509	                height = figma(ACCOUNT_PROMPT_HEIGHT, scale)
  1510	            ),
  1511	        contentAlignment = contentAlignment
  1512	    ) {
  1513	        Text(
  1514	            text = text,
  1515	            color = Color.White,
  1516	            fontFamily = CinerificAppTextFontFamily,
  1517	            fontSize = (textSize * scale).sp,
  1518	            fontWeight = fontWeight,
  1519	            lineHeight = (ACCOUNT_PROMPT_LINE_HEIGHT * textSize / ACCOUNT_PROMPT_TEXT_SIZE * scale).sp,
  1520	            maxLines = 1,
  1521	            softWrap = false,
  1522	            textAlign = textAlign
  1523	        )
  1524	    }
  1525	}

  1563	    alpha: Float = 1f
  1564	) {

  1566	    FigmaAssetImage(
  1567	        x = 0f,

  1580	                .requiredSize(figma(1584f, scale), figma(1584f, scale))

  1582	                .background(Color.Black.copy(alpha = darkOverlay))

  1587	@Composable
  1588	private fun TransformedFigmaAssetImage(
  1589	    base: FigmaBounds,
  1590	    target: FigmaBounds,
  1591	    scale: Float,
  1592	    @DrawableRes resId: Int,
  1593	    alpha: Float = 1f
  1594	) {
  1595	    val density = LocalDensity.current.density
  1596	    Image(
  1597	        painter = painterResource(id = resId),
  1598	        contentDescription = null,
  1599	        contentScale = ContentScale.FillBounds,
  1600	        modifier = Modifier
  1601	            .absoluteOffset(x = figma(base.x, scale), y = figma(base.y, scale))
  1602	            .requiredSize(figma(base.w, scale), figma(base.h, scale))
  1603	            .graphicsLayer {
  1604	                this.alpha = alpha
  1605	                transformOrigin = TransformOrigin(0f, 0f)
  1606	                translationX = (target.x - base.x) * scale * density
  1607	                translationY = (target.y - base.y) * scale * density
  1608	                scaleX = target.w / base.w
  1609	                scaleY = target.h / base.h
  1610	            }
  1611	    )
  1612	}
  1613	
  1614	@Composable
  1615	private fun FigmaAssetImage(
  1616	    x: Float,
  1617	    y: Float,
  1618	    w: Float,
  1619	    h: Float,
  1620	    scale: Float,
  1621	    @DrawableRes resId: Int,
  1622	    alpha: Float = 1f
  1623	) {
  1624	    Image(
  1625	        painter = painterResource(id = resId),
  1626	        contentDescription = null,
  1627	        contentScale = ContentScale.FillBounds,
  1628	        modifier = Modifier
  1629	            .absoluteOffset(x = figma(x, scale), y = figma(y, scale))
  1630	            .requiredSize(figma(w, scale), figma(h, scale))
  1631	            .graphicsLayer(alpha = alpha)
  1632	    )
  1633	}
  1634	
  1635	@Composable
  1636	private fun FigmaStage(background: Brush, content: @Composable BoxWithConstraintsScope.(Float) -> Unit) {
  1637	    BoxWithConstraints(modifier = Modifier.fillMaxSize()) {
  1638	        val outerMaxWidth = this.maxWidth
  1639	        val outerMaxHeight = this.maxHeight
  1640	        val scale = min(outerMaxWidth.value / FIGMA_FRAME_WIDTH, outerMaxHeight.value / FIGMA_FRAME_HEIGHT)
  1641	        val stageWidth = figma(FIGMA_FRAME_WIDTH, scale)
  1642	        val stageHeight = figma(FIGMA_FRAME_HEIGHT, scale)
  1643	
  1644	        Box(
  1645	            modifier = Modifier
  1646	                .fillMaxSize()
  1647	                .background(background)
  1648	        ) {
  1649	            BoxWithConstraints(
  1650	                modifier = Modifier
  1651	                    .align(Alignment.Center)
  1652	                    .requiredSize(stageWidth, stageHeight)
  1653	            ) {
  1654	                content(scale)
  1655	            }
  1656	        }
  1657	    }
  1658	}
  1659	
  1660	private fun figma(px: Float, scale: Float) = (px * scale).dp
  1661	
  1662	private fun signInStackShiftY(isLandscape: Boolean): Float {
  1663	    return if (isLandscape) {
  1664	        SIGN_IN_LANDSCAPE_STACK_SHIFT_Y
  1665	    } else {
  1666	        SIGN_IN_PORTRAIT_STACK_SHIFT_Y
  1667	    }
  1668	}
  1669	
  1670	private fun signInAccountPromptExtraShiftY(isLandscape: Boolean): Float {
  1671	    return if (isLandscape) SIGN_IN_LANDSCAPE_ACCOUNT_PROMPT_EXTRA_SHIFT_Y else 0f
  1672	}
  1673	
  1674	private fun signInAccountPromptTextSize(isLandscape: Boolean): Float {
  1675	    return if (isLandscape) ACCOUNT_PROMPT_LANDSCAPE_TEXT_SIZE else ACCOUNT_PROMPT_TEXT_SIZE
  1676	}
  1677	
  1678	private fun introBackgroundBrush(solidProgress: Float, gradientProgress: Float): Brush {
  1679	    val solid = lerpColor(ColorFrame1Background, ColorFrame2Background, solidProgress)
  1680	    return Brush.verticalGradient(
  1681	        colors = listOf(
  1682	            lerpColor(solid, ColorIntroGradientTop, gradientProgress),
  1683	            lerpColor(solid, ColorIntroGradientCenter, gradientProgress),
  1684	            lerpColor(solid, ColorIntroGradientBottom, gradientProgress)
  1685	        )
  1686	    )
  1687	}
  1688	
  1689	private fun solidBrush(color: Color): Brush = Brush.verticalGradient(
  1690	    colors = listOf(color, color)
  1691	)
  1692	
  1693	private fun bootProgressAt(bootStartMillis: Long): Float {
  1694	    val elapsed = SystemClock.uptimeMillis() - bootStartMillis
  1695	    return (elapsed / BOOT_ANIMATION_MS.toFloat()).coerceIn(0f, 1f)
  1696	}
  1697	
  1698	private fun easedSegment(value: Float, start: Float, end: Float): Float {
  1699	    val progress = ((value - start) / (end - start)).coerceIn(0f, 1f)
  1700	    return FastOutSlowInEasing.transform(progress)
  1701	}
  1702	
  1703	private fun linearSegmentMs(progress: Float, startMs: Int, endMs: Int): Float {
  1704	    val ms = progress.coerceIn(0f, 1f) * BOOT_ANIMATION_MS
  1705	    return ((ms - startMs) / (endMs - startMs).toFloat()).coerceIn(0f, 1f)
  1706	}
  1707	
  1708	private fun easedSegmentMs(progress: Float, startMs: Int, endMs: Int): Float {
  1709	    return FastOutSlowInEasing.transform(linearSegmentMs(progress, startMs, endMs))
  1710	}
  1711	
  1712	private fun bouncySegmentMs(progress: Float, startMs: Int, endMs: Int): Float {
  1713	    return bouncyEasing(linearSegmentMs(progress, startMs, endMs))
  1714	}
  1715	
  1716	private fun bouncyEasing(value: Float): Float {
  1717	    val t = value.coerceIn(0f, 1f)
  1718	    val base = FastOutSlowInEasing.transform(t)
  1719	    val bounce = sin(t * PI.toFloat() * 4.5f) * (1f - t) * 0.13f
  1720	    return base + bounce
  1721	}
  1722	
  1723	private fun lerpFloat(start: Float, end: Float, amount: Float) = start + (end - start) * amount
  1724	
  1725	private fun lerpBounds(start: FigmaBounds, end: FigmaBounds, amount: Float) = FigmaBounds(
  1726	    x = lerpFloat(start.x, end.x, amount),
  1727	    y = lerpFloat(start.y, end.y, amount),
  1728	    w = lerpFloat(start.w, end.w, amount),
  1729	    h = lerpFloat(start.h, end.h, amount)
  1730	)
  1731	
  1732	private val CinerificIntroSnapshotSaver = listSaver<CinerificIntroSnapshot, Any>(
  1733	    save = { snapshot ->
  1734	        listOf(
  1735	            snapshot.activeFlowName,
  1736	            snapshot.usernameText,
  1737	            snapshot.passwordText,
  1738	            snapshot.confirmPasswordText,
  1739	            snapshot.emailText,
  1740	            snapshot.monthText,
  1741	            snapshot.dayText,
  1742	            snapshot.yearText,
  1743	            snapshot.subscriptionTierText,
  1744	            snapshot.forgotRecoveryTargetName,
  1745	            snapshot.forgotPasswordSubmissionStateName,
  1746	            snapshot.rememberMeChecked,
  1747	            snapshot.createAvatarIndex,
  1748	            snapshot.portraitProfileIndex
  1749	        )
  1750	    },
  1751	    restore = { values ->
  1752	        CinerificIntroSnapshot(
  1753	            activeFlowName = values.getOrNull(0) as? String ?: "",
  1754	            usernameText = values.getOrNull(1) as? String ?: "",
  1755	            passwordText = values.getOrNull(2) as? String ?: "",
  1756	            confirmPasswordText = values.getOrNull(3) as? String ?: "",
  1757	            emailText = values.getOrNull(4) as? String ?: "",
  1758	            monthText = values.getOrNull(5) as? String ?: "Mon",
  1759	            dayText = values.getOrNull(6) as? String ?: "Day",
  1760	            yearText = values.getOrNull(7) as? String ?: "Year",
  1761	            subscriptionTierText = values.getOrNull(8) as? String ?: "Subscription Tier",
  1762	            forgotRecoveryTargetName = values.getOrNull(9) as? String ?: "Password",
  1763	            forgotPasswordSubmissionStateName = values.getOrNull(10) as? String ?: "Idle",
  1764	            rememberMeChecked = values.getOrNull(11) as? Boolean ?: false,
  1765	            createAvatarIndex = values.getOrNull(12) as? Int ?: 0,
  1766	            portraitProfileIndex = values.getOrNull(13) as? Int ?: 0
  1767	        )
  1768	    }

   499	    fun handleSystemBack(): Boolean {
   500	        if (activeMockFlow == null || mockSignInTransitionTargetProgress <= 0f) return false
   501	        closeMockSignInScreen()
   502	        return true
   503	    }
   504	
   505	    fun restoreIntroSnapshot(snapshot: CinerificIntroSnapshot) {
   506	        if (appliedIntroSnapshot == snapshot) return
   507	
   508	        val restoredFlow = enumValueOrNull<MockAccountFlow>(snapshot.activeFlowName)
   509	        val restoredRecoveryTarget = enumValueOrNull<MockForgotRecoveryTarget>(
   510	            snapshot.forgotRecoveryTargetName
   511	        ) ?: MockForgotRecoveryTarget.Password
   512	        val restoredSubmissionState = enumValueOrNull<MockForgotPasswordSubmissionState>(
   513	            snapshot.forgotPasswordSubmissionStateName
   514	        ) ?: MockForgotPasswordSubmissionState.Idle
   515	
   516	        pressedAvatarProfile = null
   517	        clickAvatarProfile = null
   518	        pressedCreateAccountPrompt = false
   519	        pressedSignInPrompt = false
   520	        pressedForgotPasswordPrompt = false
   521	        pressedMockField = null
   522	        pressedRememberMe = false
   523	        pressedBackChevron = false
   524	        pressedMockCreateAvatarNav = null
   525	        activeMockCreateAvatarDrag = false
   526	        mockCreateAvatarDragMoved = false
   527	        mockCreateAvatarDragDeltaX = 0f
   528	        lockedMockCreateAvatarDragDirection = null
   529	        pressedMockDropdown = null
   530	        pressedMockDropdownOption = null
   531	        pressedForgotRecoveryTarget = null
   532	        pressedForgotPasswordSubmit = false
   533	        activeMockDropdownScroll = null
   534	        mockDropdownScrollMoved = false
   535	        mockDragReturnInProgress = false
   536	        activeComposingText = ""
   537	        expandedMockDropdown = null
   538	        focusedMockField = null
   539	        mockInputDimAwaitingField = null
   540	        mockInputDimActiveField = null
   541	        mockInputDimActiveDropdown = null
   542	
   543	        activeMockFlow = restoredFlow
   544	        if (restoredFlow == null) {
   545	            mockSignInStartMillis = null
   546	            mockSignInTransitionStartProgress = 0f
   547	            mockSignInTransitionTargetProgress = 0f
   548	        } else {
   549	            mockSignInStartMillis = SystemClock.uptimeMillis()
   550	            mockSignInTransitionStartProgress = 1f
   551	            mockSignInTransitionTargetProgress = 1f
   552	        }
   553	
   554	        rememberMeChecked = snapshot.rememberMeChecked
   555	        usernameText = snapshot.usernameText
   556	        mockCreateUsernameRevealProgress = if (usernameText.isEmpty()) 0f else 1f
   557	        passwordText = snapshot.passwordText
   558	        confirmPasswordText = snapshot.confirmPasswordText
   559	        emailText = snapshot.emailText
   560	        monthText = snapshot.monthText
   561	        dayText = snapshot.dayText
   562	        yearText = snapshot.yearText
   563	        subscriptionTierText = normalizedMembershipTierSelection(snapshot.subscriptionTierText)
   564	        forgotRecoveryTarget = restoredRecoveryTarget
   565	        forgotPasswordSubmissionState = restoredSubmissionState
   566	        forgotPasswordSubmissionStartMillis = if (
   567	            restoredSubmissionState == MockForgotPasswordSubmissionState.Loading
   568	        ) {
   569	            SystemClock.uptimeMillis()
   570	        } else {
   571	            null
   572	        }
   573	
   574	        mockCreateAvatarIndex = snapshot.createAvatarIndex.coerceIn(0, MOCK_CREATE_AVATAR_COUNT - 1)
   575	        portraitProfileIndex = snapshot.portraitProfileIndex.coerceIn(0, FINAL_AVATAR_TARGETS.lastIndex)
   576	        resetPortraitProfileCarousel()
   577	        mockCreateAvatarCarouselStartMillis = null
   578	        mockCreateAvatarCarouselFromIndex = mockCreateAvatarIndex
   579	        mockCreateAvatarCarouselToIndex = mockCreateAvatarIndex
   580	        mockCreateAvatarCarouselDirection = MockAvatarCarouselDirection.Next
   581	        mockCreateAvatarCarouselStartProgress = 0f
   582	        mockDropdownScrollOffsets.keys.forEach { dropdown ->
   583	            mockDropdownScrollOffsets[dropdown] = 0
   584	        }
   585	
   586	        restartMockFormLabelAnimation()
   587	        restartMockFormFocusAnimation()
   588	        restartMockLandscapeInputLiftAnimation()
   589	        restartMockInputDimAnimation()
   590	        appliedIntroSnapshot = currentIntroSnapshot()
   591	        postInvalidateOnAnimation()
   592	    }

   594	    override fun onSizeChanged(width: Int, height: Int, oldWidth: Int, oldHeight: Int) {
   595	        super.onSizeChanged(width, height, oldWidth, oldHeight)
   596	        pressedAvatarProfile = null
   597	        clickAvatarProfile = null
   598	        pressedCreateAccountPrompt = false
   599	        pressedSignInPrompt = false
   600	        pressedForgotPasswordPrompt = false
   601	        pressedMockField = null
   602	        pressedRememberMe = false
   603	        pressedBackChevron = false
   604	        pressedMockCreateAvatarNav = null
   605	        activeMockCreateAvatarDrag = false
   606	        mockCreateAvatarDragMoved = false
   607	        pressedMockDropdown = null
   608	        pressedMockDropdownOption = null
   609	        pressedForgotRecoveryTarget = null
   610	        pressedForgotPasswordSubmit = false
   611	        activeMockDropdownScroll = null
   612	        mockDropdownScrollMoved = false
   613	        mockDragReturnInProgress = false
   614	        resetPortraitProfileCarousel()
   615	        postInvalidateOnAnimation()
   616	    }
