   618	    override fun onApplyWindowInsets(insets: WindowInsets): WindowInsets {
   619	        val systemBars = WindowInsetsCompat.toWindowInsetsCompat(insets, this)
   620	            .getInsets(WindowInsetsCompat.Type.systemBars())
   621	        systemBarInsetTopPx = systemBars.top
   622	        systemBarInsetBottomPx = systemBars.bottom
   623	        postInvalidateOnAnimation()
   624	        return super.onApplyWindowInsets(insets)
   625	    }
   626	
   627	    override fun onDraw(canvas: Canvas) {
   628	        super.onDraw(canvas)
   629	
   630	        if (IntroAnimationClock.processStartMillis == 0L) {
   631	            IntroAnimationClock.processStartMillis = SystemClock.uptimeMillis()
   632	        }
   633	        val progress = bootProgressAt(IntroAnimationClock.processStartMillis)
   634	        val blackToPurple = linearSegmentMs(
   635	            progress,
   636	            SCREEN_BLACK_HOLD_MS,
   637	            SCREEN_BLACK_HOLD_MS + SCREEN_PINK_MS
   638	        )
   639	        val purpleToSettle = easedSegmentMs(progress, 2500, 3900)
   640	        drawIntroBackground(canvas, blackToPurple, purpleToSettle)
   641	
   642	        val stage = currentStageMetrics() ?: return
   643	        finishMockSignInTransitionIfNeeded()
   644	        finishMockFlowSwitchIfNeeded()
   645	        finishPortraitProfileCarouselIfNeeded()
   646	        finishMockCreateAvatarCarouselIfNeeded()
   647	        finishMockForgotPasswordSubmissionIfNeeded()
   648	        val mockProgress = mockSignInProgress()
   649	        updateMockFormFocusAnimation()
   650	        updateMockLandscapeInputLiftAnimation()
   651	        updateMockInputDimAnimation()
   652	        updateMockCreateUsernameRevealAnimation()
   653	        val formFocusMotion = FastOutSlowInEasing.transform(mockFormFocusProgress)
   654	
   655	        val logoAlpha = linearSegmentMs(progress, 90, LOGO_ENTRY_START_MS)
   656	        if (logoAlpha > 0.01f) {
   657	            drawSettlingLogo(canvas, stage.left, stage.top, stage.scale, progress, logoAlpha)
   658	        }
   659	
   660	        val avatarAlpha = easedSegmentMs(progress, 2300, FINAL_SETTLE_END_MS)
   661	        val mockMotion = FastOutSlowInEasing.transform(mockProgress)
   662	        val createWelcomeTransition = isMockCreateWelcomeTransition()
   663	        if (avatarAlpha > 0.01f) {
   664	            val stackAlpha = avatarAlpha * (1f - mockMotion)
   665	            val y = lerpFloat(56f, 0f, avatarAlpha) + MOCK_SIGN_IN_STACK_EXIT_Y * mockMotion
   666	            if (!createWelcomeTransition && stackAlpha > 0.01f) {
   667	                drawWelcomeAvatarStack(canvas, stage, y, stackAlpha)
   668	            }
   669	        }
   670	        if (mockSignInStartMillis != null) {
   671	            val outgoingFlow = outgoingMockFlow
   672	            if (outgoingFlow != null) {
   673	                val switchMotion = FastOutSlowInEasing.transform(mockFlowSwitchProgress())
   674	                drawMockSignInFormForFlow(
   675	                    canvas,
   676	                    outgoingFlow,
   677	                    stage.left,
   678	                    stage.top,
   679	                    stage.scale,
   680	                    1f - switchMotion,
   681	                    formFocusMotion,
   682	                    updateLabelAnimation = false
   683	                )
   684	                activeMockFlow?.let { incomingFlow ->
   685	                    drawMockSignInFormForFlow(
   686	                        canvas,
   687	                        incomingFlow,
   688	                        stage.left,
   689	                        stage.top,
   690	                        stage.scale,
   691	                        switchMotion,
   692	                        formFocusMotion,
   693	                        updateLabelAnimation = true
   694	                    )
   695	                }
   696	            } else {
   697	                drawMockSignInForm(canvas, stage.left, stage.top, stage.scale, mockMotion, formFocusMotion)
   698	            }
   699	        }
   700	        drawAccountPrompt(
   701	            canvas = canvas,
   702	            stageLeft = stage.left,
   703	            stageTop = if (isPortraitIntroLayout()) stage.top else accountPromptStageTop(stage.scale),
   704	            stageScale = stage.scale,
   705	            yOffset = if (mockSignInStartMillis == null) {
   706	                lerpFloat(56f, 0f, avatarAlpha)
   707	            } else {
   708	                0f
   709	            },
   710	            alpha = avatarAlpha
   711	        )
   712	        drawMockCreateAvatarTransitionOverlay(
   713	            canvas,
   714	            stage,
   715	            mockMotion,
   716	            formFocusMotion
   717	        )
   718	        if (mockSignInStartMillis != null) {
   719	            drawPortraitStickyBottomBannerForeground(
   720	                canvas,
   721	                stage,
   722	                blackToPurple,
   723	                purpleToSettle
   724	            )
   725	            drawBackChevron(
   726	                canvas,
   727	                ACCOUNT_PROMPT_SIGN_IN_CENTER_X,
   728	                mockBackChevronRowTopY(),
   729	                stage.left,
   730	                if (usesPortraitStackedAuthLayout()) {
   731	                    stage.top
   732	                } else {
   733	                    accountPromptStageTop(stage.scale)
   734	                },
   735	                stage.scale,
   736	                mockMotion
   737	            )
   738	        }
   739	
   740	        if (
   741	            progress < 1f ||
   742	            isMockSignInAnimating() ||
   743	            isMockFlowSwitchAnimating() ||
   744	            isMockFormLabelAnimating() ||
   745	            isMockFormFocusAnimating() ||
   746	            isMockLandscapeInputLiftAnimating() ||
   747	            isPortraitProfileCarouselAnimating() ||
   748	            isMockCreateAvatarCarouselAnimating() ||
   749	            isMockForgotPasswordSubmissionAnimating() ||
   750	            isMockInputDimAnimating() ||
   751	            isMockCreateUsernameRevealAnimating()
   752	        ) {
   753	            postInvalidateOnAnimation()
   754	        }
   755	    }
   756	
   757	    override fun onTouchEvent(event: MotionEvent): Boolean {
   758	        return when (event.actionMasked) {
   759	            MotionEvent.ACTION_DOWN -> {
   760	                mockTouchDownX = event.x
   761	                mockTouchDownY = event.y
   762	                mockDragReturnEligibleAtTouchDown = false
   763	                portraitProfileSwipeMoved = false
   764	                portraitProfileDragDeltaX = 0f
   765	                if (mockSignInStartMillis != null) {
   766	                    mockDragReturnInProgress = false
   767	                    mockDropdownScrollMoved = false
   768	                    portraitAuthScrollStartY = event.y
   769	                    portraitAuthScrollStartOffset = portraitAuthScrollY
   770	                    portraitAuthScrollMoved = false
   771	                    if (isMockFlowSwitchAnimating()) {
   772	                        return true
   773	                    }
   774	                    mockDragReturnEligibleAtTouchDown = isMockAuthScreenAtBottom()
   775	                    pressedCreateAccountPrompt = settledCreateAccountPromptHit(event.x, event.y)
   776	                    pressedSignInPrompt = !pressedCreateAccountPrompt &&
   777	                        settledSignInPromptHit(event.x, event.y)
   778	                    pressedForgotPasswordPrompt = !pressedCreateAccountPrompt &&
   779	                        !pressedSignInPrompt &&
   780	                        settledForgotPasswordPromptHit(event.x, event.y)
   781	                    if (
   782	                        pressedCreateAccountPrompt ||
   783	                        pressedSignInPrompt ||
   784	                        pressedForgotPasswordPrompt
   785	                    ) {
   786	                        postInvalidateOnAnimation()
   787	                        return true
   788	                    }
   789	                    val touchedDropdownOptions = mockDropdownOptionsHit(event.x, event.y)
   790	                    activeMockDropdownScroll = touchedDropdownOptions?.takeIf { mockDropdownCanScroll(it) }
   791	                    mockDropdownScrollStartY = event.y
   792	                    mockDropdownScrollStartOffset = activeMockDropdownScroll?.let { mockDropdownScrollOffset(it) } ?: 0
   793	                    pressedMockDropdownOption = if (touchedDropdownOptions != null) {
   794	                        mockDropdownOptionHit(event.x, event.y)
   795	                    } else {
   796	                        null
   797	                    }
   798	                    pressedMockDropdown = if (pressedMockDropdownOption == null && touchedDropdownOptions == null) {
   799	                        mockDropdownHit(event.x, event.y)
   800	                    } else {
   801	                        null
   802	                    }
   803	                    pressedForgotRecoveryTarget = if (
   804	                        touchedDropdownOptions == null &&
   805	                        pressedMockDropdownOption == null &&
   806	                        pressedMockDropdown == null
   807	                    ) {
   808	                        mockForgotRecoveryTargetHit(event.x, event.y)
   809	                    } else {
   810	                        null
   811	                    }
   812	                    pressedForgotPasswordSubmit = touchedDropdownOptions == null &&
   813	                        pressedMockDropdownOption == null &&
   814	                        pressedMockDropdown == null &&
   815	                        pressedForgotRecoveryTarget == null &&
   816	                        mockForgotPasswordSubmitHit(event.x, event.y)
   817	                    val touchedCreateAvatarCarousel = touchedDropdownOptions == null &&
   818	                        pressedMockDropdownOption == null &&
   819	                        pressedMockDropdown == null &&
   820	                        pressedForgotRecoveryTarget == null &&
   821	                        !pressedForgotPasswordSubmit &&
   822	                        mockCreateAvatarCarouselHit(event.x, event.y) &&
   823	                        !isMockCreateAvatarCarouselAnimating()
   824	                    activeMockCreateAvatarDrag = touchedCreateAvatarCarousel
   825	                    mockCreateAvatarDragMoved = false
   826	                    mockCreateAvatarDragDeltaX = 0f
   827	                    lockedMockCreateAvatarDragDirection = null
   828	                    pressedMockCreateAvatarNav = if (
   829	                        touchedDropdownOptions == null &&
   830	                        pressedMockDropdownOption == null &&
   831	                        pressedMockDropdown == null &&
   832	                        pressedForgotRecoveryTarget == null &&
   833	                        !pressedForgotPasswordSubmit &&
   834	                        touchedCreateAvatarCarousel
   835	                    ) {
   836	                        mockCreateAvatarNavHit(event.x, event.y)
   837	                    } else {
   838	                        null
   839	                    }
   840	                    pressedMockField = if (
   841	                        touchedDropdownOptions == null &&
   842	                        pressedMockDropdownOption == null &&
   843	                        pressedMockDropdown == null &&
   844	                        pressedForgotRecoveryTarget == null &&
   845	                        pressedMockCreateAvatarNav == null &&
   846	                        !pressedForgotPasswordSubmit &&
   847	                        !touchedCreateAvatarCarousel
   848	                    ) {
   849	                        mockSignInFieldHit(event.x, event.y)
   850	                    } else {
   851	                        null
   852	                    }
   853	                    pressedRememberMe = touchedDropdownOptions == null &&
   854	                        !touchedCreateAvatarCarousel &&
   855	                        !pressedForgotPasswordSubmit &&
   856	                        pressedMockField == null &&
   857	                        mockRememberMeHit(event.x, event.y)
   858	                    pressedBackChevron = pressedMockField == null &&
   859	                        touchedDropdownOptions == null &&
   860	                        !touchedCreateAvatarCarousel &&
   861	                        !pressedRememberMe &&
   862	                        pressedMockDropdown == null &&
   863	                        pressedMockDropdownOption == null &&
   864	                        pressedForgotRecoveryTarget == null &&
   865	                        pressedMockCreateAvatarNav == null &&
   866	                        !pressedForgotPasswordSubmit &&
   867	                        mockBackChevronHit(event.x, event.y)
   868	                    postInvalidateOnAnimation()
   869	                    return true
   870	                }
   871	                pressedCreateAccountPrompt = settledCreateAccountPromptHit(event.x, event.y)
   872	                pressedSignInPrompt = !pressedCreateAccountPrompt && settledSignInPromptHit(event.x, event.y)
   873	                pressedForgotPasswordPrompt = !pressedCreateAccountPrompt &&
   874	                    !pressedSignInPrompt &&
   875	                    settledForgotPasswordPromptHit(event.x, event.y)
   876	                pressedAvatarProfile = if (
   877	                    pressedCreateAccountPrompt ||
   878	                    pressedSignInPrompt ||
   879	                    pressedForgotPasswordPrompt ||
   880	                    isPortraitProfileCarouselAnimating()
   881	                ) {
   882	                    null
   883	                } else {
   884	                    settledAvatarHitProfile(event.x, event.y)
   885	                }
   886	                // This custom view owns the entire welcome surface. Returning false for a
   887	                // slightly imprecise DOWN lets AndroidView's parent cancel the rest of the
   888	                // gesture, so a valid UP never reaches the link or avatar hit test.
   889	                true
   890	            }
   891	            MotionEvent.ACTION_MOVE -> {
   892	                if (mockSignInStartMillis != null) {
   893	                    if (updatePortraitAuthScroll(event)) {
   894	                        return true
   895	                    }
   896	                    if (updateMockCreateAvatarDrag(event)) {
   897	                        return true
   898	                    }
   899	                    if (updateMockDropdownScroll(event)) {
   900	                        return true
   901	                    }
   902	                    if (mockDropdownOptionsHit(mockTouchDownX, mockTouchDownY) == null && shouldStartMockDragReturn(event)) {
   903	                        mockDragReturnInProgress = true
   904	                        portraitAuthScrollMoved = false
   905	                        closeMockSignInScreen()
   906	                    }
   907	                    return true
   908	                }
   909	                if (isPortraitIntroLayout() && pressedAvatarProfile != null) {
   910	                    val horizontalDelta = event.x - mockTouchDownX
   911	                    val verticalDelta = event.y - mockTouchDownY
   912	                    if (abs(horizontalDelta) > touchSlop && abs(horizontalDelta) > abs(verticalDelta)) {
   913	                        portraitProfileSwipeMoved = true
   914	                    }
   915	                    if (portraitProfileSwipeMoved) {
   916	                        portraitProfileDragDeltaX = horizontalDelta
   917	                        postInvalidateOnAnimation()
   918	                    }
   919	                    return true
   920	                }
   921	                true
   922	            }
   923	            MotionEvent.ACTION_UP -> {
   924	                mockDragReturnEligibleAtTouchDown = false
   925	                if (portraitAuthScrollMoved) {
   926	                    portraitAuthScrollMoved = false
   927	                    pressedMockField = null
   928	                    pressedMockDropdown = null
   929	                    pressedMockDropdownOption = null
   930	                    pressedMockCreateAvatarNav = null
   931	                    activeMockCreateAvatarDrag = false
   932	                    mockCreateAvatarDragMoved = false
   933	                    activeMockDropdownScroll = null
   934	                    mockDropdownScrollMoved = false
   935	                    postInvalidateOnAnimation()
   936	                    return true
   937	                }
   938	                if (mockDragReturnInProgress) {
   939	                    mockDragReturnInProgress = false
   940	                    pressedMockField = null
   941	                    pressedRememberMe = false
   942	                    pressedBackChevron = false
   943	                    pressedMockCreateAvatarNav = null
   944	                    activeMockCreateAvatarDrag = false
   945	                    mockCreateAvatarDragMoved = false
   946	                    mockCreateAvatarDragDeltaX = 0f
   947	                    lockedMockCreateAvatarDragDirection = null
   948	                    pressedMockDropdown = null
   949	                    pressedMockDropdownOption = null
   950	                    pressedForgotRecoveryTarget = null
   951	                    pressedForgotPasswordSubmit = false
   952	                    activeMockDropdownScroll = null
   953	                    mockDropdownScrollMoved = false
   954	                    return true
   955	                }
   956	                if (mockSignInStartMillis != null) {
   957	                    if (isMockFlowSwitchAnimating()) {
   958	                        pressedCreateAccountPrompt = false
   959	                        pressedSignInPrompt = false
   960	                        pressedForgotPasswordPrompt = false
   961	                        return true
   962	                    }
   963	                    val requestedFlow = when {
   964	                        pressedCreateAccountPrompt &&
   965	                            settledCreateAccountPromptHit(event.x, event.y) -> {
   966	                            MockAccountFlow.CreateAccount
   967	                        }
   968	                        pressedSignInPrompt &&
   969	                            settledSignInPromptHit(event.x, event.y) -> {
   970	                            MockAccountFlow.SignIn
   971	                        }
   972	                        pressedForgotPasswordPrompt &&
   973	                            settledForgotPasswordPromptHit(event.x, event.y) -> {
   974	                            MockAccountFlow.ForgotPassword
   975	                        }
   976	                        else -> null
   977	                    }
   978	                    pressedCreateAccountPrompt = false
   979	                    pressedSignInPrompt = false
   980	                    pressedForgotPasswordPrompt = false
   981	                    if (requestedFlow != null) {
   982	                        super.performClick()
   983	                        switchMockAccountFlow(requestedFlow)
   984	                        return true
   985	                    }
   986	                    val releasedDropdownOption = mockDropdownOptionHit(event.x, event.y)
   987	                    val releasedDropdown = mockDropdownHit(event.x, event.y)
   988	                    val releasedField = mockSignInFieldHit(event.x, event.y)
   989	                    val releasedRememberMe = mockRememberMeHit(event.x, event.y)
   990	                    val releasedBackChevron = mockBackChevronHit(event.x, event.y)
   991	                    val releasedCreateAvatarNav = mockCreateAvatarNavHit(event.x, event.y)
   992	                    val releasedForgotRecoveryTarget = mockForgotRecoveryTargetHit(event.x, event.y)
   993	                    val releasedForgotPasswordSubmit = mockForgotPasswordSubmitHit(event.x, event.y)
   994	                    val wasTouchingDropdownOptions = activeMockDropdownScroll != null ||
   995	                        mockDropdownOptionsHit(mockTouchDownX, mockTouchDownY) != null
   996	                    val wasScrollingDropdown = mockDropdownScrollMoved
   997	                    val wasPressingRememberMe = pressedRememberMe
   998	                    val wasPressingBackChevron = pressedBackChevron
   999	                    val wasPressingForgotRecoveryTarget = pressedForgotRecoveryTarget != null
  1000	                    val wasPressingForgotPasswordSubmit = pressedForgotPasswordSubmit
  1001	                    val wasDraggingCreateAvatar = activeMockCreateAvatarDrag
  1002	                    val createAvatarDragMoved = mockCreateAvatarDragMoved
  1003	                    val createAvatarDragDirection = mockCreateAvatarDragDirection()
  1004	                    val createAvatarDragProgress = mockCreateAvatarDragProgress()
  1005	                    val pressedDropdown = pressedMockDropdown
  1006	                    val pressedOption = pressedMockDropdownOption
  1007	                    val pressedCreateAvatarNav = pressedMockCreateAvatarNav
  1008	                    val pressedForgotRecoverySelection = pressedForgotRecoveryTarget
  1009	                    val shouldSelectDropdownOption = !wasScrollingDropdown &&
  1010	                        pressedOption != null &&
  1011	                        pressedOption == releasedDropdownOption
  1012	                    val shouldSelectForgotRecoveryTarget = pressedForgotRecoverySelection != null &&
  1013	                        pressedForgotRecoverySelection == releasedForgotRecoveryTarget
  1014	                    val shouldToggleDropdown = pressedDropdown != null && pressedDropdown == releasedDropdown
  1015	                    val shouldToggleRememberMe = wasPressingRememberMe && releasedRememberMe
  1016	                    val shouldCloseMockSignIn = wasPressingBackChevron && releasedBackChevron
  1017	                    val shouldSubmitForgotPassword = wasPressingForgotPasswordSubmit && releasedForgotPasswordSubmit
  1018	                    val shouldNavigateCreateAvatar = pressedCreateAvatarNav != null &&
  1019	                        !createAvatarDragMoved &&
  1020	                        pressedCreateAvatarNav == releasedCreateAvatarNav
  1021	                    val shouldSwipeCreateAvatar = wasDraggingCreateAvatar &&
  1022	                        createAvatarDragMoved &&
  1023	                        createAvatarDragDirection != null &&
  1024	                        createAvatarDragProgress >= MOCK_CREATE_AVATAR_DRAG_COMMIT_PROGRESS
  1025	                    val shouldFocusField = releasedField != null &&
  1026	                        !wasTouchingDropdownOptions &&
  1027	                        !wasPressingRememberMe &&
  1028	                        !wasPressingBackChevron &&
  1029	                        !wasPressingForgotRecoveryTarget &&
  1030	                        !wasPressingForgotPasswordSubmit &&
  1031	                        !wasDraggingCreateAvatar &&
  1032	                        pressedDropdown == null &&
  1033	                        pressedOption == null &&
  1034	                        pressedCreateAvatarNav == null &&
  1035	                        (pressedMockField == null || pressedMockField == releasedField)
  1036	                    pressedMockField = null
  1037	                    pressedRememberMe = false
  1038	                    pressedBackChevron = false
  1039	                    pressedMockCreateAvatarNav = null
  1040	                    activeMockCreateAvatarDrag = false
  1041	                    mockCreateAvatarDragMoved = false
  1042	                    mockCreateAvatarDragDeltaX = 0f
  1043	                    lockedMockCreateAvatarDragDirection = null
  1044	                    pressedMockDropdown = null
  1045	                    pressedMockDropdownOption = null
  1046	                    this.pressedForgotRecoveryTarget = null
  1047	                    pressedForgotPasswordSubmit = false
  1048	                    activeMockDropdownScroll = null
  1049	                    mockDropdownScrollMoved = false
  1050	                    super.performClick()
  1051	                    if (shouldCloseMockSignIn) {
  1052	                        closeMockSignInScreen()
  1053	                    } else if (shouldSwipeCreateAvatar && createAvatarDragDirection != null) {
  1054	                        expandedMockDropdown = null
  1055	                        clearMockSignInFieldFocus()
  1056	                        startMockCreateAvatarCarousel(createAvatarDragDirection, createAvatarDragProgress)
  1057	                    } else if (shouldNavigateCreateAvatar && pressedCreateAvatarNav != null) {
  1058	                        expandedMockDropdown = null
  1059	                        clearMockSignInFieldFocus()
  1060	                        startMockCreateAvatarCarousel(pressedCreateAvatarNav)
  1061	                    } else if (wasDraggingCreateAvatar) {
  1062	                        postInvalidateOnAnimation()
  1063	                    } else if (shouldSelectDropdownOption && pressedOption != null) {
  1064	                        setMockDropdownValue(pressedOption)
  1065	                        expandedMockDropdown = null
  1066	                        restartMockLandscapeInputLiftAnimation()
  1067	                        restartMockInputDimAnimation()
  1068	                        clearMockSignInFieldFocus()
  1069	                        postInvalidateOnAnimation()
  1070	                    } else if (shouldToggleDropdown && pressedDropdown != null) {
  1071	                        expandedMockDropdown = if (expandedMockDropdown == pressedDropdown) null else pressedDropdown
  1072	                        expandedMockDropdown?.let { prepareMockDropdownScroll(it) }
  1073	                        restartMockLandscapeInputLiftAnimation()
  1074	                        restartMockInputDimAnimation()
  1075	                        clearMockSignInFieldFocus()
  1076	                        postInvalidateOnAnimation()
  1077	                    } else if (shouldToggleRememberMe) {
  1078	                        rememberMeChecked = !rememberMeChecked
  1079	                        notifyIntroSnapshotChanged()
  1080	                        postInvalidateOnAnimation()
  1081	                    } else if (shouldSelectForgotRecoveryTarget && pressedForgotRecoverySelection != null) {
  1082	                        setMockForgotRecoveryTarget(pressedForgotRecoverySelection)
  1083	                    } else if (shouldSubmitForgotPassword) {
  1084	                        startMockForgotPasswordSubmission()
  1085	                    } else if (shouldFocusField && releasedField != null) {
  1086	                        expandedMockDropdown = null
  1087	                        restartMockLandscapeInputLiftAnimation()
  1088	                        restartMockInputDimAnimation()
  1089	                        focusMockSignInField(releasedField)
  1090	                    } else if (wasTouchingDropdownOptions || wasScrollingDropdown) {
  1091	                        postInvalidateOnAnimation()
  1092	                    } else if (!wasPressingRememberMe && !wasPressingBackChevron && !wasPressingForgotRecoveryTarget) {
  1093	                        if (expandedMockDropdown != null && activeMockFlow == MockAccountFlow.CreateAccount) {
  1094	                            expandedMockDropdown = null
  1095	                            restartMockLandscapeInputLiftAnimation()
  1096	                            restartMockInputDimAnimation()
  1097	                        }
  1098	                        clearMockSignInFieldFocus()
  1099	                    } else {
  1100	                        postInvalidateOnAnimation()
  1101	                    }
  1102	                    return true
  1103	                }
  1104	                val tapStayedWithinSlop =
  1105	                    abs(event.x - mockTouchDownX) <= touchSlop &&
  1106	                        abs(event.y - mockTouchDownY) <= touchSlop
  1107	                val shouldOpenMockCreateAccount = tapStayedWithinSlop &&
  1108	                    pressedCreateAccountPrompt &&
  1109	                    settledCreateAccountPromptHit(event.x, event.y)
  1110	                val shouldOpenMockSignIn = tapStayedWithinSlop &&
  1111	                    pressedSignInPrompt &&
  1112	                    settledSignInPromptHit(event.x, event.y)
  1113	                val shouldOpenMockForgotPassword = tapStayedWithinSlop &&
  1114	                    pressedForgotPasswordPrompt &&
  1115	                    settledForgotPasswordPromptHit(event.x, event.y)
  1116	                val releasedAvatarProfile = settledAvatarHitProfile(event.x, event.y)
  1117	                val swipeDirection = portraitProfileSwipeDirection()
  1118	                val swipeProgress = portraitProfileDragProgress()
  1119	                val shouldSwipeProfile = isPortraitIntroLayout() &&
  1120	                    pressedAvatarProfile != null &&
  1121	                    portraitProfileSwipeMoved &&
  1122	                    swipeProgress >= PORTRAIT_PROFILE_SWIPE_COMMIT_PROGRESS &&
  1123	                    swipeDirection != null
  1124	                val shouldNavigate = tapStayedWithinSlop &&
  1125	                    !shouldSwipeProfile &&
  1126	                    !portraitProfileSwipeMoved &&
  1127	                    pressedAvatarProfile != null &&
  1128	                    pressedAvatarProfile == releasedAvatarProfile
  1129	                clickAvatarProfile = releasedAvatarProfile
  1130	                pressedAvatarProfile = null
  1131	                pressedCreateAccountPrompt = false
  1132	                pressedSignInPrompt = false
  1133	                pressedForgotPasswordPrompt = false
  1134	                if (shouldSwipeProfile && swipeDirection != null) {
  1135	                    clickAvatarProfile = null
  1136	                    portraitProfileSwipeMoved = false
  1137	                    portraitProfileDragDeltaX = 0f
  1138	                    startPortraitProfileCarousel(swipeDirection, swipeProgress)
  1139	                    true
  1140	                } else if (shouldOpenMockCreateAccount) {
  1141	                    super.performClick()
  1142	                    openMockCreateAccountScreen()
  1143	                    true
  1144	                } else if (shouldOpenMockSignIn) {
  1145	                    super.performClick()
  1146	                    openMockSignInScreen()
  1147	                    true
  1148	                } else if (shouldOpenMockForgotPassword) {
  1149	                    super.performClick()
  1150	                    openMockForgotPasswordScreen()
  1151	                    true
  1152	                } else if (shouldNavigate) {
  1153	                    performClick()
  1154	                    true
  1155	                } else {
  1156	                    clickAvatarProfile = null
  1157	                    portraitProfileSwipeMoved = false
  1158	                    portraitProfileDragDeltaX = 0f
  1159	                    postInvalidateOnAnimation()
  1160	                    true
  1161	                }
  1162	            }
  1163	            MotionEvent.ACTION_CANCEL -> {
  1164	                pressedAvatarProfile = null
  1165	                clickAvatarProfile = null
  1166	                pressedCreateAccountPrompt = false
  1167	                pressedSignInPrompt = false
  1168	                pressedForgotPasswordPrompt = false
  1169	                pressedMockField = null
  1170	                pressedRememberMe = false
  1171	                pressedBackChevron = false
  1172	                pressedMockCreateAvatarNav = null
  1173	                activeMockCreateAvatarDrag = false
  1174	                mockCreateAvatarDragMoved = false
  1175	                mockCreateAvatarDragDeltaX = 0f
  1176	                lockedMockCreateAvatarDragDirection = null
  1177	                pressedMockDropdown = null
  1178	                pressedMockDropdownOption = null
  1179	                pressedForgotRecoveryTarget = null
  1180	                pressedForgotPasswordSubmit = false
  1181	                activeMockDropdownScroll = null
  1182	                mockDropdownScrollMoved = false
  1183	                mockDragReturnInProgress = false
  1184	                mockDragReturnEligibleAtTouchDown = false
  1185	                portraitAuthScrollMoved = false
  1186	                portraitProfileSwipeMoved = false
  1187	                portraitProfileDragDeltaX = 0f
  1188	                true
  1189	            }
  1190	            else -> pressedCreateAccountPrompt ||
  1191	                pressedSignInPrompt ||
  1192	                pressedForgotPasswordPrompt ||
  1193	                pressedAvatarProfile != null ||
  1194	                mockSignInStartMillis != null
  1195	        }
  1196	    }
  1197	
  1198	    override fun onCheckIsTextEditor(): Boolean {
  1199	        return mockSignInStartMillis != null && !isMockSignInClosing()
  1200	    }
  1201	
  1202	    override fun onCreateInputConnection(outAttrs: EditorInfo): InputConnection? {
  1203	        val field = focusedMockField ?: return null
  1204	        outAttrs.inputType = when (field) {
  1205	            MockSignInField.Password,
  1206	            MockSignInField.ConfirmPassword -> InputType.TYPE_CLASS_TEXT or InputType.TYPE_TEXT_VARIATION_PASSWORD
  1207	            MockSignInField.Email -> InputType.TYPE_CLASS_TEXT or InputType.TYPE_TEXT_VARIATION_EMAIL_ADDRESS
  1208	            MockSignInField.Month,
  1209	            MockSignInField.Day,
  1210	            MockSignInField.Year -> InputType.TYPE_CLASS_NUMBER
  1211	            else -> InputType.TYPE_CLASS_TEXT or InputType.TYPE_TEXT_VARIATION_NORMAL
  1212	        }
  1213	        outAttrs.imeOptions = if (field == activeMockFields().lastOrNull()) {
  1214	            EditorInfo.IME_ACTION_DONE
  1215	        } else {
  1216	            EditorInfo.IME_ACTION_NEXT
  1217	        }
  1218	        val textLength = mockSignInFieldText(field).length
  1219	        outAttrs.initialSelStart = textLength
  1220	        outAttrs.initialSelEnd = textLength
  1221	        return MockSignInInputConnection()
  1222	    }
  1223	
  1224	    override fun onKeyDown(keyCode: Int, event: KeyEvent): Boolean {
  1225	        if (focusedMockField != null) {
  1226	            return handleMockSignInKeyEvent(keyCode, event) || super.onKeyDown(keyCode, event)
  1227	        }
  1228	        return super.onKeyDown(keyCode, event)
  1229	    }
  1230	
  1231	    @Suppress("DEPRECATION")
  1232	    override fun onKeyMultiple(keyCode: Int, repeatCount: Int, event: KeyEvent): Boolean {
  1233	        val characters = event.getCharacters()
  1234	        if (focusedMockField != null && !characters.isNullOrEmpty()) {
  1235	            return appendMockSignInInput(characters)
  1236	        }
  1237	        return super.onKeyMultiple(keyCode, repeatCount, event)
  1238	    }
  1239	
  1240	    override fun performClick(): Boolean {
  1241	        super.performClick()
  1242	        if (!isFinalFrameSettled()) return false
  1243	        val profile = clickAvatarProfile ?: return false
  1244	        onAvatarSelected?.invoke(profile)
  1245	        clickAvatarProfile = null
  1246	        return true
  1247	    }

  1249	    private fun drawSettlingLogo(
  1250	        canvas: Canvas,
  1251	        stageLeft: Float,
  1252	        stageTop: Float,
  1253	        stageScale: Float,
  1254	        progress: Float,
  1255	        alpha: Float
  1256	    ) {
  1257	        val logoEntry = bouncySegmentMs(progress, LOGO_ENTRY_START_MS, LOGO_ENTRY_END_MS)
  1258	        val finalProgress = easedSegmentMs(progress, FINAL_SETTLE_START_MS, FINAL_SETTLE_END_MS)
  1259	        val focusShiftY = mockLogoFocusShiftY()
  1260	        val portraitAmount = portraitIntroAmount()
  1261	        val portraitLogo = Bounds(
  1262	            (FIGMA_FRAME_WIDTH - 600f * PORTRAIT_LOGO_SCALE) / 2f +
  1263	                PORTRAIT_LOGO_VISIBLE_CENTER_OFFSET_X,
  1264	            portraitLogoTop(),
  1265	            600f * PORTRAIT_LOGO_SCALE,
  1266	            LOGO_FINAL_HEIGHT * PORTRAIT_LOGO_SCALE
  1267	        )
  1268	        val portraitCenteredLogo = Bounds(
  1269	            x = portraitLogo.x + portraitLogo.w / 2f -
  1270	                portraitLogo.w * LOGO_PRE_SETTLE_SCALE / 2f,
  1271	            y = portraitStackCenter(0.5f) -
  1272	                portraitLogo.h * LOGO_PRE_SETTLE_SCALE / 2f,
  1273	            w = portraitLogo.w * LOGO_PRE_SETTLE_SCALE,
  1274	            h = portraitLogo.h * LOGO_PRE_SETTLE_SCALE
  1275	        )
  1276	        val base = offsetBounds(
  1277	            lerpBounds(Bounds(222f, 150f, 750f, 535f), portraitCenteredLogo, portraitAmount),
  1278	            focusShiftY
  1279	        )
  1280	        val final = offsetBounds(
  1281	            lerpBounds(Bounds(297f, LOGO_FINAL_TOP, 600f, LOGO_FINAL_HEIGHT), portraitLogo, portraitAmount),
  1282	            focusShiftY
  1283	        )
  1284	
  1285	        if (logoEntry >= 0.999f) {
  1286	            drawFigmaBitmap(canvas, logoCombined, lerpBounds(base, final, finalProgress), stageLeft, stageTop, stageScale, alpha)
  1287	            return
  1288	        }
  1289	
  1290	        val simpleEntry = lerpBounds(Bounds(447f, 1037f, 300f, 214f), base, logoEntry)
  1291	        val eyesEntry = lerpBounds(Bounds(453f, -417f, 300f, 214f), base, logoEntry)
  1292	        drawFigmaBitmap(canvas, logoSimple, lerpBounds(simpleEntry, final, finalProgress), stageLeft, stageTop, stageScale, alpha)
  1293	        drawFigmaBitmap(canvas, logoEyes, lerpBounds(eyesEntry, final, finalProgress), stageLeft, stageTop, stageScale, alpha)
  1294	    }
  1295	
  1296	    private fun drawPortraitProfile(
  1297	        canvas: Canvas,
  1298	        stage: StageMetrics,
  1299	        yOffset: Float,
  1300	        alpha: Float
  1301	    ) {
  1302	        val slideDistance = portraitAvatarSize()
  1303	        val dragDirection = portraitProfileSwipeDirection()
  1304	        val dragProgress = portraitProfileDragProgress()
  1305	        val carouselProgress = portraitProfileCarouselProgress()
  1306	        val carouselDirection = portraitProfileCarouselDirection.stageDirection
  1307	
  1308	        if (portraitProfileSwipeMoved && dragDirection != null && !isPortraitProfileCarouselAnimating()) {
  1309	            val direction = dragDirection.stageDirection
  1310	            drawPortraitProfileAtIndex(
  1311	                canvas, stage, yOffset, alpha * (1f - dragProgress), portraitProfileIndex,
  1312	                -direction * dragProgress * slideDistance
  1313	            )
  1314	            drawPortraitProfileAtIndex(
  1315	                canvas, stage, yOffset, alpha * dragProgress, portraitProfileIndexForDirection(dragDirection),
  1316	                direction * (1f - dragProgress) * slideDistance
  1317	            )
  1318	        } else if (isPortraitProfileCarouselAnimating()) {
  1319	            drawPortraitProfileAtIndex(
  1320	                canvas, stage, yOffset, alpha * (1f - carouselProgress), portraitProfileCarouselFromIndex,
  1321	                -carouselDirection * carouselProgress * slideDistance
  1322	            )
  1323	            drawPortraitProfileAtIndex(
  1324	                canvas, stage, yOffset, alpha * carouselProgress, portraitProfileCarouselToIndex,
  1325	                carouselDirection * (1f - carouselProgress) * slideDistance
  1326	            )
  1327	        } else {
  1328	            drawPortraitProfileAtIndex(canvas, stage, yOffset, alpha, portraitProfileIndex, 0f)
  1329	        }
  1330	    }
  1331	
  1332	    private fun drawWelcomeAvatarStack(
  1333	        canvas: Canvas,
  1334	        stage: StageMetrics,
  1335	        yOffset: Float,
  1336	        alpha: Float
  1337	    ) {
  1338	        if (isPortraitIntroLayout()) {
  1339	            drawPortraitProfile(canvas, stage, yOffset, alpha)
  1340	            return
  1341	        }
  1342	
  1343	        val stackShiftY = signInStackShiftY()
  1344	        drawFigmaBitmap(canvas, steveAvatar, Bounds(152f, 450f + stackShiftY + yOffset, SIGN_IN_AVATAR_SIZE, SIGN_IN_AVATAR_SIZE), stage.left, stage.top, stage.scale, alpha)
  1345	        drawFigmaBitmap(canvas, martinAvatar, Bounds(390f, 450f + stackShiftY + yOffset, SIGN_IN_AVATAR_SIZE, SIGN_IN_AVATAR_SIZE), stage.left, stage.top, stage.scale, alpha)
  1346	        drawFigmaBitmap(canvas, jannyAvatar, Bounds(628f, 454f + stackShiftY + yOffset, SIGN_IN_AVATAR_SIZE, SIGN_IN_AVATAR_SIZE), stage.left, stage.top, stage.scale, alpha)
  1347	        drawFigmaBitmap(canvas, guestAvatar, Bounds(866f, 450f + stackShiftY + yOffset, SIGN_IN_AVATAR_SIZE, SIGN_IN_AVATAR_SIZE), stage.left, stage.top, stage.scale, alpha)
  1348	
  1349	        drawFigmaBitmap(canvas, steveName, Bounds(130f, SIGN_IN_NAME_TOP + stackShiftY + yOffset, 220f, 72f), stage.left, stage.top, stage.scale, alpha)
  1350	        drawFigmaBitmap(canvas, martinName, Bounds(368f, SIGN_IN_NAME_TOP + stackShiftY + yOffset, 220f, 72f), stage.left, stage.top, stage.scale, alpha)
  1351	        drawFigmaBitmap(canvas, jannyName, Bounds(606f, SIGN_IN_NAME_TOP + stackShiftY + yOffset, 220f, 72f), stage.left, stage.top, stage.scale, alpha)
  1352	        drawFigmaBitmap(canvas, guestName, Bounds(854f, SIGN_IN_NAME_TOP + stackShiftY + yOffset, 200f, 72f), stage.left, stage.top, stage.scale, alpha)
  1353	    }

  1356	    private fun drawPortraitProfileAtIndex(
  1357	        canvas: Canvas,
  1358	        stage: StageMetrics,
  1359	        yOffset: Float,
  1360	        alpha: Float,
  1361	        profileIndex: Int,
  1362	        offsetX: Float
  1363	    ) {
  1364	        val profile = FINAL_AVATAR_TARGETS[profileIndex].profile
  1365	        val avatarBitmap = when (profile) {
  1366	            CinerificProfile.Steve -> steveAvatar
  1367	            CinerificProfile.Martin -> martinAvatar
  1368	            CinerificProfile.Janny -> jannyAvatar
  1369	            CinerificProfile.Guest -> guestAvatar
  1370	        }
  1371	        val nameBitmap = when (profile) {
  1372	            CinerificProfile.Steve -> steveName
  1373	            CinerificProfile.Martin -> martinName
  1374	            CinerificProfile.Janny -> jannyName
  1375	            CinerificProfile.Guest -> guestName
  1376	        }
  1377	        val avatarSize = portraitAvatarSize()
  1378	        val nameWidth = (if (profile == CinerificProfile.Guest) 200f else 220f) * PORTRAIT_PROFILE_SCALE
  1379	        drawFigmaBitmap(
  1380	            canvas,
  1381	            avatarBitmap,
  1382	            Bounds(
  1383	                (FIGMA_FRAME_WIDTH - avatarSize) / 2f + offsetX,
  1384	                portraitAvatarTop() + yOffset,
  1385	                avatarSize,
  1386	                avatarSize
  1387	            ),
  1388	            stage.left,
  1389	            stage.top,
  1390	            stage.scale,
  1391	            alpha
  1392	        )
  1393	        drawFigmaBitmap(
  1394	            canvas,
  1395	            nameBitmap,
  1396	            Bounds(
  1397	                (FIGMA_FRAME_WIDTH - nameWidth) / 2f + offsetX,
  1398	                portraitProfileNameTop() + yOffset,
  1399	                nameWidth,
  1400	                portraitProfileNameHeight()
  1401	            ),
  1402	            stage.left,
  1403	            stage.top,
  1404	            stage.scale,
  1405	            alpha
  1406	        )
  1407	    }
  1408	
  1409	    private fun drawIntroBackground(canvas: Canvas, solidProgress: Float, gradientProgress: Float) {
  1410	        val standardSignInBackground = standardSignInBackgroundForSize()
  1411	        if (gradientProgress <= 0.001f) {
  1412	            canvas.drawColor(lerpColor(COLOR_FRAME_1, COLOR_FRAME_2, solidProgress))
  1413	            return
  1414	        }
  1415	
  1416	        val solid = lerpColor(COLOR_FRAME_1, COLOR_FRAME_2, solidProgress)
  1417	        backgroundPaint.shader = LinearGradient(
  1418	            0f,
  1419	            0f,
  1420	            0f,
  1421	            height.toFloat(),
  1422	            intArrayOf(
  1423	                lerpColor(solid, COLOR_GRADIENT_TOP, gradientProgress),
  1424	                lerpColor(solid, COLOR_GRADIENT_CENTER, gradientProgress),
  1425	                lerpColor(solid, COLOR_GRADIENT_BOTTOM, gradientProgress)
  1426	            ),
  1427	            null,
  1428	            Shader.TileMode.CLAMP
  1429	        )
  1430	        canvas.drawRect(0f, 0f, width.toFloat(), height.toFloat(), backgroundPaint)
  1431	        backgroundPaint.shader = null
  1432	
  1433	        if (standardSignInBackground != null) {
  1434	            drawStandardSignInBlackMatte(canvas, gradientProgress)
  1435	            drawStandardSignInBackground(canvas, standardSignInBackground, gradientProgress)
  1436	        }
  1437	    }
  1438	
  1439	    private fun drawStandardSignInBlackMatte(canvas: Canvas, alpha: Float) {
  1440	        backgroundPaint.color = COLOR_FRAME_1
  1441	        backgroundPaint.alpha = (alpha.coerceIn(0f, 1f) * 255f).roundToInt()
  1442	        canvas.drawRect(0f, 0f, width.toFloat(), height.toFloat(), backgroundPaint)
  1443	        backgroundPaint.alpha = 255
  1444	    }
  1445	
  1446	    private fun drawStandardSignInBackground(canvas: Canvas, bitmap: Bitmap, alpha: Float) {
  1447	        tempRect.set(0f, 0f, width.toFloat(), height.toFloat())
  1448	        imagePaint.alpha = (alpha.coerceIn(0f, 1f) * STANDARD_BACKGROUND_SETTLED_ALPHA * 255f).roundToInt()
  1449	        canvas.drawBitmap(bitmap, null, tempRect, imagePaint)
  1450	        imagePaint.alpha = 255
  1451	    }
  1452	
  1453	    private fun drawFigmaBitmap(
  1454	        canvas: Canvas,
  1455	        bitmap: Bitmap,
  1456	        bounds: Bounds,
  1457	        stageLeft: Float,
  1458	        stageTop: Float,
  1459	        stageScale: Float,
  1460	        alpha: Float
  1461	    ) {
  1462	        tempRect.set(
  1463	            stageLeft + bounds.x * stageScale,
  1464	            stageTop + bounds.y * stageScale,
  1465	            stageLeft + (bounds.x + bounds.w) * stageScale,
  1466	            stageTop + (bounds.y + bounds.h) * stageScale
  1467	        )
  1468	        imagePaint.alpha = (alpha.coerceIn(0f, 1f) * 255f).roundToInt()
  1469	        canvas.drawBitmap(bitmap, null, tempRect, imagePaint)
  1470	        imagePaint.alpha = 255
  1471	    }
  1472	
  1473	    private fun drawAccountPrompt(
  1474	        canvas: Canvas,
  1475	        stageLeft: Float,
  1476	        stageTop: Float,
  1477	        stageScale: Float,
  1478	        yOffset: Float,
  1479	        alpha: Float
  1480	    ) {
  1481	        if (usesPortraitStackedAuthLayout(activeMockFlow) ||
  1482	            usesPortraitStackedAuthLayout(outgoingMockFlow)
  1483	        ) {
  1484	            return
  1485	        }
  1486	        val isLandscape = width > height
  1487	        val portraitAmount = portraitIntroAmount()
  1488	        val textSize = lerpFloat(
  1489	            signInAccountPromptTextSize(),
  1490	            ACCOUNT_PROMPT_TEXT_SIZE * PORTRAIT_ACTION_SCALE,
  1491	            portraitAmount
  1492	        ) * stageScale
  1493	        val paintAlpha = (alpha.coerceIn(0f, 1f) * 255f).roundToInt()
  1494	        accountPromptPaint.textSize = textSize
  1495	        formTitlePaint.textSize = textSize
  1496	        accountPromptPaint.alpha = paintAlpha
  1497	        formTitlePaint.alpha = paintAlpha
  1498	
  1499	        val accountPromptShiftY = if (isLandscape) SIGN_IN_LANDSCAPE_ACCOUNT_PROMPT_EXTRA_SHIFT_Y else 0f
  1500	        val standardCenterY = stageTop +
  1501	            (ACCOUNT_PROMPT_CENTER_Y + signInStackShiftY() + accountPromptShiftY + yOffset) * stageScale
  1502	        val secondaryMetrics = accountPromptPaint.fontMetrics
  1503	        val primaryMetrics = formTitlePaint.fontMetrics
  1504	        fun promptCenterY(portraitCenterY: Float): Float = lerpFloat(
  1505	            standardCenterY,
  1506	            stageTop + (portraitCenterY + yOffset) * stageScale,
  1507	            portraitAmount
  1508	        )
  1509	        val createCenterY = promptCenterY(portraitPromptCreateCenterY())
  1510	        val signInCenterY = promptCenterY(portraitPromptSignInCenterY())
  1511	        val forgotCenterY = promptCenterY(portraitPromptForgotCenterY())
  1512	        val createBaselineY = createCenterY - (secondaryMetrics.ascent + secondaryMetrics.descent) / 2f
  1513	        val signInBaselineY = signInCenterY - (primaryMetrics.ascent + primaryMetrics.descent) / 2f
  1514	        val forgotBaselineY = forgotCenterY - (secondaryMetrics.ascent + secondaryMetrics.descent) / 2f
  1515	        val portraitCenterX = FIGMA_FRAME_WIDTH / 2f
  1516	        if (
  1517	            activeMockFlow != MockAccountFlow.CreateAccount &&
  1518	            outgoingMockFlow != MockAccountFlow.CreateAccount
  1519	        ) {
  1520	            accountPromptPaint.textAlign = Paint.Align.CENTER
  1521	            canvas.drawText(
  1522	                ACCOUNT_PROMPT_CREATE_TEXT,
  1523	                stageLeft + lerpFloat(accountPromptCreateCenterX(), portraitCenterX, portraitAmount) * stageScale,
  1524	                createBaselineY,
  1525	                accountPromptPaint
  1526	            )
  1527	        }
  1528	        if (
  1529	            activeMockFlow != MockAccountFlow.SignIn &&
  1530	            outgoingMockFlow != MockAccountFlow.SignIn
  1531	        ) {
  1532	            formTitlePaint.textAlign = Paint.Align.CENTER
  1533	            canvas.drawText(
  1534	                ACCOUNT_PROMPT_SIGN_IN_TEXT,
  1535	                stageLeft + lerpFloat(ACCOUNT_PROMPT_SIGN_IN_CENTER_X, portraitCenterX, portraitAmount) * stageScale,
  1536	                signInBaselineY,
  1537	                formTitlePaint
  1538	            )
  1539	        }
  1540	        if (
  1541	            activeMockFlow != MockAccountFlow.ForgotPassword &&
  1542	            outgoingMockFlow != MockAccountFlow.ForgotPassword
  1543	        ) {
  1544	            accountPromptPaint.textAlign = Paint.Align.CENTER
  1545	            canvas.drawText(
  1546	                ACCOUNT_PROMPT_FORGOT_TEXT,
  1547	                stageLeft + lerpFloat(accountPromptForgotCenterX(), portraitCenterX, portraitAmount) * stageScale,
  1548	                forgotBaselineY,
  1549	                accountPromptPaint
  1550	            )
  1551	        }
  1552	        accountPromptPaint.alpha = 255
  1553	        accountPromptPaint.textAlign = Paint.Align.CENTER
  1554	        formTitlePaint.alpha = 255
  1555	    }
  1556	
  1557	    private fun drawMockSignInForm(
  1558	        canvas: Canvas,
  1559	        stageLeft: Float,
  1560	        stageTop: Float,
  1561	        stageScale: Float,
  1562	        progress: Float,
  1563	        formFocusMotion: Float,
  1564	        updateLabelAnimation: Boolean = true
  1565	    ) {
  1566	        if (updateLabelAnimation) {
  1567	            updateMockFormLabelAnimation()
  1568	        }
  1569	
  1570	        val alpha = progress.coerceIn(0f, 1f)
  1571	        if (activeMockFlow == MockAccountFlow.CreateAccount) {
  1572	            drawMockCreateAccountForm(canvas, stageLeft, stageTop, stageScale, alpha, formFocusMotion)
  1573	            return
  1574	        }
  1575	        if (activeMockFlow == MockAccountFlow.ForgotPassword) {
  1576	            drawMockForgotPasswordForm(canvas, stageLeft, stageTop, stageScale, alpha, formFocusMotion)
  1577	            return
  1578	        }
  1579	
  1580	        val yOffset = MOCK_FORM_ENTRY_Y * (1f - alpha)
  1581	        val fieldFillAlpha = (alpha * 26f).roundToInt()
  1582	        val fieldStrokeAlpha = (alpha * 185f).roundToInt()
  1583	        val baseStage = StageMetrics(stageLeft, stageTop, stageScale)
  1584	        val usernameY = mockPrimaryFormY() + yOffset
  1585	        val passwordY = usernameY + MOCK_FORM_FIELD_HEIGHT + mockFormFieldGap()
  1586	        val focusShiftY = mockFormFocusShiftY(formFocusMotion)
  1587	        val contentStage = mockSignInContentStageMetrics(baseStage, yOffset, focusShiftY)
  1588	        val contentLeft = contentStage.left
  1589	        val contentTop = contentStage.top
  1590	        val contentScale = contentStage.scale
  1591	        val fieldX = mockFormX()
  1592	
  1593	        formFieldFillPaint.color = Color.argb(fieldFillAlpha, 255, 255, 255)
  1594	        formFieldStrokePaint.color = Color.argb(fieldStrokeAlpha, 255, 255, 255)
  1595	        formFieldStrokePaint.strokeWidth = MOCK_FORM_FIELD_STROKE_WIDTH * contentScale
  1596	        formCaretPaint.strokeWidth = 1.45f * contentScale
  1597	
  1598	        val rememberMeY = passwordY + MOCK_FORM_FIELD_HEIGHT + MOCK_REMEMBER_ME_TOP_GAP + focusShiftY
  1599	        val titleFieldY = usernameY + if (isPortraitIntroLayout()) 0f else focusShiftY
  1600	        if (isPortraitIntroLayout()) {
  1601	            canvas.save()
  1602	            canvas.clipRect(
  1603	                0f,
  1604	                stageTop + portraitLogoVisibleBottom() * stageScale,
  1605	                width.toFloat(),
  1606	                portraitAuthViewportBottomPx(baseStage)
  1607	            )
  1608	        }
  1609	        drawMockSignInTitle(canvas, titleFieldY, stageLeft, stageTop, stageScale, alpha)
  1610	        if (isPortraitIntroLayout()) {
  1611	            canvas.restore()
  1612	            canvas.save()
  1613	            canvas.clipRect(
  1614	                0f,
  1615	                portraitAuthViewportTopPx(baseStage),
  1616	                width.toFloat(),
  1617	                portraitAuthViewportBottomPx(baseStage)
  1618	            )
  1619	        }
  1620	        drawMockSignInField(canvas, MockSignInField.Username, fieldX, usernameY + focusShiftY, contentLeft, contentTop, contentScale, alpha)
  1621	        drawMockSignInField(canvas, MockSignInField.Password, fieldX, passwordY + focusShiftY, contentLeft, contentTop, contentScale, alpha)
  1622	        drawRememberMeCheckbox(
  1623	            canvas,
  1624	            fieldX,
  1625	            rememberMeY,
  1626	            contentLeft,
  1627	            contentTop,
  1628	            contentScale,
  1629	            alpha
  1630	        )
  1631	        drawMockInputDimOverlayAndActiveControl(
  1632	            canvas,
  1633	            contentLeft,
  1634	            contentTop,
  1635	            contentScale,
  1636	            alpha,
  1637	            yOffset,
  1638	            focusShiftY
  1639	        )
  1640	        if (isPortraitIntroLayout()) {
  1641	            canvas.restore()
  1642	        }
  1643	
  1644	        formLabelPaint.alpha = 255
  1645	        formInputPaint.alpha = 255
  1646	        formTitlePaint.alpha = 255
  1647	        formCaretPaint.alpha = 255
  1648	    }

  1894	            focusShiftY
  1895	        )
  1896	        if (isPortraitIntroLayout()) {
  1897	            canvas.restore()
  1898	        }
  1899	
  1900	        formLabelPaint.alpha = 255
  1901	        formInputPaint.alpha = 255
  1902	        formTitlePaint.alpha = 255
  1903	        formCaretPaint.alpha = 255
  1904	    }
  1905	
  1906	    private fun drawMockInputDimOverlayAndActiveControl(
  1907	        canvas: Canvas,
  1908	        stageLeft: Float,
  1909	        stageTop: Float,
  1910	        stageScale: Float,
  1911	        alpha: Float,
  1912	        yOffset: Float,
  1913	        focusShiftY: Float
  1914	    ) {
  1915	        val dimProgress = mockInputDimProgress.coerceIn(0f, 1f)
  1916	        if (dimProgress <= 0.01f) return
  1917	
  1918	        inputDimPaint.alpha = (dimProgress * MOCK_INPUT_DIM_ALPHA * 255f).roundToInt()
  1919	        canvas.drawRect(0f, 0f, width.toFloat(), height.toFloat(), inputDimPaint)
  1920	        inputDimPaint.alpha = 255
  1921	
  1922	        formFieldFillPaint.color = Color.argb((alpha * 26f).roundToInt(), 255, 255, 255)
  1923	        formFieldStrokePaint.color = Color.argb((alpha * 185f).roundToInt(), 255, 255, 255)
  1924	        formFieldStrokePaint.strokeWidth = MOCK_FORM_FIELD_STROKE_WIDTH * stageScale
  1925	        formCaretPaint.strokeWidth = 1.45f * stageScale
  1926	
  1927	        val activeDropdown = expandedMockDropdown ?: mockInputDimActiveDropdown
  1928	        if (activeDropdown != null && activeMockFlow == MockAccountFlow.CreateAccount) {
  1929	            drawMockDropdownControl(
  1930	                canvas,
  1931	                activeDropdown,
  1932	                mockDropdownX(activeDropdown),
  1933	                mockDropdownY(activeDropdown, yOffset, focusShiftY),
  1934	                stageLeft,
  1935	                stageTop,
  1936	                stageScale,
  1937	                alpha,
  1938	                mockDropdownWidth(activeDropdown)
  1939	            )
  1940	            if (expandedMockDropdown == activeDropdown) {
  1941	                drawMockDropdownOptions(canvas, stageLeft, stageTop, stageScale, alpha)
  1942	            }
  1943	            return
  1944	        }
  1945	
  1946	        val activeField = focusedMockField ?: mockInputDimActiveField ?: return
  1947	        drawMockSignInField(
  1948	            canvas,
  1949	            activeField,
  1950	            mockActiveFieldX(activeField),
  1951	            mockActiveFieldY(activeField, yOffset, focusShiftY),
  1952	            stageLeft,
  1953	            stageTop,
  1954	            stageScale,
  1955	            alpha,
  1956	            mockActiveFieldWidth(activeField)
  1957	        )
  1958	    }

  1984	        }
  1985	    }

  1994	    ) {

  1998	        val sourceTextSize = if (portrait) portraitTextSize else signInAccountPromptTextSize() * stageScale

  2006	        }) * stageScale

  2024	            lerpFloat(sourceBaselineY, targetBaselineY, motion),

  2026	        )
  2027	    }

  2121	    private fun mockFormTitleTargetBaseline(
  2122	        title: String,
  2123	        contentTopY: Float,
  2124	        stageTop: Float,
  2125	        stageScale: Float,
  2126	        targetTextSize: Float
  2127	    ): Float {
  2128	        formTitlePaint.textSize = targetTextSize
  2129	        val titlePath = Path()
  2130	        val titleBounds = RectF()
  2131	        formTitlePaint.getTextPath(title, 0, title.length, 0f, 0f, titlePath)
  2132	        titlePath.computeBounds(titleBounds, true)
  2133	        val contentScaleRatio = if (isPortraitIntroLayout()) portraitCreateContentScale() else 1f
  2134	        return stageTop + contentTopY * stageScale -
  2135	            mockFormFieldGap() * 2f * stageScale * contentScaleRatio -
  2136	            titleBounds.bottom +
  2137	            MOCK_FORM_TITLE_OPTICAL_BOTTOM_OFFSET * stageScale * contentScaleRatio
  2138	    }

  2140	    private fun drawMockForgotPasswordButton(
  2141	        canvas: Canvas,
  2142	        x: Float,
  2143	        y: Float,
  2144	        stageLeft: Float,
  2145	        stageTop: Float,
  2146	        stageScale: Float,
  2147	        alpha: Float,
  2148	        buttonWidth: Float = MOCK_FORM_WIDTH
  2149	    ) {
  2150	        tempRect.set(
  2151	            stageLeft + x * stageScale,
  2152	            stageTop + y * stageScale,
  2153	            stageLeft + (x + buttonWidth) * stageScale,
  2154	            stageTop + (y + MOCK_FORGOT_PASSWORD_BUTTON_HEIGHT) * stageScale
  2155	        )
  2156	        val radius = MOCK_FORGOT_PASSWORD_BUTTON_RADIUS * stageScale
  2157	        val submissionState = forgotPasswordSubmissionState
  2158	        val enabled = mockForgotPasswordCanSubmit()
  2159	        val pressed = pressedForgotPasswordSubmit && enabled
  2160	        val fillAlpha = when {
  2161	            submissionState == MockForgotPasswordSubmissionState.Sent -> 86f
  2162	            submissionState == MockForgotPasswordSubmissionState.Loading -> 72f
  2163	            pressed -> 92f
  2164	            enabled -> 68f
  2165	            else -> 18f
  2166	        }
  2167	        val strokeAlpha = when {
  2168	            submissionState == MockForgotPasswordSubmissionState.Sent -> 245f
  2169	            submissionState == MockForgotPasswordSubmissionState.Loading -> 228f
  2170	            pressed -> 238f
  2171	            enabled -> 210f
  2172	            else -> 118f
  2173	        }
  2174	        formFieldFillPaint.shader = null
  2175	        formFieldFillPaint.color = Color.argb((alpha * fillAlpha).roundToInt(), 255, 255, 255)
  2176	        formFieldStrokePaint.color = Color.argb((alpha * strokeAlpha).roundToInt(), 255, 255, 255)
  2177	        formFieldStrokePaint.strokeWidth = MOCK_FORM_FIELD_STROKE_WIDTH * stageScale
  2178	        canvas.drawRoundRect(tempRect, radius, radius, formFieldFillPaint)
  2179	        canvas.drawRoundRect(tempRect, radius, radius, formFieldStrokePaint)
  2180	        if (submissionState == MockForgotPasswordSubmissionState.Loading) {
  2181	            drawMockForgotPasswordButtonShimmer(canvas, tempRect, radius, alpha)
  2182	        }
  2183	
  2184	        formTitlePaint.textSize = MOCK_REMEMBER_ME_TEXT_SIZE * stageScale
  2185	        formTitlePaint.textAlign = Paint.Align.CENTER
  2186	        val metrics = formTitlePaint.fontMetrics
  2187	        val baselineY = tempRect.centerY() - (metrics.ascent + metrics.descent) / 2f
  2188	        val maxTextWidth = buttonWidth * stageScale - MOCK_FORM_LABEL_INSET_X * stageScale * 2f
  2189	        val showCheck = submissionState == MockForgotPasswordSubmissionState.Sent
  2190	        val checkSize = if (showCheck) MOCK_FORGOT_PASSWORD_SENT_CHECK_SIZE * stageScale else 0f
  2191	        val checkGap = if (showCheck) MOCK_FORGOT_PASSWORD_SENT_CHECK_GAP * stageScale else 0f
  2192	        val textMaxWidth = (maxTextWidth - checkSize - checkGap).coerceAtLeast(maxTextWidth * 0.5f)
  2193	        val buttonText = trailingFittingText(
  2194	            mockForgotPasswordButtonText(),
  2195	            if (showCheck) textMaxWidth else maxTextWidth,
  2196	            formTitlePaint
  2197	        )
  2198	        val textAlpha = (alpha.coerceIn(0f, 1f) * when {
  2199	            submissionState == MockForgotPasswordSubmissionState.Loading -> 226f
  2200	            submissionState == MockForgotPasswordSubmissionState.Sent -> 255f
  2201	            enabled -> 255f
  2202	            else -> 138f
  2203	        }).roundToInt()
  2204	        formTitlePaint.alpha = textAlpha
  2205	        val textX = if (showCheck) {
  2206	            val textWidth = formTitlePaint.measureText(buttonText)
  2207	            val groupWidth = checkSize + checkGap + textWidth
  2208	            val groupLeft = tempRect.centerX() - groupWidth / 2f
  2209	            val checkLeft = groupLeft
  2210	            val checkCenterY = tempRect.centerY()
  2211	            backChevronPaint.alpha = textAlpha
  2212	            backChevronPaint.strokeWidth = 2.1f * stageScale
  2213	            canvas.drawLine(
  2214	                checkLeft + checkSize * 0.18f,
  2215	                checkCenterY + checkSize * 0.03f,
  2216	                checkLeft + checkSize * 0.42f,
  2217	                checkCenterY + checkSize * 0.26f,
  2218	                backChevronPaint
  2219	            )
  2220	            canvas.drawLine(
  2221	                checkLeft + checkSize * 0.42f,
  2222	                checkCenterY + checkSize * 0.26f,
  2223	                checkLeft + checkSize * 0.84f,
  2224	                checkCenterY - checkSize * 0.28f,
  2225	                backChevronPaint
  2226	            )
  2227	            groupLeft + checkSize + checkGap + textWidth / 2f
  2228	        } else {
  2229	            tempRect.centerX()
  2230	        }
  2231	        canvas.drawText(
  2232	            buttonText,
  2233	            textX,
  2234	            baselineY,
  2235	            formTitlePaint
  2236	        )
  2237	    }
  2238	
  2239	    private fun drawMockForgotPasswordButtonShimmer(
  2240	        canvas: Canvas,
  2241	        buttonRect: RectF,
  2242	        radius: Float,
  2243	        alpha: Float
  2244	    ) {
  2245	        val progress = mockForgotPasswordSubmissionProgress()
  2246	        val sweepWidth = buttonRect.width() * 0.44f
  2247	        val sweepCenterX = buttonRect.left + buttonRect.width() * (progress * 1.35f - 0.18f)
  2248	        val sweepLeft = sweepCenterX - sweepWidth / 2f
  2249	        val sweepRight = sweepCenterX + sweepWidth / 2f
  2250	        formFieldFillPaint.shader = LinearGradient(
  2251	            sweepLeft,
  2252	            buttonRect.top,
  2253	            sweepRight,
  2254	            buttonRect.top,
  2255	            intArrayOf(
  2256	                Color.argb(0, 255, 255, 255),
  2257	                Color.argb((alpha.coerceIn(0f, 1f) * 54f).roundToInt(), 255, 255, 255),
  2258	                Color.argb(0, 255, 255, 255)
  2259	            ),
  2260	            floatArrayOf(0f, 0.5f, 1f),
  2261	            Shader.TileMode.CLAMP
  2262	        )
  2263	        canvas.save()
  2264	        tempPath.reset()
  2265	        tempPath.addRoundRect(buttonRect, radius, radius, Path.Direction.CW)
  2266	        canvas.clipPath(tempPath)
  2267	        canvas.drawRect(sweepLeft, buttonRect.top, sweepRight, buttonRect.bottom, formFieldFillPaint)
  2268	        canvas.restore()
  2269	        formFieldFillPaint.shader = null
  2270	    }

  2272	    private fun drawMockForgotRecoverySelector(

  2320	            MockForgotRecoveryTarget.Password,
  2321	            layout.passwordBoxX,

  2324	            stageScale,
  2325	            alpha

  2329	    private fun drawMockForgotRecoveryOption(
  2330	        canvas: Canvas,
  2331	        target: MockForgotRecoveryTarget,
  2332	        left: Float,
  2333	        top: Float,
  2334	        boxWidth: Float,
  2335	        stageScale: Float,
  2336	        alpha: Float
  2337	    ) {
  2338	        val right = left + boxWidth
  2339	        val bottom = top + MOCK_FORGOT_PASSWORD_SELECTOR_BOX_HEIGHT * stageScale
  2340	        tempRect.set(left, top, right, bottom)
  2341	        val selected = forgotRecoveryTarget == target
  2342	        val pressed = pressedForgotRecoveryTarget == target
  2343	        val fillAlpha = (alpha * when {
  2344	            pressed -> 82f
  2345	            selected -> 64f
  2346	            else -> 14f
  2347	        }).roundToInt()
  2348	        val strokeAlpha = (alpha * when {
  2349	            pressed -> 245f
  2350	            selected -> 235f
  2351	            else -> 142f
  2352	        }).roundToInt()
  2353	        val radius = MOCK_FORM_FIELD_RADIUS * stageScale
  2354	
  2355	        formFieldFillPaint.color = Color.argb(fillAlpha, 255, 255, 255)
  2356	        formFieldStrokePaint.color = Color.argb(strokeAlpha, 255, 255, 255)
  2357	        formFieldStrokePaint.strokeWidth = MOCK_FORM_FIELD_STROKE_WIDTH * stageScale
  2358	        canvas.drawRoundRect(tempRect, radius, radius, formFieldFillPaint)
  2359	        canvas.drawRoundRect(tempRect, radius, radius, formFieldStrokePaint)
  2360	
  2361	        formInputPaint.textSize = MOCK_FORGOT_PASSWORD_SELECTOR_OPTION_TEXT_SIZE * stageScale
  2362	        formInputPaint.alpha = (alpha.coerceIn(0f, 1f) * if (selected || pressed) 255f else 178f).roundToInt()
  2363	        formInputPaint.textAlign = Paint.Align.CENTER
  2364	        val metrics = formInputPaint.fontMetrics
  2365	        canvas.drawText(
  2366	            target.displayText,
  2367	            tempRect.centerX(),
  2368	            tempRect.centerY() - (metrics.ascent + metrics.descent) / 2f,
  2369	            formInputPaint
  2370	        )
  2371	    }

  2373	    private fun drawMockForgotPasswordBodyParagraph(
  2374	        canvas: Canvas,
  2375	        text: String,
  2376	        centerX: Float,
  2377	        topY: Float,
  2378	        maxWidth: Float,
  2379	        stageLeft: Float,
  2380	        stageTop: Float,
  2381	        stageScale: Float,
  2382	        alpha: Float
  2383	    ): Float {
  2384	        val paragraphs = text.split(Regex("\\n\\s*\\n"))
  2385	        val leadBottomY = drawMockFormParagraph(
  2386	            canvas,
  2387	            paragraphs.firstOrNull().orEmpty(),
  2388	            centerX,
  2389	            topY,
  2390	            maxWidth,
  2391	            stageLeft,
  2392	            stageTop,
  2393	            stageScale,
  2394	            alpha,
  2395	            MOCK_FORGOT_PASSWORD_BODY_LEAD_TEXT_SIZE,
  2396	            MOCK_FORGOT_PASSWORD_BODY_LEAD_LINE_HEIGHT,
  2397	            forceLineBreaks = true
  2398	        )
  2399	        val middleText = paragraphs.getOrNull(1) ?: return leadBottomY
  2400	        val middleBottomY = drawMockFormParagraph(
  2401	            canvas,
  2402	            middleText,
  2403	            centerX,
  2404	            leadBottomY + MOCK_FORGOT_PASSWORD_BODY_LINE_HEIGHT,
  2405	            maxWidth,
  2406	            stageLeft,
  2407	            stageTop,
  2408	            stageScale,
  2409	            alpha,
  2410	            MOCK_FORGOT_PASSWORD_BODY_MIDDLE_TEXT_SIZE,
  2411	            MOCK_FORGOT_PASSWORD_BODY_MIDDLE_LINE_HEIGHT,
  2412	            Paint.Align.LEFT
  2413	        )
  2414	        val bottomText = paragraphs.drop(2).joinToString("\n\n")
  2415	        if (bottomText.isEmpty()) return middleBottomY
  2416	        return drawMockFormParagraph(
  2417	            canvas,
  2418	            bottomText,
  2419	            centerX,
  2420	            middleBottomY + MOCK_FORGOT_PASSWORD_BODY_LINE_HEIGHT,
  2421	            maxWidth,
  2422	            stageLeft,
  2423	            stageTop,
  2424	            stageScale,
  2425	            alpha,
  2426	            MOCK_FORGOT_PASSWORD_BODY_MIDDLE_TEXT_SIZE,
  2427	            MOCK_FORGOT_PASSWORD_BODY_MIDDLE_LINE_HEIGHT,
  2428	            Paint.Align.LEFT
  2429	        )
  2430	    }
  2431	
  2432	    private fun drawMockFormParagraph(
  2433	        canvas: Canvas,
  2434	        text: String,
  2435	        centerX: Float,
  2436	        topY: Float,
  2437	        maxWidth: Float,
  2438	        stageLeft: Float,
  2439	        stageTop: Float,
  2440	        stageScale: Float,
  2441	        alpha: Float,
  2442	        textSize: Float,
  2443	        lineHeight: Float,
  2444	        textAlign: Paint.Align = Paint.Align.CENTER,
  2445	        forceLineBreaks: Boolean = false
  2446	    ): Float {
  2447	        formInputPaint.textSize = textSize * stageScale
  2448	        formInputPaint.alpha = (alpha.coerceIn(0f, 1f) * 218f).roundToInt()
  2449	        formInputPaint.textAlign = textAlign
  2450	
  2451	        val lines = if (forceLineBreaks) {
  2452	            text.split('\n').flatMap { line ->
  2453	                wrappedTextLines(line, maxWidth * stageScale, formInputPaint)
  2454	            }
  2455	        } else {
  2456	            wrappedTextLines(text, maxWidth * stageScale, formInputPaint)
  2457	        }
  2458	        val metrics = formInputPaint.fontMetrics
  2459	        val x = when (textAlign) {
  2460	            Paint.Align.LEFT -> stageLeft + (centerX - maxWidth / 2f) * stageScale
  2461	            Paint.Align.RIGHT -> stageLeft + (centerX + maxWidth / 2f) * stageScale
  2462	            Paint.Align.CENTER -> stageLeft + centerX * stageScale
  2463	        }
  2464	        var baselineY = stageTop + topY * stageScale - metrics.ascent
  2465	        lines.forEach { line ->
  2466	            canvas.drawText(line, x, baselineY, formInputPaint)
  2467	            baselineY += lineHeight * stageScale
  2468	        }
  2469	        return topY + lines.size * lineHeight
  2470	    }

  2472	    private fun drawMockCreateAccountAvatarText(
  2473	        canvas: Canvas,

  2477	        stageTop: Float,

  2500	                canvas,

  2502	                avatarBounds,
  2503	                stageLeft,
  2504	                stageTop,
  2505	                stageScale,
  2506	                alpha
  2507	            )
  2508	        }

  2511	            MockAvatarCarouselDirection.Previous,
  2512	            avatarBounds,
  2513	            stageLeft,
  2514	            stageTop,
  2515	            stageScale,
  2516	            alpha
  2517	        )

  2523	            stageTop,

  2529	    }

  2531	    private fun drawMockCreateAvatarTransitionOverlay(
  2532	        canvas: Canvas,
  2533	        stage: StageMetrics,
  2534	        mockMotion: Float,
  2535	        formFocusMotion: Float
  2536	    ) {
  2537	        if (!isPortraitIntroLayout()) return
  2538	
  2539	        if (isMockCreateWelcomeTransition()) {
  2540	            drawMockCreateWelcomeTransition(
  2541	                canvas,
  2542	                stage,
  2543	                mockMotion.coerceIn(0f, 1f),
  2544	                formFocusMotion
  2545	            )
  2546	            return
  2547	        }
  2548	
  2549	        val outgoingFlow = outgoingMockFlow
  2550	        val transitionProgress = when {
  2551	            outgoingFlow == MockAccountFlow.CreateAccount -> {
  2552	                1f - FastOutSlowInEasing.transform(mockFlowSwitchProgress())
  2553	            }
  2554	            activeMockFlow == MockAccountFlow.CreateAccount && outgoingFlow != null -> {
  2555	                FastOutSlowInEasing.transform(mockFlowSwitchProgress())
  2556	            }
  2557	            activeMockFlow == MockAccountFlow.CreateAccount && isMockSignInAnimating() -> mockMotion
  2558	            else -> return
  2559	        }.coerceIn(0f, 1f)
  2560	
  2561	        val yOffset = MOCK_FORM_ENTRY_Y * (1f - transitionProgress)
  2562	        val focusShiftY = mockFormFocusShiftY(formFocusMotion)
  2563	        val contentStage = mockCreateContentStageMetrics(stage, yOffset, focusShiftY)
  2564	
  2565	        if (isPortraitIntroLayout()) {
  2566	            canvas.save()
  2567	            canvas.clipRect(
  2568	                0f,
  2569	                portraitAuthViewportTopPx(stage),
  2570	                width.toFloat(),
  2571	                portraitAuthViewportBottomPx(stage)
  2572	            )
  2573	        }
  2574	        drawMockCreateAccountAvatarText(
  2575	            canvas,
  2576	            mockCreateAvatarColumnX(),
  2577	            mockCreateAvatarSectionY(yOffset, focusShiftY),
  2578	            contentStage.left,
  2579	            contentStage.top,
  2580	            contentStage.scale,
  2581	            1f
  2582	        )
  2583	        if (isPortraitIntroLayout()) {
  2584	            canvas.restore()
  2585	        }
  2586	    }
  2587	
  2588	    private fun isMockCreateWelcomeTransition(): Boolean {
  2589	        return isPortraitIntroLayout() &&
  2590	            activeMockFlow == MockAccountFlow.CreateAccount &&
  2591	            outgoingMockFlow == null &&
  2592	            isMockSignInAnimating()
  2593	    }

  2595	    private fun drawMockCreateWelcomeTransition(
  2596	        canvas: Canvas,
  2597	        stage: StageMetrics,
  2598	        motion: Float,
  2599	        formFocusMotion: Float
  2600	    ) {
  2601	        val focusShiftY = mockFormFocusShiftY(formFocusMotion)
  2602	        val accessoryYOffset = MOCK_SIGN_IN_STACK_EXIT_Y * (1f - motion)
  2603	        val contentStage = mockCreateContentStageMetrics(stage, accessoryYOffset, focusShiftY)
  2604	        val columnX = mockCreateAvatarColumnX()
  2605	        val sectionY = mockCreateAvatarSectionY(accessoryYOffset, focusShiftY)
  2606	        val settledSectionY = mockCreateAvatarSectionY(0f, 0f)
  2607	        val settledContentStage = mockCreateContentStageMetrics(stage, 0f, 0f)
  2608	        val settledAvatarBounds = mockCreateAvatarBounds(columnX, settledSectionY)
  2609	
  2610	        drawWelcomeAvatarRelatedAssetsForCreateTransition(canvas, stage, motion)
  2611	
  2612	        if (isPortraitIntroLayout()) {
  2613	            canvas.save()
  2614	            canvas.clipRect(
  2615	                0f,
  2616	                portraitAuthViewportTopPx(stage),
  2617	                width.toFloat(),
  2618	                portraitAuthViewportBottomPx(stage)
  2619	            )
  2620	        }
  2621	        drawMockCreateUsername(
  2622	            canvas,
  2623	            contentStage.left + (columnX + MOCK_FORM_WIDTH / 2f) * contentStage.scale,
  2624	            contentStage.top + mockCreateUsernameBaselineY(sectionY) * contentStage.scale,
  2625	            contentStage.scale,
  2626	            1f
  2627	        )
  2628	        drawMockCreateAvatarChevron(
  2629	            canvas,
  2630	            MockAvatarCarouselDirection.Previous,
  2631	            settledAvatarBounds,
  2632	            settledContentStage.left,
  2633	            settledContentStage.top,
  2634	            settledContentStage.scale,
  2635	            motion,
  2636	            mockCreateTransitionChevronCenterX(
  2637	                MockAvatarCarouselDirection.Previous,
  2638	                settledAvatarBounds,
  2639	                settledContentStage,
  2640	                motion
  2641	            )
  2642	        )
  2643	        drawMockCreateAvatarChevron(
  2644	            canvas,
  2645	            MockAvatarCarouselDirection.Next,
  2646	            settledAvatarBounds,
  2647	            settledContentStage.left,
  2648	            settledContentStage.top,
  2649	            settledContentStage.scale,
  2650	            motion,
  2651	            mockCreateTransitionChevronCenterX(
  2652	                MockAvatarCarouselDirection.Next,
  2653	                settledAvatarBounds,
  2654	                settledContentStage,
  2655	                motion
  2656	            )
  2657	        )
  2658	        if (isPortraitIntroLayout()) {
  2659	            canvas.restore()
  2660	        }
  2661	
  2662	        val destinationBounds = Bounds(
  2663	            (settledContentStage.left + settledAvatarBounds.x * settledContentStage.scale - stage.left) /
  2664	                stage.scale,
  2665	            (settledContentStage.top + settledAvatarBounds.y * settledContentStage.scale - stage.top) /
  2666	                stage.scale,
  2667	            settledAvatarBounds.w * settledContentStage.scale / stage.scale,
  2668	            settledAvatarBounds.h * settledContentStage.scale / stage.scale
  2669	        )
  2670	        val sourceBounds = mockCreateWelcomeAvatarSourceBounds()
  2671	        val movingBounds = lerpBounds(sourceBounds, destinationBounds, motion)
  2672	        val avatarIndex = mockCreateAvatarIndex.coerceIn(0, MOCK_CREATE_AVATAR_COUNT - 1)
  2673	
  2674	        drawFigmaBitmap(
  2675	            canvas,
  2676	            createAvatarBitmaps[avatarIndex],
  2677	            movingBounds,
  2678	            stage.left,
  2679	            stage.top,
  2680	            stage.scale,
  2681	            1f
  2682	        )
  2683	    }
