  2685	    private fun mockCreateTransitionChevronCenterX(
  2686	        direction: MockAvatarCarouselDirection,
  2687	        avatarBounds: Bounds,
  2688	        contentStage: StageMetrics,
  2689	        motion: Float
  2690	    ): Float {
  2691	        val settledCenterX = contentStage.left +
  2692	            mockCreateAvatarChevronCenterX(direction, avatarBounds) * contentStage.scale
  2693	        val halfChevronWidth = MOCK_CREATE_AVATAR_CHEVRON_WIDTH * contentStage.scale / 2f
  2694	        val edgeCenterX = if (direction == MockAvatarCarouselDirection.Previous) {
  2695	            -halfChevronWidth
  2696	        } else {
  2697	            width.toFloat() + halfChevronWidth
  2698	        }
  2699	        return lerpFloat(edgeCenterX, settledCenterX, motion.coerceIn(0f, 1f))
  2700	    }

  2702	    private fun mockCreateWelcomeAvatarSourceBounds(): Bounds {
  2703	        if (isPortraitIntroLayout()) {
  2704	            val avatarSize = portraitAvatarSize()
  2705	            return Bounds(
  2706	                (FIGMA_FRAME_WIDTH - avatarSize) / 2f,
  2707	                portraitAvatarTop(),
  2708	                avatarSize,
  2709	                avatarSize
  2710	            )
  2711	        }
  2712	
  2713	        val source = FINAL_AVATAR_TARGETS[
  2714	            mockCreateAvatarIndex.coerceIn(0, MOCK_CREATE_AVATAR_COUNT - 1)
  2715	        ].bounds
  2716	        return Bounds(source.x, source.y + signInStackShiftY(), source.w, source.h)
  2717	    }

  2719	    private fun drawWelcomeAvatarRelatedAssetsForCreateTransition(
  2720	        canvas: Canvas,
  2721	        stage: StageMetrics,
  2722	        motion: Float
  2723	    ) {
  2724	        val exitY = mockCreateRelatedAssetsExitY() * motion
  2725	        if (isPortraitIntroLayout()) {
  2726	            val profile = FINAL_AVATAR_TARGETS[portraitProfileIndex].profile
  2727	            val nameBitmap = when (profile) {
  2728	                CinerificProfile.Steve -> steveName
  2729	                CinerificProfile.Martin -> martinName
  2730	                CinerificProfile.Janny -> jannyName
  2731	                CinerificProfile.Guest -> guestName
  2732	            }
  2733	            val nameWidth = (if (profile == CinerificProfile.Guest) 200f else 220f) *
  2734	                PORTRAIT_PROFILE_SCALE
  2735	            drawFigmaBitmap(
  2736	                canvas,
  2737	                nameBitmap,
  2738	                Bounds(
  2739	                    (FIGMA_FRAME_WIDTH - nameWidth) / 2f,
  2740	                    portraitProfileNameTop() + exitY,
  2741	                    nameWidth,
  2742	                    portraitProfileNameHeight()
  2743	                ),
  2744	                stage.left,
  2745	                stage.top,
  2746	                stage.scale,
  2747	                1f
  2748	            )
  2749	            return
  2750	        }
  2751	
  2752	        val selectedIndex = mockCreateAvatarIndex.coerceIn(0, MOCK_CREATE_AVATAR_COUNT - 1)
  2753	        val avatarBitmaps = listOf(steveAvatar, martinAvatar, jannyAvatar, guestAvatar)
  2754	        val nameBitmaps = listOf(steveName, martinName, jannyName, guestName)
  2755	        val nameWidths = listOf(220f, 220f, 220f, 200f)
  2756	        val stackShiftY = signInStackShiftY()
  2757	        FINAL_AVATAR_TARGETS.forEachIndexed { index, target ->
  2758	            if (index != selectedIndex) {
  2759	                drawFigmaBitmap(
  2760	                    canvas,
  2761	                    avatarBitmaps[index],
  2762	                    Bounds(
  2763	                        target.bounds.x,
  2764	                        target.bounds.y + stackShiftY + exitY,
  2765	                        target.bounds.w,
  2766	                        target.bounds.h
  2767	                    ),
  2768	                    stage.left,
  2769	                    stage.top,
  2770	                    stage.scale,
  2771	                    1f
  2772	                )
  2773	            }
  2774	            drawFigmaBitmap(
  2775	                canvas,
  2776	                nameBitmaps[index],
  2777	                Bounds(
  2778	                    target.bounds.x - if (index == 3) 12f else 22f,
  2779	                    SIGN_IN_NAME_TOP + stackShiftY + exitY,
  2780	                    nameWidths[index],
  2781	                    72f
  2782	                ),
  2783	                stage.left,
  2784	                stage.top,
  2785	                stage.scale,
  2786	                1f
  2787	            )
  2788	        }
  2789	    }
  2790	
  2791	    private fun mockCreateRelatedAssetsExitY(): Float {
  2792	        return if (isPortraitIntroLayout()) {
  2793	            portraitSafeBottom() - portraitProfileNameTop() + portraitProfileNameHeight()
  2794	        } else {
  2795	            FIGMA_FRAME_HEIGHT - (SIGN_IN_NAME_TOP + signInStackShiftY()) + 72f
  2796	        }
  2797	    }

  2799	    private fun drawMockCreateUsername(
  2800	        canvas: Canvas,
  2801	        centerX: Float,
  2802	        baselineY: Float,
  2803	        stageScale: Float,
  2804	        alpha: Float
  2805	    ) {
  2806	        if (usernameText.isEmpty()) return
  2807	
  2808	        val maxTextWidth =
  2809	            (MOCK_FORM_WIDTH - MOCK_CREATE_USERNAME_MAX_WIDTH_INSET * 2f) * stageScale
  2810	        val preferredTextSize = MOCK_CREATE_USERNAME_TEXT_SIZE * stageScale
  2811	        createUsernameFillPaint.textSize = preferredTextSize
  2812	        val preferredTextWidth = createUsernameFillPaint.measureText(usernameText)
  2813	        val fittedTextSize = if (preferredTextWidth > maxTextWidth && preferredTextWidth > 0f) {
  2814	            preferredTextSize * maxTextWidth / preferredTextWidth
  2815	        } else {
  2816	            preferredTextSize
  2817	        }
  2818	        val paintAlpha = (alpha.coerceIn(0f, 1f) * 255f).roundToInt()
  2819	
  2820	        createUsernameStrokePaint.textSize = fittedTextSize
  2821	        createUsernameStrokePaint.strokeWidth = MOCK_CREATE_USERNAME_STROKE_WIDTH * stageScale
  2822	        createUsernameStrokePaint.alpha = paintAlpha
  2823	        createUsernameStrokePaint.setShadowLayer(
  2824	            MOCK_CREATE_USERNAME_SHADOW_RADIUS * stageScale,
  2825	            0f,
  2826	            MOCK_CREATE_USERNAME_SHADOW_OFFSET_Y * stageScale,
  2827	            Color.argb((paintAlpha * 0.25f).roundToInt(), 0, 0, 0)
  2828	        )
  2829	        canvas.drawText(usernameText, centerX, baselineY, createUsernameStrokePaint)
  2830	        createUsernameStrokePaint.clearShadowLayer()
  2831	
  2832	        createUsernameFillPaint.textSize = fittedTextSize
  2833	        createUsernameFillPaint.alpha = paintAlpha
  2834	        canvas.drawText(usernameText, centerX, baselineY, createUsernameFillPaint)
  2835	
  2836	        createUsernameStrokePaint.alpha = 255
  2837	        createUsernameFillPaint.alpha = 255
  2838	    }

  2860	
  2861	    private fun drawMockCreateAvatarMotionLayer(
  2862	        canvas: Canvas,
  2863	        bitmaps: List<Bitmap>,
  2864	        bounds: Bounds,
  2865	        stageLeft: Float,
  2866	        stageTop: Float,
  2867	        stageScale: Float,
  2868	        alpha: Float,
  2869	        transitionProgressOverride: Float? = null
  2870	    ) {
  2871	        val carouselProgress = transitionProgressOverride ?: mockCreateAvatarCarouselProgress()
  2872	        val slideDirection = mockCreateAvatarCarouselDirection.stageDirection
  2873	        val slideDistance = mockCreateAvatarSlideDistance(bounds.w)
  2874	        val dragDirection = mockCreateAvatarDragDirection()
  2875	        val dragProgress = transitionProgressOverride ?:
  2876	            mockCreateAvatarDragProgress(slideDistance, stageScale)
  2877	        val isDraggingAvatar = activeMockCreateAvatarDrag &&
  2878	            mockCreateAvatarDragMoved &&
  2879	            dragDirection != null &&
  2880	            !isMockCreateAvatarCarouselAnimating()
  2881	
  2882	        fun draw(index: Int, offsetX: Float) {
  2883	            drawFigmaBitmap(
  2884	                canvas,
  2885	                bitmaps[index.coerceIn(0, MOCK_CREATE_AVATAR_COUNT - 1)],
  2886	                Bounds(bounds.x + offsetX, bounds.y, bounds.w, bounds.h),
  2887	                stageLeft,
  2888	                stageTop,
  2889	                stageScale,
  2890	                alpha
  2891	            )
  2892	        }
  2893	
  2894	        if (isDraggingAvatar && dragDirection != null) {
  2895	            val direction = dragDirection.stageDirection
  2896	            draw(mockCreateAvatarIndex, -direction * dragProgress * slideDistance)
  2897	            draw(mockCreateAvatarIndexForDirection(dragDirection), direction * (1f - dragProgress) * slideDistance)
  2898	        } else if (isMockCreateAvatarCarouselAnimating()) {
  2899	            draw(mockCreateAvatarCarouselFromIndex, -slideDirection * carouselProgress * slideDistance)
  2900	            draw(mockCreateAvatarCarouselToIndex, slideDirection * (1f - carouselProgress) * slideDistance)
  2901	        } else {
  2902	            draw(mockCreateAvatarIndex, 0f)
  2903	        }
  2904	    }
  2905	
  2906	    private fun clipMockCreateAvatarCarousel(
  2907	        canvas: Canvas,
  2908	        avatarBounds: Bounds,
  2909	        stageLeft: Float,
  2910	        stageTop: Float,
  2911	        stageScale: Float
  2912	    ) {
  2913	        tempPath.reset()
  2914	        tempPath.addCircle(
  2915	            stageLeft + (avatarBounds.x + avatarBounds.w / 2f) * stageScale,
  2916	            stageTop + (avatarBounds.y + avatarBounds.h / 2f) * stageScale,
  2917	            avatarBounds.w * 0.5f * stageScale,
  2918	            Path.Direction.CW
  2919	        )
  2920	        canvas.clipPath(tempPath)
  2921	    }
  2922	
  2923	    private fun drawMockCreateAvatarChevron(
  2924	        canvas: Canvas,
  2925	        direction: MockAvatarCarouselDirection,
  2926	        avatarBounds: Bounds,
  2927	        stageLeft: Float,
  2928	        stageTop: Float,
  2929	        stageScale: Float,
  2930	        alpha: Float,
  2931	        centerXOverridePx: Float? = null
  2932	    ) {
  2933	        val centerX = centerXOverridePx ?:
  2934	            stageLeft + mockCreateAvatarChevronCenterX(direction, avatarBounds) * stageScale
  2935	        val centerY = stageTop + mockCreateAvatarChevronCenterY(avatarBounds) * stageScale
  2936	        val halfWidth = MOCK_CREATE_AVATAR_CHEVRON_WIDTH * stageScale / 2f
  2937	        val halfHeight = MOCK_CREATE_AVATAR_CHEVRON_HEIGHT * stageScale / 2f
  2938	        val pressedAlpha = if (pressedMockCreateAvatarNav == direction) 0.62f else 0.9f
  2939	        backChevronPaint.alpha = (alpha.coerceIn(0f, 1f) * pressedAlpha * 255f).roundToInt()
  2940	        backChevronPaint.strokeWidth = MOCK_CREATE_AVATAR_CHEVRON_STROKE_WIDTH * stageScale
  2941	
  2942	        if (direction == MockAvatarCarouselDirection.Previous) {
  2943	            canvas.drawLine(centerX + halfWidth, centerY - halfHeight, centerX - halfWidth, centerY, backChevronPaint)
  2944	            canvas.drawLine(centerX - halfWidth, centerY, centerX + halfWidth, centerY + halfHeight, backChevronPaint)
  2945	        } else {
  2946	            canvas.drawLine(centerX - halfWidth, centerY - halfHeight, centerX + halfWidth, centerY, backChevronPaint)
  2947	            canvas.drawLine(centerX + halfWidth, centerY, centerX - halfWidth, centerY + halfHeight, backChevronPaint)
  2948	        }
  2949	        backChevronPaint.alpha = 255
  2950	    }
  2951	
  2952	    private fun drawMockDropdownControl(
  2953	        canvas: Canvas,
  2954	        dropdown: MockDropdown,
  2955	        x: Float,
  2956	        y: Float,
  2957	        stageLeft: Float,
  2958	        stageTop: Float,
  2959	        stageScale: Float,
  2960	        alpha: Float,
  2961	        dropdownWidth: Float = MOCK_FORM_WIDTH
  2962	    ) {
  2963	        tempRect.set(
  2964	            stageLeft + x * stageScale,
  2965	            stageTop + y * stageScale,
  2966	            stageLeft + (x + dropdownWidth) * stageScale,
  2967	            stageTop + (y + MOCK_FORM_FIELD_HEIGHT) * stageScale
  2968	        )
  2969	        val radius = MOCK_FORM_FIELD_RADIUS * stageScale
  2970	        canvas.drawRoundRect(tempRect, radius, radius, formFieldFillPaint)
  2971	        canvas.drawRoundRect(tempRect, radius, radius, formFieldStrokePaint)
  2972	
  2973	        val textAlpha = (alpha.coerceIn(0f, 1f) * 255f).roundToInt()
  2974	        formInputPaint.textSize = mockDropdownTextSize(dropdown) * stageScale
  2975	        formInputPaint.alpha = textAlpha
  2976	        formInputPaint.textAlign = Paint.Align.LEFT
  2977	        val textInset = mockDropdownTextInsetX(dropdown) * stageScale
  2978	        val chevronInset = mockDropdownChevronInsetX(dropdown) * stageScale
  2979	        val displayText = mockDropdownDisplayText(dropdown)
  2980	        val membershipIcon = membershipTierArtworkIconForOption(displayText)
  2981	            ?.takeIf { dropdown == MockDropdown.SubscriptionTier }
  2982	        val iconLaneWidth = if (membershipIcon != null) {
  2983	            (MOCK_MEMBERSHIP_TIER_CONTROL_ICON_SIZE + MOCK_MEMBERSHIP_TIER_ICON_TEXT_GAP) * stageScale
  2984	        } else {
  2985	            0f
  2986	        }
  2987	        val maxTextWidth = (dropdownWidth * stageScale - textInset - iconLaneWidth - chevronInset * 2f)
  2988	            .coerceAtLeast(0f)
  2989	        if (membershipIcon != null) {
  2990	            drawMembershipTierIcon(
  2991	                canvas = canvas,
  2992	                bitmap = membershipIcon,
  2993	                left = tempRect.left + textInset,
  2994	                centerY = tempRect.centerY(),
  2995	                iconSize = MOCK_MEMBERSHIP_TIER_CONTROL_ICON_SIZE * stageScale,
  2996	                alpha = textAlpha
  2997	            )
  2998	        }
  2999	        canvas.drawText(
  3000	            trailingFittingText(displayText, maxTextWidth, formInputPaint),
  3001	            tempRect.left + textInset + iconLaneWidth,
  3002	            tempRect.centerY() - (formInputPaint.fontMetrics.ascent + formInputPaint.fontMetrics.descent) / 2f,
  3003	            formInputPaint
  3004	        )
  3005	
  3006	        val centerX = tempRect.right - chevronInset
  3007	        val centerY = tempRect.centerY() + 2f * stageScale
  3008	        val halfSize = mockDropdownChevronSize(dropdown) * stageScale / 2f
  3009	        backChevronPaint.alpha = textAlpha
  3010	        backChevronPaint.strokeWidth = 1.65f * stageScale
  3011	        canvas.drawLine(centerX - halfSize, centerY - halfSize / 2f, centerX, centerY + halfSize / 2f, backChevronPaint)
  3012	        canvas.drawLine(centerX, centerY + halfSize / 2f, centerX + halfSize, centerY - halfSize / 2f, backChevronPaint)
  3013	        backChevronPaint.alpha = 255
  3014	    }

  3016	    private fun drawMockDropdownOptions(
  3017	        canvas: Canvas,
  3018	        stageLeft: Float,
  3019	        stageTop: Float,
  3020	        stageScale: Float,
  3021	        alpha: Float
  3022	    ) {

  3024	        if (activeMockFlow != MockAccountFlow.CreateAccount) return

  3031	        )

  3033	        val top = stageTop + y * stageScale
  3034	        val right = stageLeft + (x + mockDropdownWidth(dropdown)) * stageScale
  3035	        val optionHeight = mockDropdownOptionHeight(dropdown) * stageScale
  3036	        val optionAlpha = 255
  3037	        val optionTextInset = mockDropdownOptionTextInsetX(dropdown) * stageScale
  3038	        val scrollbarReservedWidth = if (mockDropdownCanScroll(dropdown)) {
  3039	            (MOCK_DATE_DROPDOWN_SCROLLBAR_WIDTH + MOCK_DATE_DROPDOWN_SCROLLBAR_INSET_X * 2f) * stageScale
  3040	        } else {
  3041	            0f
  3042	        }
  3043

  3045	        visibleOptions.forEachIndexed { index, option ->
  3046	            val optionTop = top + index * optionHeight
  3047	            val optionBottom = optionTop + optionHeight
  3048	            val fillColor = if (option == mockDropdownSelectedValue(dropdown)) {
  3049	                Color.argb(optionAlpha, 76, 12, 94)
  3050	            } else {
  3051	                Color.argb(optionAlpha, 14, 2, 18)
  3052	            }

  3062	            formInputPaint.textAlign = Paint.Align.LEFT
  3063	            val metrics = formInputPaint.fontMetrics
  3064	            val membershipIcon = membershipTierArtworkIconForOption(option)
  3065	                ?.takeIf { dropdown == MockDropdown.SubscriptionTier }

  3068	            } else {

  3096	                right,

  3100	            )

  3104	    private fun drawMembershipTierIcon(
  3105	        canvas: Canvas,
  3106	        bitmap: Bitmap,
  3107	        left: Float,
  3108	        centerY: Float,
  3109	        iconSize: Float,
  3110	        alpha: Int
  3111	    ) {
  3112	        val bitmapAspect = bitmap.width.toFloat() / bitmap.height.toFloat()
  3113	        val width: Float
  3114	        val height: Float
  3115	        if (bitmapAspect >= 1f) {
  3116	            width = iconSize
  3117	            height = iconSize / bitmapAspect
  3118	        } else {
  3119	            width = iconSize * bitmapAspect
  3120	            height = iconSize
  3121	        }
  3122	        val top = centerY - height / 2f
  3123	        tempIconRect.set(left, top, left + width, top + height)
  3124	        imagePaint.alpha = alpha.coerceIn(0, 255)
  3125	        canvas.drawBitmap(bitmap, null, tempIconRect, imagePaint)
  3126	        imagePaint.alpha = 255
  3127	    }

  3129	    private fun drawMockDropdownScrollbar(
  3130	        canvas: Canvas,
  3131	        dropdown: MockDropdown,
  3132	        top: Float,
  3133	        right: Float,
  3134	        dropdownHeight: Float,
  3135	        stageScale: Float,
  3136	        alpha: Int
  3137	    ) {
  3138	        val options = mockDropdownOptions(dropdown)
  3139	        val visibleCount = mockDropdownVisibleOptionCount(dropdown)
  3140	        val maxOffset = mockDropdownMaxScrollOffset(dropdown)
  3141	        if (options.isEmpty() || maxOffset <= 0) return
  3142	
  3143	        val trackRight = right - MOCK_DATE_DROPDOWN_SCROLLBAR_INSET_X * stageScale
  3144	        val trackLeft = trackRight - MOCK_DATE_DROPDOWN_SCROLLBAR_WIDTH * stageScale
  3145	        val trackTop = top + MOCK_DATE_DROPDOWN_SCROLLBAR_INSET_Y * stageScale
  3146	        val trackBottom = top + dropdownHeight - MOCK_DATE_DROPDOWN_SCROLLBAR_INSET_Y * stageScale
  3147	        val trackHeight = (trackBottom - trackTop).coerceAtLeast(0f)
  3148	        val thumbHeight = maxOf(14f * stageScale, trackHeight * visibleCount / options.size)
  3149	            .coerceAtMost(trackHeight)
  3150	        val thumbProgress = mockDropdownScrollOffset(dropdown) / maxOffset.toFloat()
  3151	        val thumbTop = trackTop + (trackHeight - thumbHeight) * thumbProgress
  3152	        val radius = MOCK_DATE_DROPDOWN_SCROLLBAR_WIDTH * stageScale / 2f
  3153	
  3154	        tempRect.set(trackLeft, trackTop, trackRight, trackBottom)
  3155	        formFieldFillPaint.color = Color.argb((alpha * 0.28f).roundToInt(), 255, 255, 255)
  3156	        canvas.drawRoundRect(tempRect, radius, radius, formFieldFillPaint)
  3157	
  3158	        tempRect.set(trackLeft, thumbTop, trackRight, thumbTop + thumbHeight)
  3159	        formFieldFillPaint.color = Color.argb((alpha * 0.78f).roundToInt(), 255, 255, 255)
  3160	        canvas.drawRoundRect(tempRect, radius, radius, formFieldFillPaint)
  3161	    }

  3163	    private fun drawMockSignInField(
  3164	        canvas: Canvas,
  3165	        field: MockSignInField,
  3166	        x: Float,
  3167	        y: Float,
  3168	        stageLeft: Float,
  3169	        stageTop: Float,
  3170	        stageScale: Float,
  3171	        alpha: Float,
  3172	        fieldWidth: Float = MOCK_FORM_WIDTH
  3173	    ) {
  3174	        tempRect.set(
  3175	            stageLeft + x * stageScale,
  3176	            stageTop + y * stageScale,
  3177	            stageLeft + (x + fieldWidth) * stageScale,
  3178	            stageTop + (y + MOCK_FORM_FIELD_HEIGHT) * stageScale
  3179	        )
  3180	        val radius = MOCK_FORM_FIELD_RADIUS * stageScale
  3181	        canvas.drawRoundRect(tempRect, radius, radius, formFieldFillPaint)
  3182	        canvas.drawRoundRect(tempRect, radius, radius, formFieldStrokePaint)
  3183	
  3184	        val label = mockSignInFieldLabel(field)
  3185	        val labelProgress = mockSignInFieldLabelProgress(field)
  3186	        val textAlpha = (alpha.coerceIn(0f, 1f) * 255f).roundToInt()
  3187	        formLabelPaint.textSize = lerpFloat(
  3188	            MOCK_FORM_LABEL_TEXT_SIZE,
  3189	            MOCK_FORM_LABEL_TEXT_SIZE * MOCK_FORM_FLOATING_LABEL_SCALE,
  3190	            labelProgress
  3191	        ) * stageScale
  3192	        formLabelPaint.alpha = textAlpha
  3193	        formLabelPaint.textAlign = Paint.Align.LEFT
  3194	        val metrics = formLabelPaint.fontMetrics
  3195	        val placeholderBaselineY = tempRect.centerY() - (metrics.ascent + metrics.descent) / 2f
  3196	        val floatingBaselineY = tempRect.top + MOCK_FORM_LABEL_FLOAT_TOP_INSET_Y * stageScale - metrics.ascent
  3197	        val labelX = lerpFloat(
  3198	            tempRect.left + MOCK_FORM_LABEL_INSET_X * stageScale,
  3199	            tempRect.left + MOCK_FORM_LABEL_FLOAT_INSET_X * stageScale,
  3200	            labelProgress
  3201	        )
  3202	        val labelBaselineY = lerpFloat(placeholderBaselineY, floatingBaselineY, labelProgress)
  3203	        canvas.drawText(
  3204	            label,
  3205	            labelX,
  3206	            labelBaselineY,
  3207	            formLabelPaint
  3208	        )
  3209	
  3210	        val fieldText = mockSignInFieldDisplayText(field)
  3211	        val shouldDrawInput = fieldText.isNotEmpty() || focusedMockField == field
  3212	        if (shouldDrawInput) {
  3213	            val inputAlpha = (alpha.coerceIn(0f, 1f) * labelProgress * 255f).roundToInt()
  3214	            formInputPaint.textSize = MOCK_FORM_LABEL_TEXT_SIZE * stageScale
  3215	            formInputPaint.alpha = inputAlpha
  3216	            formInputPaint.textAlign = Paint.Align.LEFT
  3217	            formCaretPaint.alpha = inputAlpha
  3218	
  3219	            val inputX = tempRect.left + MOCK_FORM_LABEL_INSET_X * stageScale
  3220	            val inputBaselineY = tempRect.top + MOCK_FORM_INPUT_BASELINE_FROM_TOP * stageScale
  3221	            val maxInputWidth = fieldWidth * stageScale - MOCK_FORM_LABEL_INSET_X * stageScale * 2f
  3222	            val visibleText = trailingFittingText(fieldText, maxInputWidth, formInputPaint)
  3223	            canvas.drawText(visibleText, inputX, inputBaselineY, formInputPaint)
  3224	
  3225	            if (focusedMockField == field) {
  3226	                val caretX = inputX + formInputPaint.measureText(visibleText) + 2f * stageScale
  3227	                val inputMetrics = formInputPaint.fontMetrics
  3228	                canvas.drawLine(
  3229	                    caretX,
  3230	                    inputBaselineY + inputMetrics.ascent,
  3231	                    caretX,
  3232	                    inputBaselineY + inputMetrics.descent,
  3233	                    formCaretPaint
  3234	                )
  3235	            }
  3236	        }
  3237	    }

  3298	    private fun drawBackChevron(
  3299	        canvas: Canvas,
  3300	        centerX: Float,
  3301	        topY: Float,
  3302	        stageLeft: Float,
  3303	        stageTop: Float,
  3304	        stageScale: Float,
  3305	        alpha: Float
  3306	    ) {
  3307	        val chevronAlpha = alpha.coerceIn(0f, 1f) * if (pressedBackChevron) 0.68f else 0.92f
  3308	        val chevronScale = if (isPortraitIntroLayout()) {
  3309	            MOCK_PORTRAIT_STICKY_BOTTOM_CHEVRON_SCALE
  3310	        } else {

  3312	        }
  3313	        val center = stageLeft + centerX * stageScale
  3314	        val top = stageTop + topY * stageScale
  3315	        val bottom = top + MOCK_BACK_CHEVRON_HEIGHT * chevronScale * stageScale
  3316	        val halfWidth = MOCK_BACK_CHEVRON_WIDTH * chevronScale * stageScale / 2f
  3317

  3326	        canvas.drawLine(center - halfWidth, top, center, bottom, backChevronPaint)

  3350	    private fun decode(resId: Int): Bitmap {
  3351	        return BitmapFactory.decodeResource(resources, resId, bitmapOptions)
  3352	    }
  3353	
  3354	    private data class CreateAvatarLayers(
  3355	        val bubble: Bitmap,
  3356	        val characters: List<Bitmap>,
  3357	        val bottomStrokes: List<Bitmap>
  3358	    )
  3359	
  3360	    private fun createAvatarCarouselLayers(vararg avatars: Bitmap): CreateAvatarLayers {
  3361	        require(avatars.size == MOCK_CREATE_AVATAR_COUNT)
  3362	        val bitmapWidth = avatars.first().width
  3363	        val bitmapHeight = avatars.first().height
  3364	        require(avatars.all { it.width == bitmapWidth && it.height == bitmapHeight })
  3365	        return CreateAvatarLayers(
  3366	            bubble = createAvatarBubbleShellBitmap(avatars.first()),
  3367	            characters = avatars.map(::createAvatarCharacterBitmap),
  3368	            bottomStrokes = avatars.map(::createAvatarBottomStrokeBitmap)
  3369	        )
  3370	    }
  3371	
  3372	    private fun createAvatarBottomStrokeBitmap(source: Bitmap): Bitmap {
  3373	        val bitmapWidth = source.width
  3374	        val bitmapHeight = source.height
  3375	        val pixels = IntArray(bitmapWidth * bitmapHeight)
  3376	        source.getPixels(pixels, 0, bitmapWidth, 0, 0, bitmapWidth, bitmapHeight)
  3377	        val strokePixels = IntArray(pixels.size)
  3378	        val minimumBodyY = (bitmapHeight * MOCK_CREATE_AVATAR_BOTTOM_STROKE_START_RATIO).roundToInt()
  3379	
  3380	        for (x in 0 until bitmapWidth) {
  3381	            var bodyBottomY = -1
  3382	            for (y in minimumBodyY until bitmapHeight) {
  3383	                if (isAvatarCharacterFillPixel(pixels[y * bitmapWidth + x])) bodyBottomY = y
  3384	            }
  3385	            if (bodyBottomY < 0) continue
  3386	            val strokeBottomY = minOf(bitmapHeight - 1, bodyBottomY + MOCK_CREATE_AVATAR_BOTTOM_STROKE_DEPTH_PX)
  3387	            for (y in bodyBottomY + 1..strokeBottomY) {
  3388	                val index = y * bitmapWidth + x
  3389	                val sourceAlpha = Color.alpha(pixels[index])
  3390	                if (sourceAlpha <= 16) continue
  3391	                strokePixels[index] = Color.argb(
  3392	                    sourceAlpha,
  3393	                    MOCK_CREATE_AVATAR_STROKE_CHANNEL,
  3394	                    MOCK_CREATE_AVATAR_STROKE_CHANNEL,
  3395	                    MOCK_CREATE_AVATAR_STROKE_CHANNEL
  3396	                )
  3397	            }
  3398	        }
  3399	        val cornerFilledStrokePixels = strokePixels.copyOf()
  3400	        for (y in minimumBodyY until bitmapHeight) {
  3401	            val rowStart = y * bitmapWidth
  3402	            for (x in 0 until bitmapWidth) {
  3403	                if (strokePixels[rowStart + x] == Color.TRANSPARENT) continue
  3404	                val sideStart = maxOf(0, x - MOCK_CREATE_AVATAR_BOTTOM_STROKE_SIDE_EXTEND_PX)
  3405	                val sideEnd = minOf(bitmapWidth - 1, x + MOCK_CREATE_AVATAR_BOTTOM_STROKE_SIDE_EXTEND_PX)
  3406	                for (sideX in sideStart..sideEnd) {
  3407	                    val index = rowStart + sideX
  3408	                    val sourceAlpha = Color.alpha(pixels[index])
  3409	                    if (sourceAlpha <= 16 || isAvatarCharacterFillPixel(pixels[index])) continue
  3410	                    cornerFilledStrokePixels[index] = Color.argb(
  3411	                        sourceAlpha,
  3412	                        MOCK_CREATE_AVATAR_STROKE_CHANNEL,
  3413	                        MOCK_CREATE_AVATAR_STROKE_CHANNEL,
  3414	                        MOCK_CREATE_AVATAR_STROKE_CHANNEL
  3415	                    )
  3416	                }
  3417	            }
  3418	        }
  3419	        return bitmapFromPixels(cornerFilledStrokePixels, bitmapWidth, bitmapHeight)
  3420	    }
  3421	
  3422	    private fun isAvatarCharacterFillPixel(pixel: Int): Boolean {
  3423	        if (Color.alpha(pixel) <= 16) return false
  3424	        val channelMax = maxOf(Color.red(pixel), Color.green(pixel), Color.blue(pixel))
  3425	        val channelMin = minOf(Color.red(pixel), Color.green(pixel), Color.blue(pixel))
  3426	        return channelMax > 40 && channelMax - channelMin > 8
  3427	    }
  3428	
  3429	    private fun createAvatarBubbleShellBitmap(source: Bitmap): Bitmap {
  3430	        val bitmapWidth = source.width
  3431	        val bitmapHeight = source.height
  3432	        val pixels = IntArray(bitmapWidth * bitmapHeight)
  3433	        source.getPixels(pixels, 0, bitmapWidth, 0, 0, bitmapWidth, bitmapHeight)
  3434	
  3435	        val centerX = (bitmapWidth - 1f) / 2f
  3436	        val centerY = (bitmapHeight - 1f) / 2f
  3437	        val innerRadius = min(bitmapWidth, bitmapHeight) *
  3438	            (0.5f - MOCK_CREATE_AVATAR_BUBBLE_INNER_INSET_RATIO)
  3439	        val innerRadiusSquared = innerRadius * innerRadius
  3440	        val shellPixels = IntArray(pixels.size) { index ->
  3441	            val x = index % bitmapWidth
  3442	            val y = index / bitmapWidth
  3443	            val dx = x - centerX
  3444	            val dy = y - centerY
  3445	            if (dx * dx + dy * dy >= innerRadiusSquared) pixels[index] else Color.TRANSPARENT
  3446	        }
  3447	        return bitmapFromPixels(shellPixels, bitmapWidth, bitmapHeight)
  3448	    }
  3449	
  3450	    private fun createAvatarCharacterBitmap(source: Bitmap): Bitmap {
  3451	        val bitmapWidth = source.width
  3452	        val bitmapHeight = source.height
  3453	        val pixels = IntArray(bitmapWidth * bitmapHeight)
  3454	        source.getPixels(pixels, 0, bitmapWidth, 0, 0, bitmapWidth, bitmapHeight)
  3455	
  3456	        val centerX = (bitmapWidth - 1f) / 2f
  3457	        val centerY = (bitmapHeight - 1f) / 2f
  3458	        val innerRadius = min(bitmapWidth, bitmapHeight) * MOCK_CREATE_AVATAR_CLIP_RADIUS_RATIO
  3459	        val innerRadiusSquared = innerRadius * innerRadius
  3460	        val distance = IntArray(pixels.size) { -1 }
  3461	        val queue = IntArray(pixels.size)
  3462	        var queueStart = 0
  3463	        var queueEnd = 0
  3464	
  3465	        pixels.indices.forEach { index ->
  3466	            val x = index % bitmapWidth
  3467	            val y = index / bitmapWidth
  3468	            val dx = x - centerX
  3469	            val dy = y - centerY
  3470	            if (dx * dx + dy * dy < innerRadiusSquared && isAvatarCharacterColor(pixels[index])) {
  3471	                distance[index] = 0
  3472	                queue[queueEnd++] = index
  3473	            }
  3474	        }
  3475	
  3476	        while (queueStart < queueEnd) {
  3477	            val index = queue[queueStart++]
  3478	            val currentDistance = distance[index]
  3479	            if (currentDistance >= MOCK_CREATE_AVATAR_OUTLINE_RADIUS_PX) continue
  3480	            val x = index % bitmapWidth
  3481	            val y = index / bitmapWidth
  3482	            for (dy in -1..1) {
  3483	                val nextY = y + dy
  3484	                if (nextY !in 0 until bitmapHeight) continue
  3485	                for (dx in -1..1) {
  3486	                    if (dx == 0 && dy == 0) continue
  3487	                    val nextX = x + dx
  3488	                    if (nextX !in 0 until bitmapWidth) continue
  3489	                    val nextIndex = nextY * bitmapWidth + nextX
  3490	                    if (distance[nextIndex] >= 0 || !isAvatarOutlinePixel(pixels[nextIndex])) continue
  3491	                    val centerDx = nextX - centerX
  3492	                    val centerDy = nextY - centerY
  3493	                    if (centerDx * centerDx + centerDy * centerDy >= innerRadiusSquared) continue
  3494	                    distance[nextIndex] = currentDistance + 1
  3495	                    queue[queueEnd++] = nextIndex
  3496	                }
  3497	            }
  3498	        }
  3499	
  3500	        val characterPixels = IntArray(pixels.size) { index ->
  3501	            if (distance[index] >= 0) pixels[index] else Color.TRANSPARENT
  3502	        }
  3503	        return bitmapFromPixels(characterPixels, bitmapWidth, bitmapHeight)
  3504	    }
  3505	
  3506	    private fun isAvatarCharacterColor(pixel: Int): Boolean {
  3507	        if (Color.alpha(pixel) <= 16) return false
  3508	        val red = Color.red(pixel)
  3509	        val green = Color.green(pixel)
  3510	        val blue = Color.blue(pixel)
  3511	        val channelMax = maxOf(red, green, blue)
  3512	        val channelMin = minOf(red, green, blue)
  3513	        val isNeutralBubbleOrRing = channelMax - channelMin <= 4
  3514	        return (Color.alpha(pixel) >= 250 && !isNeutralBubbleOrRing) ||
  3515	            (channelMax > 40 && channelMax - channelMin > 8)
  3516	    }
  3517	
  3518	    private fun isAvatarOutlinePixel(pixel: Int): Boolean {
  3519	        return Color.alpha(pixel) > 24 && maxOf(Color.red(pixel), Color.green(pixel), Color.blue(pixel)) <= 80
  3520	    }
  3521	
  3522	    private fun bitmapFromPixels(pixels: IntArray, width: Int, height: Int): Bitmap {
  3523	        return Bitmap.createBitmap(width, height, Bitmap.Config.ARGB_8888).apply {
  3524	            setPixels(pixels, 0, width, 0, 0, width, height)
  3525	        }
  3526	    }
  3527	
  3528	    private fun standardSignInBackgroundForSize(): Bitmap? {
  3529	        return when {
  3530	            isSizeCloseTo(width, STANDARD_TABLET_LONG_EDGE) &&
  3531	                isSizeCloseTo(height, STANDARD_TABLET_SHORT_EDGE) -> standardLandscapeBackground
  3532	            isSizeCloseTo(width, STANDARD_TABLET_SHORT_EDGE) &&
  3533	                isSizeCloseTo(height, STANDARD_TABLET_LONG_EDGE) -> standardPortraitBackground
  3534	            else -> null
  3535	        }
  3536	    }
  3537	
  3538	    private fun isSizeCloseTo(actual: Int, expected: Int): Boolean {
  3539	        return abs(actual - expected) <= STANDARD_TABLET_SIZE_TOLERANCE
  3540	    }

  3542	    private fun isFinalFrameSettled(): Boolean {
  3543	        return IntroAnimationClock.processStartMillis > 0L &&
  3544	            bootProgressAt(IntroAnimationClock.processStartMillis) >= 1f
  3545	    }

  3547	    private fun settledAvatarHitProfile(x: Float, y: Float): CinerificProfile? {
  3548	        if (mockSignInStartMillis != null) return null
  3549	        if (!isFinalFrameSettled()) return null
  3550	
  3551	        val stage = currentStageMetrics() ?: return null
  3552	
  3553	        if (isPortraitIntroLayout()) {
  3554	            val avatarSize = portraitAvatarSize()
  3555	            val centerX = stage.left + FIGMA_FRAME_WIDTH * stage.scale / 2f
  3556	            val halfWidth = (avatarSize / 2f + PORTRAIT_PROFILE_SWIPE_HIT_HORIZONTAL_PADDING) * stage.scale
  3557	            val top = stage.top + portraitAvatarTop() * stage.scale
  3558	            val bottom = stage.top + (
  3559	                portraitProfileNameTop() + portraitProfileNameHeight() +
  3560	                    PORTRAIT_PROFILE_SWIPE_HIT_BOTTOM_PADDING
  3561	                ) * stage.scale
  3562	            return if (x in (centerX - halfWidth)..(centerX + halfWidth) && y in top..bottom) {
  3563	                FINAL_AVATAR_TARGETS[portraitProfileIndex].profile
  3564	            } else {
  3565	                null
  3566	            }
  3567	        }
  3568	
  3569	        val stackShiftY = signInStackShiftY()
  3570	        return FINAL_AVATAR_TARGETS.firstOrNull { target ->
  3571	            val bounds = target.bounds
  3572	            val centerX = stage.left + (bounds.x + bounds.w / 2f) * stage.scale
  3573	            val centerY = stage.top + (bounds.y + stackShiftY + bounds.h / 2f) * stage.scale
  3574	            val radius = min(bounds.w, bounds.h) * stage.scale / 2f
  3575	            val dx = x - centerX
  3576	            val dy = y - centerY
  3577	            dx * dx + dy * dy <= radius * radius
  3578	        }?.profile
  3579	    }

  3581	    private fun settledCreateAccountPromptHit(x: Float, y: Float): Boolean {
  3582	        if (!accountPromptCanOpen(MockAccountFlow.CreateAccount)) return false

  3586	            return portraitPromptHit(
  3587	                x,
  3588	                y,
  3589	                portraitPromptCreateCenterY()
  3590	            )
  3591	        }
  3592	        val centerX = stage.left + accountPromptCreateCenterX() * stage.scale
  3593	        val centerY = accountPromptStageTop(stage.scale) +
  3594	            (ACCOUNT_PROMPT_CENTER_Y + signInStackShiftY() + signInAccountPromptExtraShiftY()) * stage.scale
  3595	        val halfWidth = ACCOUNT_PROMPT_CREATE_HIT_WIDTH * stage.scale / 2f
  3596	        val halfHeight = ACCOUNT_PROMPT_CREATE_HIT_HEIGHT * stage.scale / 2f
  3597	        return x in (centerX - halfWidth)..(centerX + halfWidth) &&
  3598	            y in (centerY - halfHeight)..(centerY + halfHeight)
  3599	    }

  3804	    private fun updateMockCreateAvatarDrag(event: MotionEvent): Boolean {
  3805	        if (!activeMockCreateAvatarDrag) return false
  3806	
  3807	        mockCreateAvatarDragDeltaX = event.x - mockTouchDownX
  3808	        val dragDeltaY = event.y - mockTouchDownY
  3809	        if (
  3810	            !mockCreateAvatarDragMoved &&
  3811	            abs(mockCreateAvatarDragDeltaX) > touchSlop &&
  3812	            abs(mockCreateAvatarDragDeltaX) > abs(dragDeltaY)
  3813	        ) {
  3814	            mockCreateAvatarDragMoved = true
  3815	            lockedMockCreateAvatarDragDirection = if (mockCreateAvatarDragDeltaX < 0f) {
  3816	                MockAvatarCarouselDirection.Next
  3817	            } else {
  3818	                MockAvatarCarouselDirection.Previous
  3819	            }
  3820	            pressedMockCreateAvatarNav = null
  3821	        }
  3822	
  3823	        if (mockCreateAvatarDragMoved) {
  3824	            postInvalidateOnAnimation()
  3825	        }
  3826	        return true
  3827	    }

  3865	    private fun mockDropdownOptionsHit(pointerX: Float, pointerY: Float): MockDropdown? {
  3866	        if (
  3867	            mockSignInStartMillis == null ||
  3868	            activeMockFlow != MockAccountFlow.CreateAccount ||
  3869	            expandedMockDropdown == null
  3870	        ) {
  3871	            return null
  3872	        }
  3873	
  3874	        val dropdown = expandedMockDropdown ?: return null
  3875	        val baseStage = currentStageMetrics() ?: return null
  3876	        val progress = FastOutSlowInEasing.transform(mockSignInProgress())
  3877	        val yOffset = MOCK_FORM_ENTRY_Y * (1f - progress)
  3878	        val focusShiftY = mockFormFocusShiftY(FastOutSlowInEasing.transform(mockFormFocusProgress))
  3879	        if (!portraitAuthPointerInViewport(pointerY, baseStage)) return null
  3880	        val stage = mockCreateContentStageMetrics(baseStage, yOffset, focusShiftY)
  3881	        val dropdownX = mockDropdownX(dropdown)
  3882	        val yStart = mockDropdownOptionsY(dropdown, yOffset, focusShiftY)
  3883	        val left = stage.left + dropdownX * stage.scale

  3885	        val top = stage.top + yStart * stage.scale
  3886	        val bottom = top + mockDropdownVisibleOptionCount(dropdown) * mockDropdownOptionHeight(dropdown) * stage.scale
  3887	        return if (pointerX in left..right && pointerY in top..bottom) dropdown else null
  3888	    }
  3889	
  3890	    private fun updateMockDropdownScroll(event: MotionEvent): Boolean {
  3891	        val dropdown = activeMockDropdownScroll ?: return false
  3892	        if (expandedMockDropdown != dropdown || !mockDropdownCanScroll(dropdown)) return false
  3893	
  3894	        val baseStage = currentStageMetrics() ?: return true
  3895	        val progress = FastOutSlowInEasing.transform(mockSignInProgress())
  3896	        val yOffset = MOCK_FORM_ENTRY_Y * (1f - progress)
  3897	        val focusShiftY = mockFormFocusShiftY(FastOutSlowInEasing.transform(mockFormFocusProgress))
  3898	        val stage = mockCreateContentStageMetrics(baseStage, yOffset, focusShiftY)
  3899	        val optionHeightPx = (mockDropdownOptionHeight(dropdown) * stage.scale).coerceAtLeast(1f)
  3900	        val dragDeltaY = event.y - mockDropdownScrollStartY
  3901	        if (abs(dragDeltaY) > touchSlop) {
  3902	            mockDropdownScrollMoved = true
  3903	            pressedMockDropdownOption = null
  3904	        }
  3905	
  3906	        if (mockDropdownScrollMoved) {
  3907	            val rowDelta = ((mockDropdownScrollStartY - event.y) / optionHeightPx).roundToInt()
  3908	            setMockDropdownScrollOffset(dropdown, mockDropdownScrollStartOffset + rowDelta)
  3909	            postInvalidateOnAnimation()
  3910	        }
  3911	        return true
  3912	    }
  3913	
  3914	    private fun updatePortraitAuthScroll(event: MotionEvent): Boolean {
  3915	        if (
  3916	            !isPortraitIntroLayout() ||
  3917	            activeMockFlow !in listOf(
  3918	                MockAccountFlow.CreateAccount,
  3919	                MockAccountFlow.ForgotPassword
  3920	            ) ||
  3921	            activeMockDropdownScroll != null
  3922	        ) {
  3923	            return false
  3924	        }
  3925	
  3926	        val deltaX = event.x - mockTouchDownX
  3927	        val deltaY = event.y - portraitAuthScrollStartY
  3928	        if (!portraitAuthScrollMoved) {
  3929	            if (abs(deltaY) <= touchSlop || abs(deltaY) <= abs(deltaX)) return false
  3930	            if (mockDragReturnEligibleAtTouchDown && deltaY < 0f) return false
  3931	            mockDragReturnEligibleAtTouchDown = false
  3932	            portraitAuthScrollMoved = true
  3933	            pressedMockField = null
  3934	            pressedMockDropdown = null
  3935	            pressedMockDropdownOption = null
  3936	            pressedMockCreateAvatarNav = null
  3937	            activeMockCreateAvatarDrag = false
  3938	            mockCreateAvatarDragMoved = false
  3939	            expandedMockDropdown = null
  3940	            clearMockSignInFieldFocus()
  3941	        }
  3942	
  3943	        val stage = currentStageMetrics() ?: return true
  3944	        val progress = FastOutSlowInEasing.transform(mockSignInProgress())
  3945	        val yOffset = MOCK_FORM_ENTRY_Y * (1f - progress)
  3946	        val focusShiftY = mockFormFocusShiftY(FastOutSlowInEasing.transform(mockFormFocusProgress))
  3947	        val maxScrollY = when (activeMockFlow) {
  3948	            MockAccountFlow.ForgotPassword -> portraitForgotPasswordMaxScrollY(stage, yOffset, focusShiftY)
  3949	            else -> portraitCreateMaxScrollY(stage, yOffset, focusShiftY)
  3950	        }
  3951	        portraitAuthScrollY = (portraitAuthScrollStartOffset - deltaY).coerceIn(0f, maxScrollY)
  3952	        postInvalidateOnAnimation()
  3953	        return true
  3954	    }

  3956	    private fun mockBackChevronHit(x: Float, y: Float): Boolean {
  3957	        if (mockSignInStartMillis == null || isMockSignInClosing()) return false
  3958	
  3959	        val stage = currentStageMetrics() ?: return false
  3960	        if (activeMockFlow == null) return false
  3961	        return mockBackChevronContains(
  3962	            x,
  3963	            y,
  3964	            ACCOUNT_PROMPT_SIGN_IN_CENTER_X,
  3965	            mockBackChevronRowTopY(),
  3966	            stage
  3967	        )
  3968	    }
  3969	
  3970	    private fun shouldStartMockDragReturn(event: MotionEvent): Boolean {
  3971	        if (
  3972	            mockDragReturnInProgress ||
  3973	            isMockSignInClosing() ||
  3974	            !mockDragReturnEligibleAtTouchDown
  3975	        ) {
  3976	            return false
  3977	        }
  3978	
  3979	        val downwardScrollY = mockTouchDownY - event.y
  3980	        if (downwardScrollY <= mockBackDragThresholdPx()) return false
  3981	
  3982	        val dragX = event.x - mockTouchDownX
  3983	        return downwardScrollY > abs(dragX)
  3984	    }
  3985	
  3986	    private fun isMockAuthScreenAtBottom(): Boolean {
  3987	        if (activeMockFlow == null) return false
  3988	        if (!isPortraitIntroLayout()) return true
  3989	
  3990	        val stage = currentStageMetrics() ?: return false
  3991	        val progress = FastOutSlowInEasing.transform(mockSignInProgress())
  3992	        val yOffset = MOCK_FORM_ENTRY_Y * (1f - progress)
  3993	        val focusShiftY = mockFormFocusShiftY(
  3994	            FastOutSlowInEasing.transform(mockFormFocusProgress)
  3995	        )
  3996	        val maxScrollY = when (activeMockFlow) {
  3997	            MockAccountFlow.CreateAccount -> portraitCreateMaxScrollY(stage, yOffset, focusShiftY)
  3998	            MockAccountFlow.ForgotPassword -> portraitForgotPasswordMaxScrollY(
  3999	                stage,
  4000	                yOffset,
  4001	                focusShiftY
  4002	            )
  4003	            MockAccountFlow.SignIn -> 0f
  4004	            null -> return false
  4005	        }
  4006	        return portraitAuthScrollY >= maxScrollY - maxOf(1f, stage.scale)
  4007	    }

  4010	        val stageThreshold = currentStageMetrics()?.let { stage ->
  4011	            MOCK_BACK_DRAG_THRESHOLD * stage.scale
  4012	        } ?: MOCK_BACK_DRAG_THRESHOLD
  4013	        return maxOf(stageThreshold, touchSlop * 3f)
  4014	    }

  4351	    private fun portraitAuthViewportTopPx(stage: StageMetrics): Float {
  4352	        val title = when (activeMockFlow) {
  4353	            MockAccountFlow.SignIn -> ACCOUNT_PROMPT_SIGN_IN_TEXT
  4354	            MockAccountFlow.CreateAccount -> ACCOUNT_PROMPT_CREATE_TEXT
  4355	            MockAccountFlow.ForgotPassword -> ACCOUNT_PROMPT_FORGOT_TEXT
  4356	            null -> null
  4357	        }

  4359	            val titleTextSize = ACCOUNT_PROMPT_TEXT_SIZE * PORTRAIT_ACTION_SCALE * stage.scale
  4360	            val titleBaseline = mockFormTitleTargetBaseline(
  4361	                title,
  4362	                mockPrimaryFormY(),

  4365	                titleTextSize

  4367	            formTitlePaint.textSize = titleTextSize
  4368	            return titleBaseline + formTitlePaint.fontMetrics.descent +
  4369	                mockFormFieldGap() * stage.scale * portraitCreateContentScale()
  4370	        }

  4372	            (portraitLogoVisibleBottom() + MOCK_CREATE_PORTRAIT_VIEWPORT_GAP) * stage.scale
  4373	    }

  4375	    private fun portraitAuthViewportBottomPx(stage: StageMetrics): Float {
  4376	        val stickyBottomHeight =
  4377	            MOCK_CREATE_PORTRAIT_VIEWPORT_BOTTOM_RESERVE *
  4378	                MOCK_PORTRAIT_STICKY_BOTTOM_SCALE * stage.scale
  4379	        return height - systemBarInsetBottomPx -
  4380	            stickyBottomHeight * portraitStickyBottomMotion()
  4381	    }
  4382	
  4383	    private fun portraitAuthPointerInViewport(y: Float, stage: StageMetrics): Boolean {
  4384	        return !isPortraitIntroLayout() ||
  4385	            y in portraitAuthViewportTopPx(stage)..portraitAuthViewportBottomPx(stage)
  4386	    }
  4387	
  4388	    private fun mockCreateContentStageMetrics(
  4389	        stage: StageMetrics,
  4390	        yOffset: Float,
  4391	        focusShiftY: Float
  4392	    ): StageMetrics {
  4393	        if (!isPortraitIntroLayout()) return stage
  4394	
  4395	        val contentScale = stage.scale * portraitCreateContentScale()
  4396	        val centerX = stage.left + FIGMA_FRAME_WIDTH * stage.scale / 2f
  4397	        val firstFieldY = mockPrimaryFormY() + yOffset + focusShiftY
  4398	        val firstFieldTop = stage.top + firstFieldY * stage.scale - portraitAuthScrollY
  4399	        return StageMetrics(
  4400	            left = centerX - FIGMA_FRAME_WIDTH * contentScale / 2f,
  4401	            top = firstFieldTop - firstFieldY * contentScale,
  4402	            scale = contentScale
  4403	        )
  4404	    }
  4405	
  4406	    private fun portraitCreateMaxScrollY(
  4407	        stage: StageMetrics,
  4408	        yOffset: Float,
  4409	        focusShiftY: Float
  4410	    ): Float {
  4411	        if (!isPortraitIntroLayout()) return 0f
  4412	        val contentStage = mockCreateContentStageMetrics(stage, yOffset, focusShiftY)
  4413	        val avatarSectionY = mockCreateAvatarSectionY(yOffset, focusShiftY)
  4414	        val avatarBottomY = mockCreateAvatarBounds(
  4415	            mockCreateAvatarColumnX(),
  4416	            avatarSectionY
  4417	        ).let { it.y + it.h }
  4418	        val finalControlBottomY = mockDropdownY(
  4419	            MockDropdown.Year,
  4420	            yOffset,
  4421	            focusShiftY
  4422	        ) + MOCK_FORM_FIELD_HEIGHT
  4423	        val contentBottomY = maxOf(avatarBottomY, finalControlBottomY)
  4424	        val contentBottom = contentStage.top + contentBottomY * contentStage.scale +
  4425	            MOCK_CREATE_PORTRAIT_CONTENT_BOTTOM_GAP * stage.scale + portraitAuthScrollY
  4426	        return (contentBottom - portraitAuthViewportBottomPx(stage)).coerceAtLeast(0f)
  4427	    }
  4428	
  4429	    private fun portraitForgotPasswordMaxScrollY(
  4430	        stage: StageMetrics,
  4431	        yOffset: Float,
  4432	        focusShiftY: Float
  4433	    ): Float {
  4434	        if (!isPortraitIntroLayout()) return 0f
  4435	        val contentStage = mockForgotPasswordContentStageMetrics(stage, yOffset, focusShiftY)
  4436	        val helpBottomY = mockForgotPasswordHelpBottomY(yOffset, focusShiftY)
  4437	        val contentBottom = contentStage.top + helpBottomY * contentStage.scale +
  4438	            MOCK_CREATE_PORTRAIT_CONTENT_BOTTOM_GAP * stage.scale + portraitAuthScrollY
  4439	        return (contentBottom - portraitAuthViewportBottomPx(stage)).coerceAtLeast(0f)
  4440	    }

  4516	    private fun mockForgotPasswordBodyBottomY(formTopY: Float): Float {
  4517	        val paragraphs = mockForgotPasswordBodyText().split(Regex("\\n\\s*\\n"))
  4518	        val copyWidth = mockForgotPasswordCopyWidth()
  4519	        var bottomY = mockParagraphBottomY(
  4520	            paragraphs.firstOrNull().orEmpty(),
  4521	            formTopY + mockForgotPasswordBodyTopGap(),
  4522	            copyWidth,
  4523	            MOCK_FORGOT_PASSWORD_BODY_LEAD_TEXT_SIZE,
  4524	            MOCK_FORGOT_PASSWORD_BODY_LEAD_LINE_HEIGHT,
  4525	            forceLineBreaks = true
  4526	        )
  4527	        paragraphs.getOrNull(1)?.let { middleText ->
  4528	            bottomY = mockParagraphBottomY(
  4529	                middleText,
  4530	                bottomY + MOCK_FORGOT_PASSWORD_BODY_LINE_HEIGHT,
  4531	                copyWidth,
  4532	                MOCK_FORGOT_PASSWORD_BODY_MIDDLE_TEXT_SIZE,
  4533	                MOCK_FORGOT_PASSWORD_BODY_MIDDLE_LINE_HEIGHT
  4534	            )
  4535	        }
  4536	        val bottomText = paragraphs.drop(2).joinToString("\n\n")
  4537	        if (bottomText.isNotEmpty()) {
  4538	            bottomY = mockParagraphBottomY(
  4539	                bottomText,
  4540	                bottomY + MOCK_FORGOT_PASSWORD_BODY_LINE_HEIGHT,
  4541	                copyWidth,
  4542	                MOCK_FORGOT_PASSWORD_BODY_MIDDLE_TEXT_SIZE,
  4543	                MOCK_FORGOT_PASSWORD_BODY_MIDDLE_LINE_HEIGHT
  4544	            )
  4545	        }
  4546	        return bottomY
  4547	    }
  4548	
  4549	    private fun mockForgotPasswordBodyTopGap(): Float {
  4550	        return if (isPortraitIntroLayout()) {
  4551	            MOCK_FORGOT_PASSWORD_PORTRAIT_BODY_TOP_GAP
  4552	        } else {
  4553	            MOCK_FORGOT_PASSWORD_LANDSCAPE_BODY_TOP_GAP
  4554	        }
  4555	    }

  4557	    private fun mockForgotPasswordHelpBottomY(yOffset: Float, focusShiftY: Float): Float {
  4558	        val helpTopY = mockForgotPasswordButtonY(yOffset, focusShiftY) +
  4559	            MOCK_FORGOT_PASSWORD_BUTTON_HEIGHT + MOCK_FORGOT_PASSWORD_HELP_TOP_GAP
  4560	        return mockParagraphBottomY(
  4561	            mockForgotPasswordHelpText(),
  4562	            helpTopY,
  4563	            mockForgotPasswordFormWidth(),
  4564	            MOCK_FORGOT_PASSWORD_HELP_TEXT_SIZE,
  4565	            MOCK_FORGOT_PASSWORD_HELP_LINE_HEIGHT
  4566	        )
  4567	    }
  4568	
  4569	    private fun mockParagraphBottomY(
  4570	        text: String,
  4571	        topY: Float,
  4572	        maxWidth: Float,
  4573	        textSize: Float,
  4574	        lineHeight: Float,
  4575	        forceLineBreaks: Boolean = false
  4576	    ): Float {
  4577	        formInputPaint.textSize = textSize
  4578	        val lineCount = if (forceLineBreaks) {
  4579	            text.split('\n').sumOf { line ->
  4580	                wrappedTextLines(line, maxWidth, formInputPaint).size
  4581	            }
  4582	        } else {
  4583	            wrappedTextLines(text, maxWidth, formInputPaint).size
  4584	        }
  4585	        return topY + lineCount * lineHeight
  4586	    }

  4588	    private fun mockForgotRecoverySelectorLayout(
  4589	        left: Float,
  4590	        width: Float,
  4591	        labelWidth: Float,
  4592	        boxWidth: Float
  4593	    ): ForgotRecoverySelectorLayout {
  4594	        val gap = ((width - labelWidth - boxWidth * 2f) / 4f).coerceAtLeast(0f)
  4595	        val labelX = left + gap
  4596	        val usernameBoxX = labelX + labelWidth + gap
  4597	        val passwordBoxX = usernameBoxX + boxWidth + gap
  4598	        return ForgotRecoverySelectorLayout(
  4599	            labelX = labelX,
  4600	            usernameBoxX = usernameBoxX,
  4601	            passwordBoxX = passwordBoxX,
  4602	            boxWidth = boxWidth
  4603	        )
  4604	    }
  4605	
  4606	    private fun mockForgotRecoverySelectorBoxWidth(
  4607	        usernameTextWidth: Float,
  4608	        passwordTextWidth: Float,
  4609	        baseBoxWidth: Float = MOCK_FORGOT_PASSWORD_SELECTOR_BOX_WIDTH
  4610	    ): Float {
  4611	        val widestTextWidth = maxOf(usernameTextWidth, passwordTextWidth)
  4612	        val currentHorizontalPadding = ((baseBoxWidth - widestTextWidth) / 2f).coerceAtLeast(0f)
  4613	        return widestTextWidth + currentHorizontalPadding * 4f
  4614	    }

  4624	    private fun mockActiveFieldX(field: MockSignInField): Float {
  4625	        if (activeMockFlow == MockAccountFlow.ForgotPassword) return mockForgotPasswordFormX()
  4626	        if (activeMockFlow != MockAccountFlow.CreateAccount) return mockFormX()
  4627	
  4628	        val firstColumnX = if (isPortraitIntroLayout()) mockCreatePortraitColumnX() else mockCreateFormX()
  4629	        val middleColumnX = if (isPortraitIntroLayout()) {
  4630	            mockCreatePortraitColumnX()
  4631	        } else {
  4632	            mockCreateFormX() + MOCK_FORM_WIDTH + MOCK_CREATE_FORM_COLUMN_GAP
  4633	        }
  4634	        return when (field) {
  4635	            MockSignInField.Username,
  4636	            MockSignInField.Password,
  4637	            MockSignInField.ConfirmPassword -> firstColumnX
  4638	            MockSignInField.Email -> middleColumnX
  4639	            MockSignInField.Month -> middleColumnX
  4640	            MockSignInField.Day -> middleColumnX + MOCK_DATE_MONTH_WIDTH + MOCK_DATE_FIELD_GAP
  4641	            MockSignInField.Year -> middleColumnX + MOCK_DATE_MONTH_WIDTH + MOCK_DATE_DAY_WIDTH + MOCK_DATE_FIELD_GAP * 2f
  4642	        }
  4643	    }
  4644	
  4645	    private fun mockActiveFieldY(field: MockSignInField, yOffset: Float, focusShiftY: Float): Float {
  4646	        if (activeMockFlow == MockAccountFlow.ForgotPassword) {
  4647	            return mockForgotPasswordFieldY(yOffset, focusShiftY)
  4648	        }
  4649	
  4650	        val usesSecondSection = activeMockFlow == MockAccountFlow.CreateAccount &&
  4651	            isPortraitIntroLayout() &&
  4652	            field in listOf(
  4653	                MockSignInField.Email,
  4654	                MockSignInField.Month,
  4655	                MockSignInField.Day,
  4656	                MockSignInField.Year
  4657	            )
  4658	        val sectionTop = if (usesSecondSection) {
  4659	            mockCreateSecondSectionY(yOffset, focusShiftY)
  4660	        } else {
  4661	            mockPrimaryFormY() + yOffset + focusShiftY
  4662	        }
  4663	        val fieldOffsetY = if (activeMockFlow == MockAccountFlow.CreateAccount) {
  4664	            when (field) {
  4665	                MockSignInField.Username -> if (isPortraitIntroLayout()) {
  4666	                    mockCreatePortraitUsernameOffsetY()
  4667	                } else {
  4668	                    0f
  4669	                }
  4670	                MockSignInField.Email -> 0f
  4671	                MockSignInField.Password -> if (isPortraitIntroLayout()) {
  4672	                    mockCreatePortraitPasswordOffsetY()
  4673	                } else {
  4674	                    MOCK_FORM_FIELD_HEIGHT + mockFormFieldGap() * 2f
  4675	                }
  4676	                MockSignInField.ConfirmPassword -> if (isPortraitIntroLayout()) {
  4677	                    mockCreatePortraitPasswordOffsetY() +
  4678	                        MOCK_FORM_FIELD_HEIGHT + mockFormFieldGap()
  4679	                } else {
  4680	                    MOCK_FORM_FIELD_HEIGHT * 2f + mockFormFieldGap() * 3f
  4681	                }
  4682	                MockSignInField.Month,
  4683	                MockSignInField.Day,
  4684	                MockSignInField.Year -> mockCreateDateRowOffsetY()
  4685	            }
  4686	        } else {
  4687	            activeMockFields().indexOf(field).coerceAtLeast(0) *
  4688	                (MOCK_FORM_FIELD_HEIGHT + mockFormFieldGap())
  4689	        }
  4690	        return sectionTop + fieldOffsetY
  4691	    }

  4712	    private fun mockBackChevronRowTopY(): Float {
  4713	        if (usesPortraitStackedAuthLayout()) {
  4714	            val stickyBottomHeight =
  4715	                MOCK_CREATE_PORTRAIT_VIEWPORT_BOTTOM_RESERVE * MOCK_PORTRAIT_STICKY_BOTTOM_SCALE
  4716	            val chevronHeight =
  4717	                MOCK_BACK_CHEVRON_HEIGHT * MOCK_PORTRAIT_STICKY_BOTTOM_CHEVRON_SCALE
  4718	            val settledTop = portraitSafeBottom() - stickyBottomHeight +
  4719	                (stickyBottomHeight - chevronHeight) / 2f
  4720	            return settledTop + stickyBottomHeight * (1f - portraitStickyBottomMotion())
  4721	        }
  4722	        return ACCOUNT_PROMPT_CENTER_Y + signInStackShiftY() + signInAccountPromptExtraShiftY() +
  4723	            MOCK_BACK_CHEVRON_ROW_TOP_GAP
  4724	    }

  4727	        if (!usesPortraitStackedAuthLayout()) return 0f
  4728	        return FastOutSlowInEasing.transform(mockSignInProgress()).coerceIn(0f, 1f)
  4729	    }

  4847	    private fun mockDropdownVisibleOptionCount(dropdown: MockDropdown): Int {
  4848	        val optionCount = mockDropdownOptions(dropdown).size
  4849	        val viewportCount = if (mockDropdownUsesDateViewport(dropdown)) {
  4850	            MOCK_DATE_DROPDOWN_VISIBLE_OPTION_COUNT
  4851	        } else {
  4852	            optionCount
  4853	        }

  4855	    }

  4858	        return mockDropdownOptions(dropdown).size > mockDropdownVisibleOptionCount(dropdown)
  4859	    }

  4861	    private fun mockDropdownScrollOffset(dropdown: MockDropdown): Int {
  4862	        return (mockDropdownScrollOffsets[dropdown] ?: 0).coerceIn(0, mockDropdownMaxScrollOffset(dropdown))
  4863	    }
  4864	
  4865	    private fun mockDropdownMaxScrollOffset(dropdown: MockDropdown): Int {
  4866	        return (mockDropdownOptions(dropdown).size - mockDropdownVisibleOptionCount(dropdown)).coerceAtLeast(0)
  4867	    }
  4868	
  4869	    private fun setMockDropdownScrollOffset(dropdown: MockDropdown, offset: Int) {
  4870	        mockDropdownScrollOffsets[dropdown] = offset.coerceIn(0, mockDropdownMaxScrollOffset(dropdown))
  4871	    }
  4872	
  4873	    private fun mockDropdownVisibleOptions(dropdown: MockDropdown): List<String> {
  4874	        val options = mockDropdownOptions(dropdown)
  4875	        val offset = mockDropdownScrollOffset(dropdown)
  4876	        return options.drop(offset).take(mockDropdownVisibleOptionCount(dropdown))
  4877	    }
  4878	
  4879	    private fun prepareMockDropdownScroll(dropdown: MockDropdown) {
  4880	        if (!mockDropdownCanScroll(dropdown)) {
  4881	            setMockDropdownScrollOffset(dropdown, 0)
  4882	            return
  4883	        }
  4884	
  4885	        val selectedIndex = mockDropdownOptions(dropdown).indexOf(mockDropdownSelectedValue(dropdown))
  4886	        setMockDropdownScrollOffset(dropdown, if (selectedIndex >= 0) selectedIndex else 0)
  4887	    }
  4888	
  4889	    private fun mockDropdownDisplayText(dropdown: MockDropdown): String {
  4890	        return when (dropdown) {
  4891	            MockDropdown.SubscriptionTier -> normalizedMembershipTierSelection(subscriptionTierText)
  4892	            else -> mockDropdownSelectedValue(dropdown)
  4893	        }
  4894	    }

  4899	            MockDropdown.Month -> monthText
  4900	            MockDropdown.Day -> dayText
  4901	            MockDropdown.Year -> yearText
  4902	        }
  4903	    }

  5158	    private fun mockCreateAvatarDragProgress(): Float {
  5159	        val baseStage = currentStageMetrics() ?: return 0f
  5160	        val progress = FastOutSlowInEasing.transform(mockSignInProgress())
  5161	        val yOffset = MOCK_FORM_ENTRY_Y * (1f - progress)
  5162	        val focusShiftY = mockFormFocusShiftY(FastOutSlowInEasing.transform(mockFormFocusProgress))
  5163	        val stage = mockCreateContentStageMetrics(baseStage, yOffset, focusShiftY)
  5164	        val scaledAvatarSize = MOCK_CREATE_AVATAR_SIZE * MOCK_CREATE_AVATAR_STACK_SCALE
  5165	        return mockCreateAvatarDragProgress(mockCreateAvatarSlideDistance(scaledAvatarSize), stage.scale)
  5166	    }

  5169	        val slideDistancePx = (slideDistance * stageScale).coerceAtLeast(1f)
  5170	        return (abs(mockCreateAvatarDragDeltaX) / slideDistancePx).coerceIn(0f, 1f)
  5171	    }

  5210	        val elapsed = (now - lastFormLabelAnimationMillis).coerceIn(0L, 48L)
  5211	        lastFormLabelAnimationMillis = now

  5213	        mockFieldLabelFloatProgress.keys.forEach { field ->
  5214	            mockFieldLabelFloatProgress[field] = moveToward(
  5215	                mockFieldLabelFloatProgress[field] ?: 0f,
  5216	                mockSignInFieldLabelTarget(field),
  5217	                step
  5218	            )
  5219	        }
  5220	    }

  5250

  5253	            mockInputDimActiveDropdown = null
  5254	        }

  5314	    private fun mockLandscapeInputLiftTarget(): Float {
  5315	        if (width <= height) return 0f
  5316	        if (mockSignInStartMillis == null || isMockSignInClosing()) return 0f
  5317	        return if (
  5318	            focusedMockField != null ||
  5319	            expandedMockDropdown != null ||
  5320	            isSoftKeyboardVisible()
  5321	        ) {
  5322	            1f
  5323	        } else {
  5324	            0f
  5325	        }
  5326	    }
  5327	
  5328	    private fun mockLandscapeInputStageLiftY(stageScale: Float): Float {
  5329	        if (width <= height || mockSignInStartMillis == null) return 0f
  5330	        val liftMotion = FastOutSlowInEasing.transform(mockLandscapeInputLiftProgress)
  5331	        return MOCK_FORM_LANDSCAPE_FOCUS_SHIFT_Y * stageScale * liftMotion
  5332	    }
  5333	
  5334	    private fun isSoftKeyboardVisible(): Boolean {
  5335	        val rootInsets = ViewCompat.getRootWindowInsets(this)
  5336	        if (rootInsets != null) {
  5337	            val imeInsets = rootInsets.getInsets(WindowInsetsCompat.Type.ime())
  5338	            if (rootInsets.isVisible(WindowInsetsCompat.Type.ime()) || imeInsets.bottom > 0) return true
  5339	        }
  5340	
  5341	        val rootHeight = rootView?.height ?: 0
  5342	        if (rootHeight <= 0) return false
  5343	        getWindowVisibleDisplayFrame(tempWindowRect)
  5344	        val obscuredHeight = rootHeight - tempWindowRect.height()
  5345	        return obscuredHeight > rootHeight * 0.18f
  5346	    }
  5347	
  5348	    private fun mockInputDimTarget(): Float {
  5349	        if (mockSignInStartMillis == null || isMockSignInClosing()) return 0f
  5350	        if (expandedMockDropdown != null) return 1f
  5351	        val awaitingField = mockInputDimAwaitingField ?: return 0f
  5352	        return if (focusedMockField == awaitingField) 1f else 0f
  5353	    }
  5354	
  5355	    private fun mockFormFocusTarget(): Float {
  5356	        if (mockSignInStartMillis == null || isMockSignInClosing()) return 0f
  5357	        if (width > height) {
  5358	            return if (focusedMockField != null || expandedMockDropdown != null) 1f else 0f
  5359	        }

  5362	            focusedMockField != null ||
  5363	            activeMockFields().any { mockSignInFieldText(it).isNotEmpty() }
  5364	        ) {
  5365	            1f
  5366	        } else {
  5367	            0f
  5368	        }
  5369	    }

  5440	        val usernameWasEmpty = usernameText.isEmpty()
  5441	        when (field) {
  5442	            MockSignInField.Username -> usernameText = limitedText
  5443	            MockSignInField.Password -> passwordText = limitedText
  5444	            MockSignInField.ConfirmPassword -> confirmPasswordText = limitedText
  5445	            MockSignInField.Email -> emailText = limitedText
  5446	            MockSignInField.Month -> monthText = limitedText
  5447	            MockSignInField.Day -> dayText = limitedText
  5448	            MockSignInField.Year -> yearText = limitedText
  5449	        }
  5450	        if (field == MockSignInField.Username && usernameWasEmpty != usernameText.isEmpty()) {
  5451	            restartMockCreateUsernameRevealAnimation()
  5452	        }
  5453	        if (activeMockFlow == MockAccountFlow.ForgotPassword && field == MockSignInField.Email) {
  5454	            resetMockForgotPasswordSubmission()

  5457	        if (mockInputDimAwaitingField == field) {
  5458	            mockInputDimAwaitingField = null
  5459	        }
  5460	        restartMockFormLabelAnimation()
  5461	        restartMockFormFocusAnimation()
  5462	        restartMockInputDimAnimation()
  5463	        postInvalidateOnAnimation()

  5466	    private fun appendMockSignInInput(rawInput: String): Boolean {
  5467	        val field = focusedMockField ?: return false
  5468	        val input = sanitizeMockSignInInput(rawInput)
  5469	        if (input.isEmpty()) return true
  5470	        val currentText = mockSignInFieldText(field)
  5471	        val baseText = currentText.dropLast(activeComposingText.length.coerceAtMost(currentText.length))
  5472	        val availableChars = (MOCK_FORM_INPUT_MAX_CHARS - baseText.length).coerceAtLeast(0)
  5473	        val insertedText = input.take(availableChars)
  5474	        setMockSignInFieldText(field, baseText + insertedText)
  5475	        activeComposingText = ""
  5476	        return true
  5477	    }
  5478	
  5479	    private fun replaceMockSignInComposingText(rawInput: String): Boolean {
  5480	        val field = focusedMockField ?: return false
  5481	        val input = sanitizeMockSignInInput(rawInput)
  5482	        val currentText = mockSignInFieldText(field)
  5483	        val baseText = currentText.dropLast(activeComposingText.length.coerceAtMost(currentText.length))
  5484	        val availableChars = (MOCK_FORM_INPUT_MAX_CHARS - baseText.length).coerceAtLeast(0)
  5485	        val composingText = input.take(availableChars)
  5486	        setMockSignInFieldText(field, baseText + composingText)
  5487	        activeComposingText = composingText
  5488	        return true
  5489	    }
  5490	
  5491	    private fun finishMockSignInComposingText(): Boolean {
  5492	        activeComposingText = ""
  5493	        return true
  5494	    }
  5495	
  5496	    private fun deleteMockSignInInput(beforeLength: Int): Boolean {
  5497	        val field = focusedMockField ?: return false
  5498	        val currentText = mockSignInFieldText(field)
  5499	        if (currentText.isEmpty()) {
  5500	            activeComposingText = ""
  5501	            return true
  5502	        }
  5503	        val deleteCount = beforeLength.coerceAtLeast(1).coerceAtMost(currentText.length)
  5504	        activeComposingText = ""
  5505	        setMockSignInFieldText(field, currentText.dropLast(deleteCount))
  5506	        return true
  5507	    }
  5508	
  5509	    private fun handleMockSignInEditorAction(actionCode: Int): Boolean {
  5510	        activeComposingText = ""
  5511	        val fields = activeMockFields()
  5512	        val fieldIndex = fields.indexOf(focusedMockField)
  5513	        if (fieldIndex >= 0 && fieldIndex < fields.lastIndex && actionCode != EditorInfo.IME_ACTION_DONE) {
  5514	            focusMockSignInField(fields[fieldIndex + 1])
  5515	        } else if (
  5516	            activeMockFlow == MockAccountFlow.ForgotPassword &&
  5517	            focusedMockField == MockSignInField.Email &&
  5518	            mockForgotPasswordCanSubmit()
  5519	        ) {
  5520	            startMockForgotPasswordSubmission()
  5521	        } else {
  5522	            val inputMethodManager = context.getSystemService(Context.INPUT_METHOD_SERVICE) as? InputMethodManager
  5523	            inputMethodManager?.hideSoftInputFromWindow(windowToken, 0)
  5524	            clearMockSignInFieldFocus()
  5525	        }
  5526	        return true
  5527	    }
  5528	
  5529	    private fun handleMockSignInKeyEvent(keyCode: Int, event: KeyEvent): Boolean {
  5530	        if (event.action != KeyEvent.ACTION_DOWN) return true
  5531	        return when (keyCode) {
  5532	            KeyEvent.KEYCODE_DEL -> deleteMockSignInInput(1)
  5533	            KeyEvent.KEYCODE_ENTER -> handleMockSignInEditorAction(EditorInfo.IME_ACTION_DONE)
  5534	            else -> {
  5535	                val typedCharacter = event.unicodeChar.takeIf { it != 0 }?.toChar()?.toString()
  5536	                typedCharacter?.let(::appendMockSignInInput) ?: false
  5537	            }
  5538	        }
  5539	    }
  5540	
  5541	    private fun sanitizeMockSignInInput(input: String): String {
  5542	        return buildString {
  5543	            input.forEach { character ->
  5544	                if (character != '\n' && character != '\r' && !character.isISOControl()) {
  5545	                    append(character)
  5546	                }
  5547	            }
  5548	        }
  5549	    }
  5550	
  5551	    private fun trailingFittingText(text: String, maxWidth: Float, paint: Paint): String {
  5552	        if (paint.measureText(text) <= maxWidth) return text
  5553	
  5554	        var startIndex = 0
  5555	        while (startIndex < text.length && paint.measureText(text.substring(startIndex)) > maxWidth) {
  5556	            startIndex += 1
  5557	        }
  5558	        return text.substring(startIndex)
  5559	    }
  5560	
  5561	    private fun wrappedTextLines(text: String, maxWidth: Float, paint: Paint): List<String> {
  5562	        val lines = mutableListOf<String>()
  5563	        text.split(Regex("\\n\\s*\\n")).forEachIndexed { paragraphIndex, paragraph ->
  5564	            if (paragraphIndex > 0) {
  5565	                lines += ""
  5566	            }
  5567	
  5568	            var currentLine = ""
  5569	            paragraph.replace('\n', ' ')
  5570	                .split(Regex("\\s+"))
  5571	                .filter { it.isNotEmpty() }
  5572	                .forEach { word ->
  5573	                    val candidateLine = if (currentLine.isEmpty()) word else "$currentLine $word"
  5574	                    if (currentLine.isEmpty() || paint.measureText(candidateLine) <= maxWidth) {
  5575	                        currentLine = candidateLine
  5576	                    } else {
  5577	                        lines += currentLine
  5578	                        currentLine = word
  5579	                    }
  5580	                }
  5581	            if (currentLine.isNotEmpty()) {
  5582	                lines += currentLine
  5583	            }
  5584	        }
  5585	        return lines
  5586	    }
  5587	
  5588	    private fun moveToward(current: Float, target: Float, amount: Float): Float {
  5589	        return when {
  5590	            current < target -> (current + amount).coerceAtMost(target)
  5591	            current > target -> (current - amount).coerceAtLeast(target)
  5592	            else -> current
  5593	        }
  5594	    }

  5596	    private fun currentStageMetrics(): StageMetrics? {
  5597	        if (width <= 0 || height <= 0) return null
  5598	
  5599	        val standardScale = min(width / FIGMA_FRAME_WIDTH, height / FIGMA_FRAME_HEIGHT)
  5600	        val portraitScale = portraitDesignScale()
  5601	        val portraitAmount = portraitIntroAmount()
  5602	        val stageScale = lerpFloat(standardScale, portraitScale, portraitAmount)
  5603	        val standardTop = (height - FIGMA_FRAME_HEIGHT * standardScale) / 2f
  5604	        return StageMetrics(
  5605	            left = (width - FIGMA_FRAME_WIDTH * stageScale) / 2f,
  5606	            top = lerpFloat(standardTop, 0f, portraitAmount) +
  5607	                mockAuthOpenStageLiftY(stageScale) +
  5608	                mockLandscapeInputStageLiftY(stageScale),
  5609	            scale = stageScale
  5610	        )
  5611	    }

  5628	        return min(widthScale, height / referenceDesignHeight)
  5629	    }

  5830	    private fun switchMockAccountFlow(flow: MockAccountFlow) {
  5831	        if (
  5832	            mockSignInStartMillis == null ||
  5833	            activeMockFlow == flow ||
  5834	            isMockSignInClosing() ||
  5835	            isMockFlowSwitchAnimating()
  5836	        ) {
  5837	            return
  5838	        }
  5839	
  5840	        val previousFlow = activeMockFlow ?: return
  5841	        clearMockSignInFieldFocus()
  5842	        activeComposingText = ""
  5843	        expandedMockDropdown = null
  5844	        activeMockDropdownScroll = null
  5845	        mockDropdownScrollMoved = false
  5846	        portraitAuthScrollY = 0f
  5847	        portraitAuthScrollMoved = false
  5848	        pressedMockField = null
  5849	        pressedRememberMe = false
  5850	        pressedBackChevron = false
  5851	        pressedMockCreateAvatarNav = null
  5852	        activeMockCreateAvatarDrag = false
  5853	        mockCreateAvatarDragMoved = false
  5854	        mockCreateAvatarDragDeltaX = 0f
  5855	        lockedMockCreateAvatarDragDirection = null
  5856	        pressedMockDropdown = null
  5857	        pressedMockDropdownOption = null
  5858	        pressedForgotRecoveryTarget = null
  5859	        pressedForgotPasswordSubmit = false
  5860	        resetMockCreateAvatarCarousel()
  5861	        if (flow == MockAccountFlow.ForgotPassword) {
  5862	            forgotRecoveryTarget = MockForgotRecoveryTarget.Password
  5863	            emailText = ""
  5864	            resetMockForgotPasswordSubmission()
  5865	        }
  5866	        outgoingMockFlow = previousFlow
  5867	        activeMockFlow = flow
  5868	        mockFlowSwitchStartMillis = SystemClock.uptimeMillis()
  5869	        restartMockFormLabelAnimation()
  5870	        restartMockFormFocusAnimation()
  5871	        restartMockLandscapeInputLiftAnimation()
  5872	        restartMockInputDimAnimation()
  5873	        notifyIntroSnapshotChanged()
  5874	        postInvalidateOnAnimation()
  5875	    }

  5966	    private fun mockSignInProgress(): Float {
  5967	        val startMillis = mockSignInStartMillis ?: return 0f
  5968	        val elapsed = SystemClock.uptimeMillis() - startMillis
  5969	        val progress = (elapsed / mockSignInTransitionDurationMs().toFloat()).coerceIn(0f, 1f)
  5970	        return lerpFloat(
  5971	            mockSignInTransitionStartProgress,
  5972	            mockSignInTransitionTargetProgress,
  5973	            progress
  5974	        )
  5975	    }
  5976	
  5977	    private fun mockFlowSwitchProgress(): Float {
  5978	        val startMillis = mockFlowSwitchStartMillis ?: return 1f
  5979	        return ((SystemClock.uptimeMillis() - startMillis) / MOCK_SIGN_IN_TRANSITION_MS.toFloat())
  5980	            .coerceIn(0f, 1f)
  5981	    }
  5982	
  5983	    private fun isMockFlowSwitchAnimating(): Boolean {
  5984	        return mockFlowSwitchStartMillis != null && mockFlowSwitchProgress() < 1f
  5985	    }
  5986	
  5987	    private fun finishMockFlowSwitchIfNeeded() {
  5988	        if (mockFlowSwitchStartMillis == null || mockFlowSwitchProgress() < 1f) return
  5989	        outgoingMockFlow = null
  5990	        mockFlowSwitchStartMillis = null
  5991	    }

  5993	    private fun isMockSignInAnimating(): Boolean {
  5994	        val startMillis = mockSignInStartMillis ?: return false
  5995	        return SystemClock.uptimeMillis() - startMillis < mockSignInTransitionDurationMs()
  5996	    }
  5997	
  5998	    private fun isMockSignInClosing(): Boolean {
  5999	        return mockSignInStartMillis != null && mockSignInTransitionTargetProgress == 0f
  6000	    }
  6001	
  6002	    private fun mockSignInTransitionDurationMs(): Long {
  6003	        val distance = abs(mockSignInTransitionTargetProgress - mockSignInTransitionStartProgress)
  6004	        return (MOCK_SIGN_IN_TRANSITION_MS * distance)
  6005	            .roundToInt()
  6006	            .coerceAtLeast(1)
  6007	            .toLong()
  6008	    }
  6009	
  6010	    private fun finishMockSignInTransitionIfNeeded() {
  6011	        val startMillis = mockSignInStartMillis ?: return
  6012	        if (SystemClock.uptimeMillis() - startMillis < mockSignInTransitionDurationMs()) return
  6013	
  6014	        if (mockSignInTransitionTargetProgress == 0f) {
  6015	            mockSignInStartMillis = null
  6016	            activeMockFlow = null
  6017	            mockSignInTransitionStartProgress = 0f
  6018	            mockSignInTransitionTargetProgress = 0f
  6019	            pressedMockField = null
  6020	            pressedRememberMe = false
  6021	            pressedBackChevron = false
  6022	            pressedMockCreateAvatarNav = null
  6023	            pressedMockDropdown = null
  6024	            pressedMockDropdownOption = null
  6025	            pressedForgotRecoveryTarget = null
  6026	            pressedForgotPasswordSubmit = false
  6027	            activeMockDropdownScroll = null
  6028	            mockDropdownScrollMoved = false
  6029	            mockDragReturnInProgress = false
  6030	            resetMockForgotPasswordSubmission()
  6031	            resetMockCreateAvatarCarousel()
  6032	            notifyIntroSnapshotChanged()
  6033	            postInvalidateOnAnimation()
  6034	            return
  6035	        }
  6036	
  6037	        mockSignInTransitionStartProgress = 1f
  6038	        mockSignInTransitionTargetProgress = 1f
  6039	    }
